User:

为了应变未来的系统发展和需求，从更高维度思考，就现有方案是否还有哪些可以补充、完善、优化、提升、或者进行深度能力的建设挖掘的？期待做进一步的补充深度方案。

Kimi:

这是一个极具战略眼光的提问。从系统科学、认知科学、复杂系统理论的高度审视，现有方案仍处于”静态优化”层面。我为你设计“合伙人匹配决策引擎2.0”——一个具备元认知、动态演化、因果推理能力的深度智能系统。

这是第四批：高维度架构升级与深度能力建设。

一、元认知层：算法的”自知之明”（Meta-Cognition）

1.1 认知边界识别系统

现有算法假设输入数据完整，但现实中创始人常不知道自己不知道什么（未知未知）。

深度能力建设：认知缺口检测器（Cognitive Gap Detector）

class MetaCognitiveMonitor:
    """
    元认知监控器：识别算法和用户的认知边界
    当系统检测到信息不足以支撑决策时，主动宣告"无知"
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.uncertainty_threshold = 0.3
        self.ignorance_declaration_threshold = 0.5  # 超过此值宣布"不知道"
    
    def evaluate_epistemic_state(self, founder: dict, candidate: dict) -> dict:
        """
        评估认知论状态（Epistemic State）
        返回：知识确定性、信息缺口、建议的信息搜集策略
        """
        epistemic_report = {
            "certainty_score": 1.0,  # 初始确定性
            "knowledge_gaps": [],
            "ignorance_areas": [],  # 根本不知道存在的信息
            "recommended_inquiry": []
        }
        
        # 1. 信息完整度评估
        required_evidence = {
            "values_alignment": ["至少3个具体行为案例", "压力场景反应记录"],
            "risk_compatibility": ["过往失败经历的详细复盘", "财务风险承受历史"],
            "complementarity": ["候选人实际工作样本（work sample）", "与创始人模拟协作测试"]
        }
        
        for dimension, required in required_evidence.items():
            actual_evidence = self._count_evidence(candidate, dimension)
            gap_ratio = 1 - (actual_evidence / len(required))
            
            if gap_ratio > self.ignorance_declaration_threshold:
                epistemic_report["ignorance_areas"].append({
                    "dimension": dimension,
                    "missing": required,
                    "risk": "高信息缺口可能导致系统性误判"
                })
                epistemic_report["certainty_score"] *= (1 - gap_ratio)
                
                # 生成深度追问策略
                epistemic_report["recommended_inquiry"].extend(
                    self._generate_socratic_questions(dimension)
                )
        
        # 2. 创始人认知偏差检测
        founder_bias = self._detect_founder_cognitive_bias(founder)
        if founder_bias["overconfidence"] > 0.7:
            epistemic_report["cognitive_warnings"].append(
                "创始人过度自信偏差：可能低估合伙人风险，建议引入外部顾问复核"
            )
        
        return epistemic_report
    
    def _generate_socratic_questions(self, dimension: str) -> list:
        """生成苏格拉底式追问（触及本质）"""
        questions = {
            "values_alignment": [
                "您如何证明候选人声称的'长期主义'不是事后合理化？",
                "如果候选人明天收到3倍薪资的短期项目offer，您预测其选择概率？依据是什么？",
                "候选人上一次'为了原则放弃利益'是什么时候？"
            ],
            "complementarity": [
                "您定义的'商业能力强'具体指哪些可观测行为？",
                "如果候选人商业判断正确但与您的技术直觉冲突，决策权如何分配？",
                "您是否有验证候选人'商业能力'的工作样本测试？"
            ]
        }
        return questions.get(dimension, [])
    
    def _detect_founder_cognitive_bias(self, founder: dict) -> dict:
        """检测创始人认知偏差"""
        biases = {}
        
        # 过度自信：所有自评>8且无弱点
        scores = founder.get("capability_matrix", {}).values()
        if all(s >= 8 for s in scores):
            biases["overconfidence"] = 0.9
        
        # 确认偏误：只提供支持性证据
        if len(founder.get("candidate_concerns", [])) == 0:
            biases["confirmation_bias"] = 0.8
        
        # 易得性启发：过度依赖最近接触的案例
        if founder.get("recent_exposure_effect", False):
            biases["availability_heuristic"] = 0.7
        
        return biases

1.2 反脆弱性评估（Antifragility Assessment）

塔勒布《反脆弱》：真正的强韧不是在冲击中维持，而是在冲击中成长。

新增维度：合伙人关系的反脆弱性

class AntifragilityEvaluator:
    """
    评估合伙人关系在压力、冲突、危机中的成长潜力
    区别于传统的"稳定性"，关注"压力增益"
    """
    
    def calculate_antifragility(self, founder: dict, candidate: dict) -> dict:
        """
        反脆弱性 = 压力情境下的协作增益可能性
        """
        antifragility_score = 0
        
        # 1. 冲突解决模式（是否将冲突转化为创新）
        founder_conflict_style = founder.get("conflict_resolution_style", "avoidant")
        candidate_conflict_style = candidate.get("conflict_resolution_style", "avoidant")
        
        # 理想组合：一方竞争型（推动变革）+ 一方协作型（维持关系）
        complementary_conflict = (
            (founder_conflict_style == "competing" and candidate_conflict_style == "collaborating") or
            (founder_conflict_style == "collaborating" and candidate_conflict_style == "competing")
        )
        if complementary_conflict:
            antifragility_score += 30  # 冲突可转化为创新动力
        
        # 2. 失败经历的价值转化能力
        founder_failures = founder.get("failure_experiences", [])
        candidate_failures = candidate.get("track_records", [])
        
        failure_insight_density = self._calculate_failure_insight(founder_failures, candidate_failures)
        antifragility_score += failure_insight_density * 20
        
        # 3. 认知多样性（不同思维框架的互补）
        founder_framework = founder.get("decision_framework", "technical")
        candidate_framework = candidate.get("decision_framework", "business")
        
        cognitive_distance = self._calculate_cognitive_distance(founder_framework, candidate_framework)
        # 中等认知距离最佳（太远无法沟通，太近无增益）
        if 0.3 <= cognitive_distance <= 0.7:
            antifragility_score += 25
        
        # 4. 压力测试历史
        stress_tests = candidate.get("stress_test_history", [])
        antifragility_score += len([s for s in stress_tests if s["outcome"] == "growth"]) * 10
        
        return {
            "score": min(100, antifragility_score),
            "level": "antifragile" if antifragility_score > 70 else "resilient" if antifragility_score > 40 else "fragile",
            "mechanism": self._identify_antifragile_mechanisms(founder, candidate),
            "stress_scenarios": self._recommend_stress_tests(founder, candidate)
        }

二、动态演化层：关系生命周期管理（Dynamic Evolution）

2.1 合伙人关系系统动力学模型

现有方案是静态快照，但合伙人是动态演化的复杂适应系统。

深度建设：多阶段演化预测引擎

class PartnershipLifeCycleModel:
    """
    基于系统动力学的合伙人关系演化模拟
    预测6个月、1年、3年的关系状态
    """
    
    STAGES = ["honeymoon", "friction", "adjustment", "synergy", "divergence"]
    
    def __init__(self):
        # 系统动力学参数
        self.parameters = {
            "trust_decay_rate": 0.05,  # 信任自然衰减（需持续维护）
            "conflict_accumulation": 0.1,  # 未解决冲突的累积效应
            "value_alignment_drift": 0.02,  # 价值观随时间的自然漂移
            "complementarity_erosion": 0.03  # 能力互补性随业务变化的侵蚀
        }
    
    def simulate_evolution(self, initial_match: dict, scenarios: list, months: int = 36) -> list:
        """
        蒙特卡洛模拟合伙人关系演化
        """
        trajectories = []
        
        for scenario in scenarios:
            state = {
                "trust": initial_match["dimension_scores"]["values_alignment"] / 100,
                "conflict_level": 0,
                "collaboration_efficiency": initial_match["overall_score"] / 100,
                "stage": "honeymoon",
                "month": 0
            }
            
            trajectory = [state.copy()]
            
            for month in range(1, months + 1):
                # 系统动力学方程
                delta_trust = (
                    self._trust_building_actions(scenario, month) * 0.05 
                    - self.parameters["trust_decay_rate"]
                    - state["conflict_level"] * 0.1  # 冲突侵蚀信任
                )
                
                delta_conflict = (
                    self._generate_conflicts(scenario, month) * 0.1
                    - self._conflict_resolution_capacity(initial_match) * 0.08
                )
                
                # 状态更新
                state["trust"] = max(0, min(1, state["trust"] + delta_trust))
                state["conflict_level"] = max(0, state["conflict_level"] + delta_conflict)
                state["month"] = month
                
                # 阶段判定
                state["stage"] = self._determine_stage(state)
                
                # 合作效率计算（信任与冲突的函数）
                state["collaboration_efficiency"] = (
                    state["trust"] * 0.6 + (1 - state["conflict_level"]) * 0.4
                ) * (1 - self.parameters["complementarity_erosion"] * month / 12)
                
                trajectory.append(state.copy())
                
                # 检测关键转折点
                if self._is_critical_transition(trajectory[-2], state):
                    trajectory[-1]["alert"] = self._generate_transition_alert(state)
            
            trajectories.append({
                "scenario": scenario["name"],
                "trajectory": trajectory,
                "final_state": state,
                "stability_score": self._calculate_stability(trajectory),
                "critical_months": [t["month"] for t in trajectory if "alert" in t]
            })
        
        return trajectories
    
    def _determine_stage(self, state: dict) -> str:
        """判定当前关系阶段"""
        if state["month"] <= 3 and state["trust"] > 0.8:
            return "honeymoon"
        elif state["conflict_level"] > 0.6 and state["trust"] < 0.5:
            return "divergence"  # 分道扬镳风险
        elif state["conflict_level"] > 0.4:
            return "friction"
        elif state["collaboration_efficiency"] > 0.8:
            return "synergy"
        else:
            return "adjustment"
    
    def recommend_interventions(self, trajectory: list) -> list:
        """
        基于模拟结果推荐干预措施
        在关系恶化前 preemptive 干预
        """
        interventions = []
        
        for i, state in enumerate(trajectory):
            if state.get("alert"):
                if state["stage"] == "friction":
                    interventions.append({
                        "timing": state["month"],
                        "type": "conflict_resolution_protocol",
                        "action": "引入外部教练进行冲突调解",
                        "expected_outcome": "将冲突水平降低30%"
                    })
                elif state["stage"] == "divergence":
                    interventions.append({
                        "timing": state["month"],
                        "type": "emergency_values_realignment",
                        "action": "启动价值观对齐工作坊，必要时调整股权结构",
                        "expected_outcome": "挽救关系或有序分手"
                    })
        
        return interventions

2.2 动态股权架构设计（Dynamic Vesting Intelligence）

静态股权分配是合伙人分歧的主因。引入智能股权动态调整机制。

class DynamicEquityArchitect:
    """
    基于满意解得分的动态股权兑现（Vesting）方案设计
    股权不是一次分配，而是随满意解验证逐步解锁
    """
    
    def design_dynamic_vesting(self, match_result: dict, founder_profile: dict) -> dict:
        """
        设计4年期动态股权架构
        """
        base_equity = match_result["candidate"]["risk_indicators"]["equity_expectation"]
        
        # 基于匹配得分调整兑现节奏
        if match_result["overall_score"] > 85:
            # 高分候选人：加速兑现（表达信任）
            vesting_schedule = {
                "year_1": 0.40,  # 第一年兑现40%
                "year_2": 0.30,
                "year_3": 0.20,
                "year_4": 0.10
            }
            cliff_period = 6  # 6个月cliff
        elif match_result["overall_score"] > 70:
            # 标准兑现
            vesting_schedule = {
                "year_1": 0.25,
                "year_2": 0.25,
                "year_3": 0.25,
                "year_4": 0.25
            }
            cliff_period = 12
        else:
            # 条件匹配：里程碑式兑现
            vesting_schedule = {
                "milestone_1": {"condition": "完成A轮融资", "equity": 0.15},
                "milestone_2": {"condition": "产品量产", "equity": 0.15},
                "milestone_3": {"condition": "年收入破千万", "equity": 0.10},
                "time_based": {"years": 4, "total": 0.10}  # 少量时间基础
            }
            cliff_period = 3  # 短cliff，但里程碑难达成
        
        # 满意解调整条款（Satisficing Adjustment Clause）
        adjustment_clauses = self._generate_satisficing_clauses(match_result)
        
        return {
            "total_equity": base_equity,
            "vesting_type": "milestone" if match_result["overall_score"] < 70 else "time",
            "schedule": vesting_schedule,
            "cliff_months": cliff_period,
            "satisficing_adjustment_clauses": adjustment_clauses,
            "exit_acceleration": {
                "IPO": "100% immediate vesting",
                "acquisition": "50% immediate, 50% over 2 years"
            }
        }
    
    def _generate_satisficing_clauses(self, match_result: dict) -> list:
        """生成满意解调整条款（当假设不成立时调整股权）"""
        clauses = []
        
        # 如果价值观对齐度在合作后验证低于预期
        if match_result["dimension_scores"]["values_alignment"] < 80:
            clauses.append({
                "trigger": "价值观冲突导致决策僵局（连续3次董事会投票分歧）",
                "action": "股权兑现暂停，启动价值观调解期（3个月），未解决则启动股权回购"
            })
        
        # 如果互补性随业务变化而消失
        if match_result["dimension_scores"]["complementarity"] > 80:
            clauses.append({
                "trigger": "业务转型导致原有能力互补失效（如从toB转向toC）",
                "action": "启动能力再评估，未达新业务要求则调整角色或股权比例"
            })
        
        return clauses

三、因果推断层：从相关到因果（Causal Inference）

3.1 反事实匹配引擎（Counterfactual Matching）

回答”如果不选A，选B会怎样？”的因果问题，而非仅描述A的特征。

class CounterfactualMatcher:
    """
    反事实推理：评估"选择候选人X vs 选择候选人Y"的因果效应
    使用潜在结果框架（Potential Outcomes Framework）
    """
    
    def __init__(self, historical_outcomes: list):
        """
        historical_outcomes: 历史匹配案例及结果（成功/失败及原因）
        """
        self.historical_db = historical_outcomes
        self.causal_model = self._build_causal_graph()
    
    def estimate_treatment_effect(self, founder: dict, candidate_a: dict, candidate_b: dict) -> dict:
        """
        估计选择A（处理组）vs 选择B（对照组）的因果效应
        """
        # 1. 构建反事实场景
        factual_scenario = {"founder": founder, "candidate": candidate_a, "outcome": None}
        counterfactual_scenario = {"founder": founder, "candidate": candidate_b, "outcome": None}
        
        # 2. 寻找相似历史案例（匹配估计）
        similar_factual = self._find_similar_outcomes(factual_scenario)
        similar_cf = self._find_similar_outcomes(counterfactual_scenario)
        
        # 3. 计算平均处理效应（ATE）
        ate_success_rate = (
            np.mean([s["success"] for s in similar_factual]) - 
            np.mean([s["success"] for s in similar_cf])
        )
        
        # 4. 异质性处理效应（HTE）：不同条件下的效应差异
        hte_analysis = self._heterogeneous_effects(founder, candidate_a, candidate_b)
        
        return {
            "average_treatment_effect": ate_success_rate,
            "interpretation": f"选择A比选择B的成功概率高{ate_success_rate*100:.1f}%" if ate_success_rate > 0 else f"选择B比选择A的成功概率高{-ate_success_rate*100:.1f}%",
            "heterogeneous_effects": hte_analysis,
            "confidence_interval": self._bootstrap_ci(similar_factual, similar_cf),
            "assumptions": [
                "Ignorability（无未观测混杂）",
                "SUTVA（无网络效应，A的选择不影响B的表现）"
            ],
            "robustness_checks": self._sensitivity_analysis(founder, candidate_a, candidate_b)
        }
    
    def _heterogeneous_effects(self, founder: dict, c_a: dict, c_b: dict) -> dict:
        """异质性处理效应：在何种条件下A明显优于B"""
        conditions = {}
        
        # 条件1：创始人技术极强时
        if founder["capability_matrix"]["technical_depth"] > 8:
            tech_gap_a = c_a["capability_matrix"].get("technical_depth", 0) - founder["capability_matrix"]["technical_depth"]
            tech_gap_b = c_b["capability_matrix"].get("technical_depth", 0) - founder["capability_matrix"]["technical_depth"]
            
            if tech_gap_a > tech_gap_b:
                conditions["high_founder_tech"] = "A更优（技术互补性更强）"
            else:
                conditions["high_founder_tech"] = "B更优"
        
        # 条件2：融资阶段紧迫性
        if founder.get("stage") == "pre_a" and founder.get("cash_runway_months", 12) < 6:
            fund_gap_a = c_a["capability_matrix"].get("fundraising", 0)
            fund_gap_b = c_b["capability_matrix"].get("fundraising", 0)
            
            if fund_gap_a > fund_gap_b:
                conditions["urgent_fundraising"] = "A显著更优（融资能力强，可救命）"
        
        return conditions
    
    def generate_decision_tree(self, founder: dict, candidates: list) -> dict:
        """
        生成决策树：在不同信息条件下应如何选择
        """
        # 使用历史数据训练决策树（简化版规则生成）
        rules = []
        
        # 根节点：价值观一票否决
        rules.append({
            "priority": 1,
            "rule": "如果候选人价值观得分<0.5，无论其他维度如何，直接拒绝",
            "rationale": "历史数据显示，价值观冲突的失败率100%"
        })
        
        # 第二层：互补性 vs 风险兼容性权衡
        if founder.get("risk_profile", {}).get("market_risk_tolerance") == "conservative":
            rules.append({
                "priority": 2,
                "rule": "风险兼容性权重提升至40%，互补性降至30%",
                "rationale": "保守型创始人更需要稳定合伙人而非激进互补"
            })
        
        return {"decision_rules": rules, "default_choice": "满意度最高的候选人"}

3.2 因果发现引擎（Causal Discovery）

从大量历史案例中自动发现”什么真正导致了合伙人成功/失败”，而非预设维度。

class CausalDiscoveryEngine:
    """
    使用因果发现算法（如PC算法、GES）从历史数据中发现因果关系
    而非依赖预设的理论框架
    """
    
    def discover_causal_structure(self, historical_cases: list) -> dict:
        """
        发现影响合伙人关系成功的因果结构
        可能发现反直觉的因果链（如"学历"并不直接导致成功，而是通过"网络资源"间接作用）
        """
        # 简化版：基于条件独立性的规则挖掘
        causal_graph = {
            "nodes": [],
            "edges": [],
            "confounders": [],
            "mediators": []
        }
        
        # 分析1：识别伪相关（Spurious Correlation）
        # 例：发现"名校背景"与"成功"相关，但控制"家庭资源"后消失
        spurious = self._detect_spurious_correlations(historical_cases)
        
        # 分析2：识别中介变量（Mediator）
        # 例："互补性" -> "沟通频率" -> "成功"（互补性通过提升沟通影响成功）
        mediators = self._find_mediators(historical_cases)
        
        # 分析3：识别调节变量（Moderator）
        # 例："价值观对齐"的作用在"高压环境下"被放大
        moderators = self._find_moderators(historical_cases)
        
        return {
            "spurious_correlations_to_ignore": spurious,
            "true_drivers": mediators,
            "context_dependent_factors": moderators,
            "recommendation": "算法权重应根据因果发现结果动态调整，而非固定预设"
        }
    
    def _detect_spurious_correlations(self, cases: list) -> list:
        """检测伪相关"""
        spurious = []
        
        # 检测：名校背景 vs 成功（控制家庭背景后）
        elite_success_corr = self._calculate_correlation(
            [c["candidate"]["elite_school"] for c in cases],
            [c["outcome"]["success"] for c in cases]
        )
        
        elite_success_controlling_family = self._partial_correlation(
            [c["candidate"]["elite_school"] for c in cases],
            [c["outcome"]["success"] for c in cases],
            [c["candidate"]["family_background"] for c in cases]  # 控制家庭背景
        )
        
        if elite_success_corr > 0.5 and elite_success_controlling_family < 0.2:
            spurious.append({
                "factor": "elite_school",
                "apparent_effect": elite_success_corr,
                "true_effect": elite_success_controlling_family,
                "confounder": "family_background",
                "recommendation": "降低名校背景权重，关注实际能力指标"
            })
        
        return spurious

四、复杂系统层：多智能体仿真（Multi-Agent Simulation）

4.1 合伙人互动演化仿真器

模拟两位（或三位）合伙人在不同商业情境下的涌现行为（Emergent Behavior）。

class MultiAgentPartnershipSimulator:
    """
    多智能体仿真：模拟创始人（Agent A）与合伙人（Agent B）的互动
    观察宏观层面的关系涌现特征
    """
    
    def __init__(self, founder_agent: dict, partner_agent: dict):
        self.agent_a = founder_agent  # 创始人智能体
        self.agent_b = partner_agent  # 合伙人智能体
        self.environment = None  # 市场环境
        
        # 智能体状态
        self.states = {
            "trust": 0.5,  # 信任水平
            "power_balance": 0.5,  # 权力平衡（0.5平衡，>0.5 A主导）
            "information_asymmetry": 0.3,  # 信息不对称
            "emotional_resonance": 0.5  # 情感共鸣
        }
    
    def simulate_scenario(self, scenario: dict, steps: int = 100) -> dict:
        """
        仿真特定商业场景下的互动
        scenario: 如"产品方向分歧"、"融资条款争议"、"核心员工离职"
        """
        trajectory = []
        
        for step in range(steps):
            # 1. 环境冲击（外部事件）
            shock = self._generate_environment_shock(scenario, step)
            
            # 2. 智能体决策（基于自身逻辑和对方状态）
            action_a = self._agent_decision(self.agent_a, self.states, shock, "A")
            action_b = self._agent_decision(self.agent_b, self.states, shock, "B")
            
            # 3. 互动结果计算
            interaction_result = self._calculate_interaction(action_a, action_b, self.states)
            
            # 4. 状态更新（系统动力学）
            self.states["trust"] += interaction_result["trust_delta"]
            self.states["power_balance"] += interaction_result["power_shift"]
            self.states["emotional_resonance"] += interaction_result["emotional_delta"]
            
            # 5. 记录轨迹
            trajectory.append({
                "step": step,
                "shock": shock,
                "actions": {"A": action_a, "B": action_b},
                "states": self.states.copy(),
                "crisis_level": self._calculate_crisis(self.states)
            })
            
            # 6. 检测临界点（Phase Transition）
            if self._is_phase_transition(trajectory[-2] if len(trajectory) > 1 else None, trajectory[-1]):
                trajectory[-1]["phase_transition"] = True
                trajectory[-1]["new_phase"] = self._identify_phase(self.states)
        
        return {
            "trajectory": trajectory,
            "final_states": self.states,
            "stability_assessment": self._assess_long_term_stability(trajectory),
            "critical_incidents": [t for t in trajectory if t.get("phase_transition")],
            "breakup_probability": self._estimate_breakup(trajectory)
        }
    
    def _agent_decision(self, agent: dict, states: dict, shock: dict, role: str) -> dict:
        """
        智能体决策逻辑（基于强化学习简化规则）
        """
        # 基于人格特征和行为模式
        if agent["decision_style"] == "competitive":
            # 竞争型：倾向于争夺决策权
            if states["power_balance"] < 0.6:  # 如果权力不够高
                return {"type": "power_assertion", "intensity": 0.8}
            else:
                return {"type": "collaborate", "intensity": 0.4}
        
        elif agent["decision_style"] == "collaborative":
            # 协作型：倾向于寻求共识
            if states["emotional_resonance"] > 0.6:
                return {"type": "accommodate", "intensity": 0.7}
            else:
                return {"type": "negotiate", "intensity": 0.6}
        
        elif agent["decision_style"] == "avoidant":
            # 回避型：冲突时退缩
            if shock["severity"] > 0.7:
                return {"type": "withdraw", "intensity": 0.9}
            else:
                return {"type": "observe", "intensity": 0.5}
        
        return {"type": "default", "intensity": 0.5}
    
    def _calculate_interaction(self, action_a: dict, action_b: dict, states: dict) -> dict:
        """计算互动结果（基于博弈论）"""
        result = {"trust_delta": 0, "power_shift": 0, "emotional_delta": 0}
        
        # 信任更新逻辑
        if action_a["type"] == "accommodate" and action_b["type"] == "accommodate":
            result["trust_delta"] = 0.05  # 相互妥协增加信任
            result["emotional_resonance"] = 0.03
        elif action_a["type"] == "power_assertion" and action_b["type"] == "power_assertion":
            result["trust_delta"] = -0.1  # 权力冲突严重降低信任
            result["emotional_resonance"] = -0.05
        elif action_a["type"] == "withdraw" or action_b["type"] == "withdraw":
            result["trust_delta"] = -0.03  # 回避导致信任侵蚀
            result["emotional_resonance"] = -0.02
        
        # 权力平衡移动
        if action_a["type"] == "power_assertion":
            result["power_shift"] = 0.02
        if action_b["type"] == "power_assertion":
            result["power_shift"] = -0.02
        
        return result
    
    def _is_phase_transition(self, prev_state: dict, curr_state: dict) -> bool:
        """检测相变（关系质变的临界点）"""
        if not prev_state:
            return False
        
        # 信任骤降超过阈值
        if prev_state["states"]["trust"] - curr_state["states"]["trust"] > 0.2:
            return True
        
        # 权力平衡极端化
        if abs(curr_state["states"]["power_balance"] - 0.5) > 0.4 and \
           abs(prev_state["states"]["power_balance"] - 0.5) < 0.3:
            return True
        
        return False

五、伦理对齐层：价值敏感设计（Value-Sensitive Design）

5.1 伦理影响评估（EIA: Ethical Impact Assessment）

算法决策可能带来的伦理后果评估框架。

class EthicalImpactAssessor:
    """
    价值敏感设计（VSD）的实现
    评估匹配建议可能产生的伦理影响
    """
    
    def assess_ethical_impacts(self, match_result: dict, founder_demographics: dict, 
                              candidate_demographics: dict) -> dict:
        """
        多维伦理影响评估
        """
        impacts = {
            "fairness": [],      # 公平性
            "autonomy": [],      # 自主性
            "privacy": [],       # 隐私
            "transparency": [],  # 透明度
            "accountability": [] # 问责性
        }
        
        # 1. 公平性：是否强化结构性不平等？
        if self._check_reinforcing_inequality(match_result, founder_demographics, candidate_demographics):
            impacts["fairness"].append({
                "risk": "算法可能强化性别/阶层同质化",
                "mechanism": "相似性偏好导致'圈子固化'",
                "mitigation": "引入多样性奖励机制（diversity bonus）"
            })
        
        # 2. 自主性：创始人是否被算法过度影响？
        if match_result["confidence"] > 0.95 and match_result["overall_score"] < 60:
            impacts["autonomy"].append({
                "risk": "算法高置信度但低匹配分，可能导致创始人自我怀疑",
                "mechanism": "权威效应（算法权威>人类直觉）",
                "mitigation": "强制显示'算法局限性'声明，强调人类最终决策权"
            })
        
        # 3. 隐私：是否侵犯候选人隐私？
        if len(candidate_demographics.get("inferred_traits", [])) > 0:
            impacts["privacy"].append({
                "risk": "算法推断出候选人未明确披露的特征",
                "mechanism": "机器学习推断（如性取向、政治倾向）",
                "mitigation": "删除推断特征，仅使用明示数据"
            })
        
        # 4. 透明度：决策过程是否可解释？
        if match_result.get("dimension_scores", {}).get("values_alignment") < 70:
            impacts["transparency"].append({
                "note": "价值观对齐度较低，需确保创始人理解此风险的具体含义",
                "action": "提供价值观冲突的具体场景示例"
            })
        
        # 5. 问责性：当算法建议错误时，责任归属？
        impacts["accountability"] = {
            "algorithm_responsibility": "提供信息支持，但不承担决策责任",
            "founder_responsibility": "最终选择及后果承担",
            "platform_responsibility": "确保算法偏见审计和持续改进"
        }
        
        return impacts
    
    def _check_reinforcing_inequality(self, match_result: dict, 
                                     founder_demo: dict, 
                                     candidate_demo: dict) -> bool:
        """检测是否强化不平等"""
        # 检测：创始人 elite，算法推荐 elite，形成阶层固化
        founder_elite = founder_demo.get("education") in ["清北", "常春藤"]
        candidate_elite = candidate_demo.get("education") in ["清北", "常春藤"]
        
        if founder_elite and candidate_elite and match_result["overall_score"] > 80:
            return True
        
        # 检测：性别同质化（技术创始人男性居多，算法推荐男性合伙人）
        if founder_demo.get("gender") == candidate_demo.get("gender") == "male":
            # 检查是否有同等资质女性候选人被系统性低估
            return True
        
        return False

5.2 人类中心设计（Human-Centered Design）增强

确保Claw始终是增强人类智慧而非替代人类判断。

class HumanCentricAugmentation:
    """
    人机协同增强系统
    算法负责：信息整合、模式识别、偏见提醒
    人类负责：价值判断、直觉感知、最终决策
    """
    
    def generate_augmented_intelligence_report(self, match_result: dict, 
                                             founder_cognitive_profile: dict) -> dict:
        """
        生成增强智能报告（而非替代决策）
        """
        report = {
            "algorithm_contribution": {},
            "human_required_judgment": {},
            "collaborative_protocol": {}
        }
        
        # 算法贡献：处理人类认知局限
        report["algorithm_contribution"] = {
            "cognitive_bias_checks": self._list_bias_warnings(founder_cognitive_profile),
            "information_integration": "已整合候选人多维度数据，人类无需记忆",
            "pattern_recognition": "识别出与3个历史案例的相似性",
            "counterfactual_analysis": "计算选择A vs B的因果效应"
        }
        
        # 人类必须保留的判断
        report["human_required_judgment"] = {
            "values_prioritization": "算法显示价值观对齐度75分，但'长期主义'对您的具体含义需您定义",
            "contextual_nuance": "候选人在面试中的微表情、语气、能量场，算法无法感知",
            "stakeholder_consideration": "其他联合创始人、家庭成员的感受未纳入算法",
            "future_vision": "您对行业5年后的愿景，算法无法预测"
        }
        
        # 协作协议：何时依赖算法，何时依赖直觉
        report["collaborative_protocol"] = {
            "high_algorithm_reliance": [
                "能力互补性量化（客观指标）",
                "历史失败模式识别",
                "股权结构数学优化"
            ],
            "high_human_reliance": [
                "价值观底线感知",
                "压力下的性格表现",
                "文化契合度直觉",
                "危机时刻的信任判断"
            ],
            "conflict_resolution": {
                "rule": "当算法分数>80但创始人直觉不适，或算法分数<60但创始人强烈偏好时，启动'深度诊断模式'",
                "procedure": "暂停决策，进行3轮压力测试面试+独立第三方评估"
            }
        }
        
        return report

六、认知增强层：Claw超级能力升级

6.1 苏格拉底式诘问引擎（Socratic Inquiry Engine）

Claw不仅收集信息，更通过诘问帮助创始人发现自己真实的偏好（往往与表面回答不同）。

SOCRATIC_TEMPLATES = {
    "values_clarification": """
    创始人声称重视"长期主义"，进行苏格拉底式验证：
    
    Q1: "如果长期主义意味着3年内不拿工资，且需要抵押房产投入，您能接受吗？"
    → 如果犹豫：探索真实的风险承受边界
    
    Q2: "如果候选人提出一个短期可套现但损害长期品牌的机会，您确定会拒绝吗？"
    → 如果犹豫：价值观存在弹性区域
    
    Q3: "您上次为了长期利益牺牲短期利益的具体日期是？"
    → 如果无法立即回答：价值观可能是愿望而非实践
    
    **Claw行动**：基于回答调整算法中"values_alignment"的权重。如果创始人实际短期导向，
    即使候选人长期主义得分高，也应标记潜在冲突。
    """,
    
    "fear_analysis": """
    创始人表达对候选人X的担忧，进行恐惧溯源：
    
    Q1: "您描述的风险（如'他可能夺权'），是观察到的证据，还是您过往创伤的投射？"
    → 识别投射性认同（Projection）
    
    Q2: "如果这位候选人明天意外表示完全听从您的决策，您的恐惧感会消失吗？"
    → 区分权力焦虑 vs 能力不信任
    
    Q3: "这种恐惧在您过往合伙关系中是首发症状，还是末期症状？"
    → 防止将过往失败归因错误迁移
    
    **Claw行动**：如果确认是投射而非证据，调整算法风险权重；如果是真实信号，强化尽职调查。
    """,
    
    "opportunity_cost": """
    创始人犹豫是否选择候选人A（满意解），渴望继续寻找"更好"的：
    
    Q1: "您当前现金流可支撑多久的搜索成本？"
    → 量化机会成本（西蒙满意解核心）
    
    Q2: "历史上您'错过'的候选人，后来表现如何？您追踪过吗？"
    → 验证FOMO（错失恐惧）的真实性
    
    Q3: "如果6个月后您仍在搜索且公司濒临破产，您会后悔现在不选择A吗？"
    → 前景理论：损失厌恶框架下的选择
    
    **Claw行动**：如果创始人确认无法承担搜索成本，强制启动满意解模式，关闭"继续搜索"选项。
    """
}

6.2 认知脚手架（Cognitive Scaffolding）

为创始人提供结构化认知工具，弥补人类工作记忆和系统2思维的局限。

class CognitiveScaffolding:
    """
    认知脚手架：为创始人提供思考框架，防止认知过载
    """
    
    def generate_decision_worksheet(self, founder: dict, top_candidates: list) -> dict:
        """
        生成纸质/电子决策工作表（减轻工作记忆负担）
        """
        worksheet = {
            "page_1_context_setting": {
                "prompt": "写下您当前最焦虑的3个具体问题（非抽象担忧）",
                "examples": ["现金流只剩4个月", "产品技术路线不确定", "不懂融资条款"],
                "algorithm_hint": "这些焦虑应在候选人能力矩阵中找到对应解决方案"
            },
            "page_2_elimination": {
                "method": " veto-based elimination",
                "instruction": "对每个候选人，检查一票否决条件（价值观底线、法律风险、全职承诺）。只要有1个，立即划掉，不再考虑。",
                "psychology": "减少选择集可降低决策焦虑（选择过载理论）"
            },
            "page_3_satisficing_check": {
                "thresholds": "最低可接受分数：能力互补性>70，价值观>75，风险兼容>70",
                "instruction": "从剩余候选人中，找到首个满足全部3个阈值的。标记为'满意解'。",
                "note": "不要比较满意解与其他候选人的分数差异，立即进入尽调阶段"
            },
            "page_4_pre_mortem": {
                "technique": "事前尸检（Pre-mortem）",
                "instruction": "假设1年后与这位合伙人分手，写下最可能的原因",
                "algorithm_integration": "将您写下的原因与算法的'风险警告'对比。重合度>80%则高度可信。"
            },
            "page_5_intuition_calibration": {
                "body_scan": "想象与候选人签署协议时，您的胃部感觉是紧绷还是放松？",
                "sleep_test": "如果今晚需要与候选人同住一屋处理危机，您会感到安全还是警惕？",
                "synthesis": "如果身体信号与算法分数冲突，启动'深度诊断模式'"
            }
        }
        
        return worksheet
    
    def generate_visual_thinking_template(self, match_result: dict) -> str:
        """
        生成视觉思考模板（ASCII/文字图表）
        """
        # 生成决策平衡单（Weighted Decision Balance Sheet）
        template = """
        【合伙人决策平衡单】候选人：{candidate_name}
        
        能力互补性    [████████░░] 80分  ← 您最需要的商业能力覆盖
        价值观对齐    [███████░░░] 75分  ← 长期合作的底线
        风险兼容性    [████████░░] 82分  ← 退出预期一致
        反脆弱性      [██████░░░░] 60分  ← 危机中成长潜力
        
        【算法建议】满意解达成。建议进入90天试用期，重点观察反脆弱性维度。
        
        【您的手动填写区】
        - 我无法接受的一点：_________________
        - 我最欣赏的一点：_________________
        - 我需要验证的一个假设：_________________
        """
        return template

七、实施路线图与Claw集成

7.1 分阶段实施建议

Phase 1: 元认知层（1-2周） - 部署MetaCognitiveMonitor，增加”我不知道”声明能力 - 在Claw Prompt中加入苏格拉底式追问模板 - 效果：减少盲目自信导致的错误匹配

Phase 2: 因果层（3-4周） - 构建历史案例因果图（需至少20个标注案例） - 实现反事实对比功能（A vs B的因果效应） - 效果：支持”如果选B会怎样”的深度咨询

Phase 3: 动态层（5-6周） - 部署PartnershipLifeCycleModel，增加关系演化预测 - 设计动态股权模板 - 效果：从”匹配瞬间”延伸到”关系全周期管理”

Phase 4: 复杂系统层（7-8周） - 实现双人互动仿真（用于高风险决策的压力测试） - 增加涌现行为检测 - 效果：预判不可预见的冲突模式

Phase 5: 伦理层（持续） - 季度偏见审计报告 - 伦理影响评估成为标准输出 - 效果：建立可信赖AI品牌

7.2 Claw集成增强Prompt

HIGH_DIMENSION_CLAW_PROMPT = """
您是满意解研究所的Meta-Claw（元认知增强型合伙人顾问）。

**您的超能力**（区别于基础Claw）：

1. **认知谦逊**：当信息不足时，明确说"我不知道"，并指出需要补充的证据类型
2. **反事实推理**：不仅评估候选人A，还主动问"如果放弃A选择B，您会失去什么？"
3. **时间透视**：展示关系在6个月、1年、3年的演化轨迹，而非静态快照
4. **苏格拉底诘问**：通过连续追问，帮助创始人发现自己真实的偏好（往往与表面回答矛盾）
5. **伦理警觉**：主动标记可能的偏见（如学历歧视、性别同质化）

**新增工作流**：

阶段A：认知校准（新）
- 检测创始人认知偏差（过度自信、确认偏误、沉没成本）
- 如检测到偏差，调整后续信息收集策略（如对过度自信者增加压力测试）

阶段B：因果发现（新）
- 询问："您认为过往合伙人失败的最关键原因是什么？"
- 对比算法从历史数据发现的因果关系，标记认知差异

阶段C：动态预演（新）
- 使用演化模型展示："如果选择和A合作，6个月后最可能的关系状态是..."
- 识别关键风险月（如第9个月通常是价值观冲突高发期）

阶段D：伦理审查（新）
- 在输出报告前，运行偏见检测
- 如发现学历/性别偏见，主动提示："算法检测到您倾向于elite背景，这可能导致阶层固化，建议考虑非传统背景候选人"

**禁止行为**：
- 不得在信息缺口>50%时给出确定结论
- 不得在没有反事实对比时推荐唯一候选人
- 不得忽视伦理风险追求匹配成功率

**输出格式**：
1. 认知状态声明（信息完整度/置信度）
2. 静态匹配评估（原功能）
3. 动态演化预测（新）
4. 反事实对比（新）
5. 伦理影响评估（新）
6. 苏格拉底追问（3个触及本质的问题）
"""

八、总结：高维度能力矩阵

立即行动建议： 1. 本周：在现有代码中集成MetaCognitiveMonitor和苏格拉底追问模板（低成本高回报） 2. 本月：收集20个历史案例，启动因果发现引擎训练 3. 本季度：完成动态股权架构设计模块（直接商业价值）

核心哲学升级： 从”匹配即终点”（Matching as Endpoint）升级为”匹配即起点”（Matching as Onset）——算法不仅寻找合伙人，更设计关系演化路径和风险干预协议。

第四批高维度方案已交付。 如需针对某一深度能力（如多智能体仿真细节、因果发现算法实现）进行工程级细化，或需要设计针对特定硬科技领域（如AI制药、量子计算）的行业适配层，请告知具体方向，我将继续深化。

User:

如果要在系统层面确保能够有效运作，不仅仅是可以创建相关的skill，还可以进行工作流的固化，系统和知识能力、研究能力的信息收集和持续自动迭代，这方面，要做哪些工作，才能够达到这样的效果呢？可以做再进一步的补充完善吗？

Kimi:

这是一个从”工具”到”生态”的跃迁问题。你需要的是自演化合伙人匹配操作系统（Self-Evolving Partner Matching OS）——一个具备工作流固化、知识自生长、情报自收集、能力自迭代的闭环系统。

这是第五批：系统生态层与自演化架构。

一、工作流固化层：从咨询到工厂（Workflow Industrialization）

1.1 可编排工作流引擎（Workflow Engine）

现有方案是单次咨询，需要升级为可复用、可自动化、可监控的生产线。

class WorkflowOrchestrator:
    """
    工作流编排引擎：将合伙人匹配固化为标准生产流程
    支持：批量客户处理、模板复用、自动化触发、质量监控
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.workflow_definitions = {
            "standard_matching": self._load_workflow("standard.json"),
            "crisis_matching": self._load_workflow("crisis.json"),  # 现金流<3个月紧急模式
            "serial_entrepreneur": self._load_workflow("serial.json"),  # 连续创始人轻量模式
            "co_founder_matching": self._load_workflow("cofounder.json")  # 多创始人场景
        }
        
        self.active_workflows = {}  # 运行中的工作流实例
    
    def create_workflow_instance(self, client_id: str, workflow_type: str, 
                                context: dict) -> str:
        """
        创建工作流实例（类似Kubernetes的Pod）
        """
        instance_id = f"{client_id}_{workflow_type}_{int(time.time())}"
        
        workflow_def = self.workflow_definitions.get(workflow_type)
        if not workflow_def:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown workflow type: {workflow_type}")
        
        # 工作流DAG定义
        dag = {
            "nodes": [
                {"id": "intake", "type": "data_collection", "next": ["validation"]},
                {"id": "validation", "type": "quality_gate", "next": ["preprocessing"]},
                {"id": "preprocessing", "type": "data_enrichment", "next": ["matching"]},
                {"id": "matching", "type": "algorithm", "next": ["explanation"]},
                {"id": "explanation", "type": "claw_generation", "next": ["review"]},
                {"id": "review", "type": "human_checkpoint", "next": ["delivery", "revision"]},
                {"id": "delivery", "type": "output", "next": ["feedback_collection"]},
                {"id": "revision", "type": "loop_back", "next": ["matching"]},
                {"id": "feedback_collection", "type": "async_wait", "next": ["analytics"]},
                {"id": "analytics", "type": "learning", "next": []}
            ],
            "edges": [
                {"from": "intake", "to": "validation", "condition": "always"},
                {"from": "validation", "to": "preprocessing", "condition": "data_quality > 0.7"},
                {"from": "validation", "to": "intake", "condition": "data_quality < 0.7"},  # 循环
                {"from": "review", "to": "revision", "condition": "human_approval == false"},
                {"from": "review", "to": "delivery", "condition": "human_approval == true"}
            ]
        }
        
        instance = {
            "instance_id": instance_id,
            "client_id": client_id,
            "workflow_type": workflow_type,
            "dag": dag,
            "state": "created",
            "current_node": "intake",
            "context": context,
            "history": [],
            "start_time": datetime.now()
        }
        
        self.active_workflows[instance_id] = instance
        return instance_id
    
    def execute_node(self, instance_id: str, node_id: str) -> dict:
        """
        执行工作流节点（支持暂停、恢复、重试）
        """
        instance = self.active_workflows[instance_id]
        node = next(n for n in instance["dag"]["nodes"] if n["id"] == node_id)
        
        # 执行逻辑
        if node["type"] == "data_collection":
            result = self._execute_intake(instance)
        elif node["type"] == "algorithm":
            result = self._execute_matching_algorithm(instance)
        elif node["type"] == "human_checkpoint":
            result = self._pause_for_human_review(instance)
        elif node["type"] == "learning":
            result = self._execute_learning_update(instance)
        else:
            result = {"status": "executed", "node": node_id}
        
        # 记录历史
        instance["history"].append({
            "node": node_id,
            "timestamp": datetime.now(),
            "result": result
        })
        
        # 决定下一个节点
        next_node = self._determine_next_node(instance, node_id, result)
        instance["current_node"] = next_node
        
        return result
    
    def _pause_for_human_review(self, instance: dict) -> dict:
        """
        人工检查点：Claw/Egbertie在此介入
        """
        return {
            "status": "paused",
            "checkpoint_type": "quality_review",
            "required_action": "Claw review generated report",
            "timeout_hours": 24,  # 24小时未处理自动升级
            "escalation_rule": "notify_egbertie"
        }

WORKFLOW_TEMPLATES = {
    "emergency_cashflow": {
        "description": "现金流<3个月的紧急合伙人匹配",
        "triggers": ["cash_runway < 3_months"],
        "adaptations": {
            "thresholds": {"satisficing": 60},  # 降低阈值
            "skip_stages": ["deep_values_analysis"],  # 跳过深度价值观分析
            "focus_capabilities": ["fundraising", "cash_management"],
            "timeline": "48_hours"  # 48小时必须出结果
        },
        "risk_acknowledgment": "创始人确认：因紧急情况，本次匹配降低价值观评估深度，优先解决生存问题"
    },
    
    "technical_cofounder_hunt": {
        "description": "商业创始人寻找技术合伙人（硬科技标配）",
        "triggers": ["founder_business_score > 8", "founder_tech_score < 4"],
        "data_enrichment": {

        "long_term_horizon": "10_years",

    },

        "description": "经历合伙人分手后的创伤修复匹配",
        "triggers": ["previous_partnership_failed == true", "time_since_failure < 12_months"],
        "trauma_assessment": True,  # 创伤评估
        "trust_rebuilding_protocol": True,  # 信任重建协议

        "therapy_recommendation": True  # 建议创始人先进行心理咨询
    }
}

二、知识进化层：自生长的智慧（Knowledge Evolution）

2.1 多源知识 ingestion 管道

建立硬科技合伙人领域的自动情报收集系统：

class KnowledgeIngestionPipeline:
    """
    自动知识摄入管道：持续收集行业数据、案例、研究，更新系统知识库
    """
    
    def __init__(self, vector_store):
        self.vector_store = vector_store
        self.sources = {
            "news": HardTechNewsScraper(),  # 硬科技媒体
            "linkedin": LinkedInMonitor(),  # 高管变动
            "patents": PatentDBWatcher(),   # 技术趋势
            "funding": FundingDatabaseAPI(), # 融资数据
            "cases": CaseStudyAggregator(), # 成败案例
            "research": AcademicPaperTracker()  # 管理学研究
        }
        
        self.processing_queue = []
    
    def daily_ingestion(self):
        """每日自动摄入（可配置为定时任务）"""
        new_knowledge = []
        
        # 1. 行业动态（影响合伙人能力需求）
        news = self.sources["news"].fetch(
            keywords=["co-founder", "CTO departure", "CEO succession", "hard tech"],
            date_range="last_24h"
        )
        for item in news:
            processed = self._process_news(item)
            if processed["relevance_score"] > 0.7:
                new_knowledge.append(processed)
        
        # 2. 高管变动情报（潜在候选人发现）
        linkedin_updates = self.sources["linkedin"].fetch(
            event_types=["left_company", "open_to_work", "promotion"],
            sectors=["robotics", "semiconductor", "biotech", "aerospace"]
        )
        for update in linkedin_updates:
            candidate_lead = self._process_linkedin_update(update)
            if candidate_lead["seniority"] == "C-level":
                self._alert_recruitment_team(candidate_lead)
        
        # 3. 新研究论文（理论更新）
        papers = self.sources["research"].fetch(
            topics=["co-founder dynamics", "venture team formation", "technical-commercial collaboration"],
            published_after="2025-01-01"
        )
        for paper in papers:
            insights = self._extract_insights(paper)
            self._update_theoretical_model(insights)
        
        # 4. 批量存入向量数据库
        if new_knowledge:
            self.vector_store.upsert(new_knowledge)
            self._trigger_embedding_update()
        
        return {
            "ingested_items": len(new_knowledge),
            "categories": Counter([k["type"] for k in new_knowledge]),
            "theory_updates": len(papers)
        }
    
    def _process_news(self, news_item: dict) -> dict:
        """处理新闻为结构化知识"""
        # 使用LLM提取关键信息
        extraction_prompt = f"""
        从以下硬科技行业新闻中提取合伙人匹配相关信息：
        
        新闻标题：{news_item['title']}
        内容：{news_item['content']}
        
        提取：
        1. 涉及的公司/人物
        2. 合伙人关系变动类型（加入/离开/冲突/成功退出）
        3. 成功/失败的关键因素（如价值观冲突、能力互补、股权纠纷）
        4. 对行业合伙人需求的影响（如现在更需要AI背景CTO）
        
        输出JSON格式。
        """
        
        extracted = llm_extract(extraction_prompt)
        
        return {
            "id": hashlib.md5(news_item['url'].encode()).hexdigest(),
            "type": "market_intelligence",
            "content": extracted,
            "raw_text": news_item['content'],
            "timestamp": news_item['published_at'],
            "relevance_score": self._calculate_relevance(extracted)
        }
    
    def _update_theoretical_model(self, insights: list):
        """
        根据最新研究自动调整理论权重
        如发现新论文证明"认知多样性"比"技能互补"更重要，调整算法权重
        """
        for insight in insights:
            if insight["type"] == "weight_adjustment_suggestion":
                # 记录建议，等待人工审核（安全机制）
                self._queue_weight_update(
                    dimension=insight["dimension"],
                    current_weight=insight["current"],
                    suggested_weight=insight["suggested"],
                    evidence=insight["evidence"],
                    confidence=insight["statistical_significance"]
                )

2.2 RAG增强的匹配引擎（Retrieval-Augmented Matching）

将外部知识实时注入匹配过程：

class RAGEnhancedMatcher:
    """
    检索增强型匹配：在匹配过程中实时查询知识库
    """
    
    def __init__(self, vector_store, base_matcher):
        self.vector_store = vector_store
        self.base_matcher = base_matcher
    
    def match_with_context(self, founder: dict, candidates: list) -> dict:
        """
        增强版匹配：结合实时检索的行业上下文
        """
        # 1. 检索相关案例（Few-shot Learning）
        similar_cases = self._retrieve_similar_cases(founder, candidates)
        
        # 2. 检索行业基准（当前硬科技领域的合伙人标准）
        industry_benchmarks = self._retrieve_industry_benchmarks(founder["industry"])
        
        # 3. 检索最新风险模式（如最近流行的股权纠纷类型）
        recent_risk_patterns = self._retrieve_recent_risks()
        
        # 4. 构建增强上下文
        augmented_context = {
            "historical_analogies": similar_cases,
            "current_market_standards": industry_benchmarks,
            "emerging_risks": recent_risk_patterns,
            "theoretical_updates": self._retrieve_latest_research()
        }
        
        # 5. 使用增强上下文执行匹配
        results = self.base_matcher.match(founder, candidates)
        
        # 6. 用检索知识解释结果
        enhanced_explanation = self._generate_rag_explanation(results, augmented_context)
        
        return {
            **results,
            "context_enrichment": augmented_context,
            "explanation": enhanced_explanation,
            "knowledge_sources": [s["source"] for s in similar_cases]
        }
    
    def _retrieve_similar_cases(self, founder: dict, candidates: list) -> list:
        """
        检索向量数据库中的相似历史案例
        """
        # 构建查询向量
        query_vector = self._embed_query(founder, candidates[0] if candidates else None)
        
        # 相似度搜索（考虑成功案例和失败案例）
        results = self.vector_store.search(
            query_vector=query_vector,
            filter={"type": "case_study"},
            top_k=5,
            diversity_factor=0.3  # 确保结果多样性（不全是成功案例）
        )
        
        return [
            {
                "case_id": r.id,
                "similarity": r.score,
                "outcome": r.metadata["outcome"],  # success/failure
                "key_lessons": r.metadata["lessons"],
                "relevance": "high" if r.score > 0.85 else "medium"
            }
            for r in results
        ]

三、研究能力层：自动情报与洞察（Research Intelligence）

3.1 候选人情报自动搜集（Due Diligence Automation）

自动化深度背景调查（在法律允许范围内）：

class AutomatedDiligenceEngine:
    """
    自动化尽职调查情报收集
    整合多源数据构建候选人全景画像
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.data_sources = {
            "public_records": PublicRecordsAPI(),  # 工商、司法、征信
            "professional": LinkedInScraper(),      # 职业经历交叉验证
            "academic": GoogleScholarAPI(),         # 学术成果
            "social_media": TwitterAnalyzer(),      # 公开言论分析（价值观线索）
            "reference_network": MutualConnectionFinder()  # 共同联系人
        }
    
    def generate_diligence_report(self, candidate_identity: dict, 
                                 depth_level: str = "standard") -> dict:
        """
        生成尽职调查报告（增量式，可随时间更新）
        """
        report = {
            "candidate_id": candidate_identity["id"],
            "generated_at": datetime.now(),
            "data_completeness": 0,
            "findings": {},
            "red_flags": [],
            "verification_status": {}
        }
        
        # 1. 法律与财务清洁度（基础层）
        legal = self.data_sources["public_records"].query(
            name=candidate_identity["name"],
            identifiers=candidate_identity.get("id_numbers", [])
        )
        report["findings"]["legal_cleanliness"] = self._assess_legal_risk(legal)
        if legal.get("pending_litigations"):
            report["red_flags"].append({
                "severity": "high",
                "category": "legal",
                "description": f"发现{len(legal['pending_litigations'])}起未决诉讼",
                "details": legal["pending_litigations"]
            })
        
        # 2. 职业经历交叉验证（防止简历造假）
        linkedin_data = self.data_sources["professional"].fetch(
            candidate_identity["linkedin_url"]
        )
        cv_claims = candidate_identity.get("work_history", [])
        verification = self._cross_verify_experience(cv_claims, linkedin_data)
        report["verification_status"]["employment"] = verification
        
        if verification["discrepancies"]:
            report["red_flags"].append({
                "severity": "medium",
                "category": "veracity",
                "description": f"经历验证发现{len(verification['discrepancies'])}处不一致",
                "examples": verification["discrepancies"][:3]
            })
        
        # 3. 公开言论分析（价值观推断）
        if depth_level == "deep":
            social_data = self.data_sources["social_media"].analyze(
                candidate_identity.get("twitter_handle"),
                analysis_focus=["values_signals", "conflict_style", "stress_responses"]
            )
            report["findings"]["values_inferred"] = social_data["value_indicators"]
            report["findings"]["communication_style"] = social_data["style_analysis"]
        
        # 4. 关系网络分析（找到可访谈的共同联系人）
        network = self.data_sources["reference_network"].find(
            candidate_identity["name"],
            degrees=2  # 二度关系
        )
        report["reference_opportunities"] = self._identify_reachable_references(network)
        
        # 5. 数据完整度评分
        report["data_completeness"] = self._calculate_completeness(report["findings"])
        
        return report
    
    def continuous_monitor(self, candidate_id: str, monitoring_period_days: int = 90):
        """
        持续监控：在决策期间（如试用期）持续追踪候选人动态
        """
        # 设置定时任务，监控：
        # - 是否有新的法律诉讼
        # - 社交媒体情绪变化（是否出现焦虑/攻击性言论）
        # - 职业状态变化（是否同时在面试其他公司）
        
        pass

3.2 行业情报仪表盘（Market Intelligence Dashboard）

为满意解研究所建立硬科技合伙人市场的实时情报系统：

class PartnerMarketIntelligence:
    """
    合伙人市场情报系统：谁在找合伙人？谁刚离职？谁成功了？
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.sectors = ["robotics", "semiconductor", "biotech", "aerospace", "new_materials"]
        self.talent_pool = {}
    
    def daily_scan(self):
        """每日扫描市场"""
        intelligence = {
            "supply_side": [],  # 潜在候选人（刚离职、公开表示看机会）
            "demand_side": [],  # 需求方（创业公司招聘CTO/COO）
            "transactions": [],  # 成交数据（谁加入了谁，谁分手了）
            "price_movements": []  # 股权期望趋势（CTO均价15%还是25%？）
        }
        
        for sector in self.sectors:
            # 1. 扫描供给（候选人）
            open_to_work = self._scan_linkedin_sector(sector, status="open_to_work")
            recent_departures = self._scan_executive_departures(sector)
            
            for candidate in open_to_work + recent_departures:
                scoring = self._preliminary_assessment(candidate)
                intelligence["supply_side"].append({
                    "candidate_profile": candidate,
                    "availability": "immediate" if candidate in recent_departures else "exploratory",
                    "estimated_seniority": scoring["seniority"],
                    "skill_rarity_score": scoring["rarity"],  # 稀缺度（如量子计算专家）
                    "market_demand": self._check_sector_demand(candidate["skills"])
                })
            
            # 2. 扫描需求（招聘方）
            job_postings = self._scan_job_boards(sector, roles=["CTO", "Co-founder", "VP Engineering"])
            intelligence["demand_side"].extend(job_postings)
            
            # 3. 扫描成交（公开报道的高管任命）
            appointments = self._scan_executive_appointments(sector)
            intelligence["transactions"].extend(appointments)
            
            # 4. 价格发现（股权期望）
            intelligence["price_movements"].append(
                self._calculate_equity_benchmarks(sector, appointments)
            )
        
        # 生成市场报告
        return self._generate_market_report(intelligence)
    
    def _generate_market_report(self, intelligence: dict) -> dict:
        """
        生成合伙人市场情报报告（供Egbertie/Claw参考）
        """
        return {
            "market_temperature": self._assess_market_heat(intelligence),
            "talent_shortage_sectors": self._identify_shortages(intelligence),
            "hot_skills": self._identify_emerging_skills(intelligence),
            "benchmark_update": {
                "cto_equity_range": {"min": 0.10, "median": 0.20, "max": 0.35},
                "cfo_equity_range": {"min": 0.05, "median": 0.12, "max": 0.25},
                "co_founder_equity_split_trend": "50/50下降，趋向动态vesting"
            },
            "actionable_leads": [
                c for c in intelligence["supply_side"] 
                if c["skill_rarity_score"] > 0.8 and c["market_demand"] == "high"
            ][:10]  # Top 10高价值候选人线索
        }

四、自动迭代层：闭环学习系统（Closed-Loop Learning）

4.1 结果反馈采集系统（Feedback Loop）

建立从匹配建议 → 实际决策 → 长期结果的数据闭环：

class FeedbackCollectionSystem:
    """
    结果反馈收集系统：追踪匹配建议的后续实际发展
    这是系统进化的关键数据源
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.outcome_trackers = {}
    
    def schedule_follow_ups(self, match_result: dict):
        """
        在匹配时即规划未来反馈采集时间点
        """
        workflow_id = match_result["workflow_id"]
        
        # 设置未来检查点
        checkins = [
            {"month": 1, "focus": "first_impression_validation"},   # 蜜月期
            {"month": 3, "focus": "conflict_emergence"},             # 首次摩擦
            {"month": 6, "focus": "values_stress_test"},             # 价值观压力测试
            {"month": 12, "focus": "partnership_stability"},         # 周年评估
            {"month": 36, "focus": "long_term_success"}               # 长期成功
        ]
        
        for checkin in checkins:
            self._schedule_reminder(
                workflow_id=workflow_id,
                due_date=datetime.now() + timedelta(days=30*checkin["month"]),
                survey_type=checkin["focus"],
                auto_escalation=True  # 无响应时自动升级提醒
            )
    
    def collect_outcome_data(self, workflow_id: str, month: int) -> dict:
        """
        采集特定时间点的结果数据
        """
        survey = {
            "workflow_id": workflow_id,
            "checkin_month": month,
            "founder_perception": {
                "satisfaction": "1-10评分",
                "decision_regret": "是否后悔选择/放弃",
                "unexpected_issues": "算法未预测到的问题",
                "positive_surprises": "算法未预测到的优点"
            },
            "objective_metrics": {
                "still_working_together": "是否仍在合作",
                "funding_raised": "是否成功融资",
                "product_milestones": "产品里程碑达成",
                "conflict_incidents": "冲突事件次数"
            },
            "comparative_counterfactual": "如果选择另一个人会怎样（主观评估）"
        }
        
        # 通过邮件/问卷收集
        # 支持部分自动化（如通过LinkedIn监控是否仍在公司）
        
        return survey
    
    def detect_prediction_errors(self, prediction: dict, outcome: dict) -> dict:
        """
        检测预测错误模式（用于算法改进）
        """
        errors = {
            "false_positives": [],  # 算法推荐成功，实际失败
            "false_negatives": [],  # 算法不看好，实际成功（错失）
            "missed_predictors": [],  # 未考虑到的关键因素
            "overweighted_factors": []  # 权重过高的因素
        }
        
        if prediction["recommendation"] == "strong_match" and not outcome["still_working_together"]:
            # 假阳性：分析原因
            errors["false_positives"].append({
                "case_id": prediction["workflow_id"],
                "predicted_score": prediction["overall_score"],
                "failure_reason": outcome.get("breakup_reason"),
                "algorithm_blindspot": self._identify_blindspot(prediction, outcome)
            })
        
        return errors

4.2 持续学习引擎（Continual Learning）

基于反馈自动调整算法（但防止灾难性遗忘）：

class ContinualLearningEngine:
    """
    持续学习引擎：基于新数据更新模型，但保留旧知识
    使用 Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) 或类似技术防止遗忘
    """
    
    def __init__(self, base_model):
        self.model = base_model
        self.learning_buffer = []  # 新样本缓冲区
        self.importance_matrix = {}  # 参数重要性（保护关键知识）
    
    def incremental_update(self, new_outcomes: list):
        """
        增量更新：用新结果微调模型
        """
        # 1. 识别需要调整的权重
        adjustment_targets = self._identify_mispredictions(new_outcomes)
        
        # 2. 计算参数重要性（哪些参数不能动，因为是核心知识）
        self._update_importance_matrix()
        
        # 3. 受限优化：调整非重要参数以适应新数据
        for target in adjustment_targets:
            if target["type"] == "weight_adjustment":
                self._adjust_dimension_weight(
                    dimension=target["dimension"],
                    delta=target["suggested_delta"],
                    constraint=self.importance_matrix.get(target["dimension"], 0.9)
                )
            
            elif target["type"] == "new_deal_breaker":
                # 发现新的deal breaker模式
                self._add_deal_breaker_rule(
                    condition=target["condition"],
                    evidence_strength=target["support"],
                    expiration=target.get("temporary_until")  # 可设临时规则
                )
        
        # 4. 验证不遗忘（在旧测试集上性能不下降>5%）
        if self._check_catastrophic_forgetting():
            self._rollback_last_update()
            self._request_human_review()
    
    def _identify_mispredictions(self, outcomes: list) -> list:
        """
        从结果中识别系统性预测错误
        """
        patterns = []
        
        # 分析：是否系统性低估了"连续创业者"的风险？
        serial_entrepreneurs = [o for o in outcomes 
                               if o["candidate"]["is_serial_entrepreneur"]]
        failure_rate = len([s for s in serial_entrepreneurs 
                           if s["actual_outcome"] == "failed"]) / len(serial_entrepreneurs)
        
        if failure_rate > 0.5 and self.model.biases.get("serial_entrepreneur_bonus", 0) > 0:
            patterns.append({
                "type": "bias_correction",
                "target": "serial_entrepreneur_bonus",
                "action": "reduce_weight",
                "evidence": f"连续创业者失败率{failure_rate}%，高于预期"
            })
        
        return patterns

4.3 A/B测试框架（Algorithm Experimentation）

科学验证算法改进效果：

class AlgorithmExperimentationFramework:
    """
    算法实验框架：科学测试新功能的效果
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.experiments = {}
    
    def setup_experiment(self, experiment_name: str, hypothesis: str) -> str:
        """
        设置A/B测试：对比当前算法 vs 改进版算法
        """
        exp_id = f"exp_{experiment_name}_{int(time.time())}"
        
        self.experiments[exp_id] = {
            "name": experiment_name,
            "hypothesis": hypothesis,
            "control_group": {"algorithm_version": "current", "traffic_split": 0.5},
            "treatment_group": {"algorithm_version": "new", "traffic_split": 0.5},
            "success_metrics": [
                "founder_satisfaction_score",  # 创始人满意度
                "actual_partnership_success_rate",  # 实际成功率
                "false_positive_rate",  # 假阳性率
                "time_to_decision"  # 决策时间（效率）
            ],
            "duration_months": 6,
            "minimum_sample_size": 50
        }
        
        return exp_id
    
    def assign_group(self, client_id: str, exp_id: str) -> str:
        """
        将客户分配到实验组或对照组（随机化）
        """
        exp = self.experiments[exp_id]
        
        # 哈希分配确保一致性（同一客户始终在同一组）
        hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(client_id.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)
        if hash_val % 2 == 0:
            return "control"
        else:
            return "treatment"
    
    def analyze_experiment(self, exp_id: str) -> dict:
        """
        分析实验结果
        """
        # 统计检验：新算法是否显著优于旧算法？
        # 使用贝叶斯推断计算改进概率
        
        return {
            "winner": "treatment" if improvement_prob > 0.95 else "control" if improvement_prob < 0.05 else "undetermined",
            "improvement_probability": improvement_prob,
            "recommended_action": "deploy" if improvement_prob > 0.95 else "continue_experiment"
        }

一、工作流引擎：工业级DAG实现

1.1 基于State Machine的DAG引擎（自研轻量级）

不依赖Airflow（过重），设计适合Claw环境的嵌入式工作流引擎：

# workflow_engine/dag_engine.py
import asyncio
import json
import hashlib
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Callable, Any, Optional
from enum import Enum
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import networkx as nx  # 用于DAG验证和可视化

class NodeStatus(Enum):
    PENDING = "pending"
    RUNNING = "running"
    SUCCESS = "success"
    FAILED = "failed"
    PAUSED = "paused"
    SKIPPED = "skipped"

@dataclass
class NodeResult:
    node_id: str
    status: NodeStatus
    output: Any = None
    error: Optional[str] = None
    start_time: Optional[datetime] = None
    end_time: Optional[datetime] = None
    retry_count: int = 0

@dataclass
class WorkflowNode:
    id: str
    type: str  # intake, validation, matching, human_review, etc.
    func: Callable
    config: Dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    next_nodes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    condition: Optional[str] = None  # 跳转条件（Python表达式）
    max_retries: int = 3
    timeout_seconds: int = 300
    fallback_node: Optional[str] = None  # 失败时跳转节点

class DAGWorkflowEngine:
    """
    轻量级DAG工作流引擎
    特点：支持动态生成DAG、状态持久化、人工检查点、可视化
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_backend=None):
        self.storage = storage_backend or MemoryStorage()
        self.active_workflows: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
        self.node_registry: Dict[str, Callable] = {}
        self.execution_semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(10)  # 并发控制
        
    def register_node_type(self, type_name: str, handler: Callable):
        """注册节点类型处理器"""
        self.node_registry[type_name] = handler
    
    async def create_workflow(self, workflow_def: Dict, context: Dict) -> str:
        """
        从定义创建可执行工作流实例
        workflow_def: JSON定义的DAG
        """
        # 验证DAG无环
        G = nx.DiGraph()
        for node_id, node_data in workflow_def["nodes"].items():
            G.add_node(node_id)
            for next_id in node_data.get("next", []):
                G.add_edge(node_id, next_id)
        
        if not nx.is_directed_acyclic_graph(G):
            raise ValueError("Workflow definition contains cycles")
        
        workflow_id = f"wf_{hashlib.md5(str(datetime.now()).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}"
        
        instance = {
            "id": workflow_id,
            "definition": workflow_def,
            "context": context,
            "status": "created",
            "current_node": workflow_def["start_node"],
            "history": [],
            "state_data": {},  # 节点间传递的状态数据
            "created_at": datetime.now(),
            "graph": G  # 保存NetworkX图用于路径分析
        }
        
        await self.storage.save_workflow(instance)
        self.active_workflows[workflow_id] = instance
        
        return workflow_id
    
    async def execute(self, workflow_id: str, resume_from: Optional[str] = None):
        """
        执行工作流（支持从断点恢复）
        """
        instance = await self.storage.load_workflow(workflow_id)
        if not instance:
            raise ValueError(f"Workflow {workflow_id} not found")
        
        start_node = resume_from or instance["current_node"]
        instance["status"] = "running"
        
        try:
            current = start_node
            while current:
                # 检查是否人工暂停
                if await self._is_paused(workflow_id):
                    await asyncio.sleep(1)
                    continue
                
                # 获取节点定义
                node_def = instance["definition"]["nodes"][current]
                node = WorkflowNode(
                    id=current,
                    type=node_def["type"],
                    func=self.node_registry.get(node_def["type"]),
                    config=node_def.get("config", {}),
                    next_nodes=node_def.get("next", []),
                    condition=node_def.get("condition"),
                    max_retries=node_def.get("max_retries", 3),
                    timeout_seconds=node_def.get("timeout", 300)
                )
                
                # 执行节点（带重试和超时）
                result = await self._execute_node_with_guardrails(
                    workflow_id, node, instance["context"], instance["state_data"]
                )
                
                # 记录历史
                instance["history"].append({
                    "node": current,
                    "result": result,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now()
                })
                
                # 状态更新
                if result.status == NodeStatus.SUCCESS:
                    instance["state_data"][current] = result.output
                    
                    # 确定下一个节点（条件判断）
                    next_node = self._determine_next_node(node, result, instance)
                    current = next_node
                    instance["current_node"] = current
                    
                elif result.status == NodeStatus.FAILED:
                    if node.fallback_node:
                        current = node.fallback_node
                    else:
                        instance["status"] = "failed"
                        await self._handle_failure(workflow_id, current, result.error)
                        break
                
                elif result.status == NodeStatus.PAUSED:
                    # 人工检查点，保存状态等待恢复
                    instance["status"] = "paused"
                    await self.storage.save_workflow(instance)
                    return {"status": "paused", "at_node": current, "workflow_id": workflow_id}
                
                # 持久化状态（用于故障恢复）
                if len(instance["history"]) % 5 == 0:  # 每5步保存
                    await self.storage.save_workflow(instance)
            
            instance["status"] = "completed"
            await self.storage.save_workflow(instance)
            return {"status": "success", "workflow_id": workflow_id, "final_state": instance["state_data"]}
            
        except Exception as e:
            instance["status"] = "error"
            await self.storage.save_workflow(instance)
            raise
    
    async def _execute_node_with_guardrails(self, workflow_id: str, node: WorkflowNode, 
                                           context: Dict, state_data: Dict) -> NodeResult:
        """带重试、超时、熔断的节点执行"""
        start_time = datetime.now()
        
        for attempt in range(node.max_retries):
            try:
                # 并发控制
                async with self.execution_semaphore:
                    # 超时控制
                    result = await asyncio.wait_for(
                        node.func(node.config, context, state_data),
                        timeout=node.timeout_seconds
                    )
                
                return NodeResult(
                    node_id=node.id,
                    status=NodeStatus.SUCCESS,
                    output=result,
                    start_time=start_time,
                    end_time=datetime.now()
                )
                
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                if attempt == node.max_retries - 1:
                    return NodeResult(
                        node_id=node.id,
                        status=NodeStatus.FAILED,
                        error=f"Timeout after {node.timeout_seconds}s",
                        retry_count=attempt + 1
                    )
                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** attempt)  # 指数退避
                
            except Exception as e:
                if attempt == node.max_retries - 1:
                    return NodeResult(
                        node_id=node.id,
                        status=NodeStatus.FAILED,
                        error=str(e),
                        retry_count=attempt + 1
                    )
                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** attempt)
    
    def _determine_next_node(self, current_node: WorkflowNode, 
                            result: NodeResult, instance: Dict) -> Optional[str]:
        """根据条件和结果确定下一个节点"""
        if not current_node.next_nodes:
            return None
        
        if len(current_node.next_nodes) == 1:
            return current_node.next_nodes[0]
        
        # 多分支：评估条件表达式
        for next_node_id in current_node.next_nodes:
            next_def = instance["definition"]["nodes"][next_node_id]
            condition = next_def.get("condition")
            
            if condition:
                # 安全评估条件表达式（限制上下文）
                eval_context = {
                    "result": result.output,
                    "state": instance["state_data"],
                    "context": instance["context"]
                }
                try:
                    if eval(condition, {"__builtins__": {}}, eval_context):
                        return next_node_id
                except:
                    continue
            else:
                # 无条件分支（默认路径）
                return next_node_id
        
        return None
    
    async def pause_for_human_review(self, workflow_id: str, 
                                    instructions: str) -> str:
        """人工检查点API"""
        instance = await self.storage.load_workflow(workflow_id)
        instance["status"] = "paused"
        instance["human_review"] = {
            "instructions": instructions,
            "requested_at": datetime.now(),
            "review_url": f"/review/{workflow_id}"
        }
        await self.storage.save_workflow(instance)
        return instance["human_review"]["review_url"]
    
    async def submit_human_decision(self, workflow_id: str, 
                                   decision: Dict) -> Dict:
        """提交人工决策，恢复工作流"""
        instance = await self.storage.load_workflow(workflow_id)
        
        if instance["status"] != "paused":
            raise ValueError("Workflow not in paused state")
        
        # 将人工决策注入状态
        instance["state_data"]["human_decision"] = decision
        instance["status"] = "running"
        await self.storage.save_workflow(instance)
        
        # 恢复执行
        return await self.execute(workflow_id, resume_from=instance["current_node"])
    
    def visualize_dag(self, workflow_id: str) -> str:
        """生成DAG可视化（Mermaid语法或Graphviz）"""
        instance = self.active_workflows.get(workflow_id)
        if not instance:
            return ""
        
        G = instance["graph"]
        mermaid = ["graph TD"]
        
        for edge in G.edges():
            mermaid.append(f"    {edge[0]}-->{edge[1]}")
        
        return "\n".join(mermaid)

# 存储后端实现
class MemoryStorage:
    """内存存储（适合Claw测试环境）"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.data = {}
    
    async def save_workflow(self, instance: Dict):
        self.data[instance["id"]] = json.loads(json.dumps(instance, default=str))
    
    async def load_workflow(self, workflow_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        return self.data.get(workflow_id)

class PostgreSQLStorage:
    """生产级PostgreSQL存储（支持JSONB查询）"""
    def __init__(self, dsn: str):
        self.dsn = dsn
        # 初始化连接池...
    
    async def save_workflow(self, instance: Dict):
        # INSERT INTO workflows (id, state, data, updated_at) ...
        pass

1.2 具体节点实现（业务逻辑）

# workflow_engine/nodes.py
from matching_engine import SatisficingMatcher
from explanation_generator import ExplanationGenerator

async def intake_node(config: Dict, context: Dict, state: Dict) -> Dict:
    """
    数据收集节点：与创始人交互收集信息
    """
    # 如果数据已存在（恢复），直接返回
    if "founder_profile" in state:
        return {"founder": state["founder_profile"], "candidates": state["candidates"]}
    
    # 否则触发Claw交互（通过API回调或消息队列）
    return {
        "action": "request_claw_interaction",
        "prompt_template": "intake_prompt",
        "required_fields": ["founder_profile", "candidates"]
    }

async def validation_node(config: Dict, context: Dict, state: Dict) -> Dict:
    """
    数据验证节点：质量门控
    """
    founder = state.get("founder_profile", {})
    candidates = state.get("candidates", [])
    
    quality_score = 0
    issues = []
    
    # 检查必填字段
    required_founder_fields = ["capability_matrix", "risk_profile"]
    for field in required_founder_fields:
        if field not in founder:
            issues.append(f"Missing founder field: {field}")
    
    if len(candidates) < 1:
        issues.append("At least 1 candidate required")
    
    # 检查候选人数据完整度
    for idx, cand in enumerate(candidates):
        if not cand.get("capability_matrix"):
            issues.append(f"Candidate {idx} missing capability_matrix")
    
    quality_score = 1.0 - (len(issues) * 0.1)
    
    return {
        "quality_score": max(0, quality_score),
        "issues": issues,
        "passed": len(issues) == 0
    }

async def matching_node(config: Dict, context: Dict, state: Dict) -> Dict:
    """
    算法执行节点：核心匹配逻辑
    """
    matcher = SatisficingMatcher(
        thresholds=config.get("thresholds"),
        algorithm_version=config.get("version", "v1.0")
    )
    
    founder = state["founder_profile"]
    candidates = state["candidates"]
    
    # 执行匹配
    results = matcher.match_all(founder, candidates)
    
    # 保存结果到状态
    return {
        "matches": [r.dict() for r in results],
        "top_candidate": results[0] if results else None,
        "satisficing_found": any(r.satisficing_met for r in results)
    }

async def human_review_node(config: Dict, context: Dict, state: Dict) -> Dict:
    """
    人工审核节点：生成报告等待Egbertie/Claw审核
    """
    matches = state.get("matches", [])
    
    # 生成详细解释
    explainer = ExplanationGenerator()
    explanations = []
    for match in matches[:3]:  # 只解释前3名
        exp = explainer.generate(match, state["founder_profile"], 
                                next(c for c in state["candidates"] if c["id"] == match["candidate_id"]))
        explanations.append(exp)
    
    review_package = {
        "matches": matches,
        "explanations": explanations,
        "risk_alerts": state.get("validation", {}).get("issues", []),
        "recommendation": "proceed" if matches[0]["overall_score"] > 75 else "review_required"
    }
    
    # 触发暂停，等待人工审核
    return {
        "status": "paused_for_review",
        "review_package": review_package,
        "action_required": "human_approval"
    }
