二、持续学习：EWC算法工程实现

2.1 Elastic Weight Consolidation核心实现

使用PyTorch实现防止灾难性遗忘的EWC：

# continual_learning/ewc_trainer.py
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
import copy

class EWCParameter:
    """EWC参数重要性封装"""
    def __init__(self, name: str, value: torch.Tensor):
        self.name = name
        self.value = value
        self.fisher_information = None  # F对角线（参数重要性）
        self.ewc_lambda = 0.0  # 正则化强度

class EWCLoss(nn.Module):
    """
    Elastic Weight Consolidation Loss
    L = L_new + λ/2 * Σ F_i * (θ_i - θ*_i)^2
    """
    def __init__(self, model: nn.Module, ewc_params: Dict[str, EWCParameter], 
                 lambda_ewc: float = 5000.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.model = model
        self.ewc_params = ewc_params
        self.lambda_ewc = lambda_ewc
        
    def forward(self, current_loss: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """计算完整EWC损失"""
        if not self.ewc_params:
            return current_loss
        
        ewc_loss = 0.0
        
        for name, param in self.model.named_parameters():
            if name in self.ewc_params and self.ewc_params[name].fisher_information is not None:
                ewc_param = self.ewc_params[name]
                
                # EWC正则项: F * (θ - θ_old)^2
                fisher = ewc_param.fisher_information
                diff = param - ewc_param.value
                
                ewc_loss += (fisher * (diff ** 2)).sum()
        
        total_loss = current_loss + (self.lambda_ewc / 2.0) * ewc_loss
        return total_loss

class MatchingModelEWC(nn.Module):
    """
    合伙人匹配分数预测模型（简化版）
    输入：创始人+候选人的特征向量
    输出：匹配成功概率（0-1）
    """
    def __init__(self, input_dim: int = 20, hidden_dims: List[int] = [64, 32]):
        super().__init__()
        
        layers = []
        prev_dim = input_dim
        for hidden_dim in hidden_dims:
            layers.extend([
                nn.Linear(prev_dim, hidden_dim),
                nn.ReLU(),
                nn.Dropout(0.2)
            ])
            prev_dim = hidden_dim
        
        layers.append(nn.Linear(prev_dim, 1))
        layers.append(nn.Sigmoid())
        
        self.network = nn.Sequential(*layers)
        
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.network(x)

class EWCTrainer:
    """
    EWC训练器：持续学习新数据而不遗忘旧知识
    """
    def __init__(self, model: MatchingModelEWC, device: str = "cpu"):
        self.model = model
        self.device = device
        self.model.to(device)
        
        # 存储每个任务的EWC参数
        self.ewc_params_history: List[Dict[str, EWCParameter]] = []
        self.optimal_params_history: List[Dict[str, torch.Tensor]] = []
        
    def compute_fisher_information(self, 
                                   dataloader: torch.utils.data.DataLoader,
                                   num_samples: int = 200) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        计算Fisher信息矩阵对角线（参数重要性）
        F_ii = E[(∂log p/∂θ_i)^2]
        """
        self.model.eval()
        fisher_information = {}
        
        # 初始化Fisher矩阵
        for name, param in self.model.named_parameters():
            fisher_information[name] = torch.zeros_like(param)
        
        # 在训练样本上计算梯度平方的期望
        for idx, (x, y) in enumerate(dataloader):
            if idx >= num_samples:
                break
                
            x, y = x.to(self.device), y.to(self.device)
            
            self.model.zero_grad()
            output = self.model(x)
            
            # 对数似然的梯度
            log_likelihood = torch.log(output + 1e-10) * y + torch.log(1 - output + 1e-10) * (1 - y)
            loss = -log_likelihood.mean()
            
            loss.backward()
            
            # 累加梯度平方
            for name, param in self.model.named_parameters():
                if param.grad is not None:
                    fisher_information[name] += (param.grad ** 2) / num_samples
        
        return fisher_information
    
    def train_task(self, 
                   task_id: str,
                   train_loader: torch.utils.data.DataLoader,
                   val_loader: torch.utils.data.DataLoader,
                   epochs: int = 50,
                   learning_rate: float = 0.001,
                   ewc_lambda: float = 5000.0) -> Dict:
        """
        训练一个新任务（新批次的匹配案例）
        """
        optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.model.parameters(), lr=learning_rate)
        
        # 如果是第一个任务，正常训练
        if len(self.ewc_params_history) == 0:
            ewc_loss_fn = None
        else:
            # 合并历史任务的EWC参数
            consolidated_ewc = self._consolidate_ewc_params()
            ewc_loss_fn = EWCLoss(self.model, consolidated_ewc, ewc_lambda)
        
        best_val_loss = float('inf')
        best_model_state = None
        
        for epoch in range(epochs):
            # 训练阶段
            self.model.train()
            train_loss = 0.0
            
            for x, y in train_loader:
                x, y = x.to(self.device), y.to(self.device)
                
                optimizer.zero_grad()
                
                output = self.model(x)
                base_loss = F.binary_cross_entropy(output, y)
                
                # 应用EWC损失
                if ewc_loss_fn:
                    loss = ewc_loss_fn(base_loss)
                else:
                    loss = base_loss
                
                loss.backward()
                optimizer.step()
                
                train_loss += loss.item()
            
            # 验证阶段
            val_loss = self._evaluate(val_loader)
            
            if val_loss < best_val_loss:
                best_val_loss = val_loss
                best_model_state = copy.deepcopy(self.model.state_dict())
            
            if epoch % 10 == 0:
                print(f"Task {task_id}, Epoch {epoch}: Train Loss={train_loss/len(train_loader):.4f}, Val Loss={val_loss:.4f}")
        
        # 加载最佳模型
        self.model.load_state_dict(best_model_state)
        
        # 计算当前任务的Fisher信息
        fisher_info = self.compute_fisher_information(train_loader)
        
        # 保存当前任务的EWC参数
        ewc_params = {}
        optimal_params = {}
        for name, param in self.model.named_parameters():
            ewc_param = EWCParameter(name, param.data.clone())
            ewc_param.fisher_information = fisher_info[name]
            ewc_param.ewc_lambda = ewc_lambda
            ewc_params[name] = ewc_param
            optimal_params[name] = param.data.clone()
        
        self.ewc_params_history.append(ewc_params)
        self.optimal_params_history.append(optimal_params)
        
        return {
            "task_id": task_id,
            "final_val_loss": best_val_loss,
            "fisher_computed": True,
            "parameters_protected": len(ewc_params)
        }
    
    def _consolidate_ewc_params(self) -> Dict[str, EWCParameter]:
        """合并多个历史任务的Fisher信息（在线EWC）"""
        if len(self.ewc_params_history) == 1:
            return self.ewc_params_history[0]
        
        consolidated = {}
        
        # 获取所有参数名
        param_names = self.ewc_params_history[0].keys()
        
        for name in param_names:
            # 取最新的参数值
            latest_value = self.ewc_params_history[-1][name].value
            
            # Fisher信息求和（在线EWC策略）
            total_fisher = sum(
                task_params[name].fisher_information 
                for task_params in self.ewc_params_history
            )
            
            ewc_param = EWCParameter(name, latest_value)
            ewc_param.fisher_information = total_fisher
            consolidated[name] = ewc_param
        
        return consolidated
    
    def _evaluate(self, dataloader: torch.utils.data.DataLoader) -> float:
        """评估模型"""
        self.model.eval()
        total_loss = 0.0
        
        with torch.no_grad():
            for x, y in dataloader:
                x, y = x.to(self.device), y.to(self.device)
                output = self.model(x)
                loss = F.binary_cross_entropy(output, y)
                total_loss += loss.item()
        
        return total_loss / len(dataloader)
    
    def check_forgetting(self, old_task_loaders: List[torch.utils.data.DataLoader]) -> Dict:
        """
        检测灾难性遗忘（应该在旧任务上性能不下降>5%）
        """
        results = {}
        self.model.eval()
        
        for idx, loader in enumerate(old_task_loaders):
            task_id = f"task_{idx}"
            loss = self._evaluate(loader)
            results[task_id] = {
                "current_loss": loss,
                "forgotten": loss > self.task_baselines.get(task_id, float('inf')) * 1.05
            }
        
        return results

2.2 应用于合伙人匹配的EWC适配

# continual_learning/matching_continual_learning.py
class MatchingModelAdapter:
    """
    将EWC应用于合伙人匹配系统的适配器
    将规则-based系统与神经网络预测结合
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.model = MatchingModelEWC(input_dim=20)  # 4维度*5特征
        self.trainer = EWCTrainer(self.model)
        self.feature_extractor = MatchingFeatureExtractor()
        
    def prepare_task_data(self, 
                         historical_cases: List[Dict], 
                         task_name: str) -> torch.utils.data.DataLoader:
        """
        将历史案例转换为PyTorch数据集
        """
        X = []
        y = []
        
        for case in historical_cases:
            # 特征工程：将创始人+候选人特征编码为向量
            features = self.feature_extractor.encode(case)
            label = 1.0 if case["outcome"] == "success" else 0.0
            
            X.append(features)
            y.append(label)
        
        X_tensor = torch.FloatTensor(X)
        y_tensor = torch.FloatTensor(y).unsqueeze(1)
        
        dataset = torch.utils.data.TensorDataset(X_tensor, y_tensor)
        return torch.utils.data.DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=16, shuffle=True)
    
    def incremental_learn(self, new_cases: List[Dict], task_id: str):
        """
        增量学习新批次案例
        """
        train_loader = self.prepare_task_data(new_cases, task_id)
        
        # 划分验证集
        train_size = int(0.8 * len(train_loader.dataset))
        val_size = len(train_loader.dataset) - train_size
        train_dataset, val_dataset = torch.utils.data.random_split(
            train_loader.dataset, [train_size, val_size]
        )
        
        train_loader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=16)
        val_loader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(val_dataset, batch_size=16)
        
        # EWC训练
        result = self.trainer.train_task(
            task_id=task_id,
            train_loader=train_loader,
            val_loader=val_loader,
            epochs=30,
            ewc_lambda=1000.0  # 根据任务重要性调整
        )
        
        return result
    
    def predict_with_uncertainty(self, founder: Dict, candidate: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        预测匹配成功概率，并给出不确定性估计
        """
        features = self.feature_extractor.encode({
            "founder": founder,
            "candidate": candidate
        })
        
        x = torch.FloatTensor([features]).to(self.trainer.device)
        
        self.model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            # MC Dropout获取不确定性（多次前向传播）
            self.model.train()  # 启用dropout
            predictions = []
            for _ in range(10):  # 10次采样
                pred = self.model(x).item()
                predictions.append(pred)
            
            mean_pred = sum(predictions) / len(predictions)
            uncertainty = (sum((p - mean_pred)**2 for p in predictions) / len(predictions)) ** 0.5
        
        return {
            "success_probability": mean_pred,
            "uncertainty": uncertainty,
            "confidence": 1 - uncertainty,  # 不确定性越高，置信度越低
            "recommendation": "proceed" if mean_pred > 0.7 and uncertainty < 0.1 else "review"
        }

class MatchingFeatureExtractor:
    """将业务特征编码为模型输入向量"""
    
    def encode(self, case: Dict) -> List[float]:
        features = []
        
        # 维度1: 互补性（归一化到0-1）
        comp_score = case.get("complementarity_score", 50) / 100.0
        features.append(comp_score)
        features.append(abs(comp_score - 0.5))  # 与中等水平的偏差
        
        # 维度2: 价值观（5维分别编码）
        for dim in ["ren", "yi", "li", "zhi", "xin"]:
            score = case.get("values", {}).get(dim, 0.5)
            features.append(score)
        
        # 维度3: 风险（3个指标）
        risk_factors = [
            case.get("equity_gap", 0.0),  # 股权期望差距
            case.get("exit_timeline_diff", 0.0) / 10.0,  # 退出时间差异（年）
            1.0 if case.get("has_litigation") else 0.0
        ]
        features.extend(risk_factors)
        
        # 维度4: 成长潜力
        growth_indicators = [
            case.get("learning_agility", 0.5),
            case.get("failure_recovery", 0.5),
            1.0 if case.get("has_mentor") else 0.0
        ]
        features.extend(growth_indicators)
        
        # 补充：行业匹配（one-hot或embedding）
        industry_match = 1.0 if case.get("industry_match") else 0.0
        features.append(industry_match)
        
        return features[:20]  # 确保维度一致

三、情报系统：具体数据源工程对接

3.1 多源数据采集架构

# intelligence_engine/data_connectors.py
import asyncio
import aiohttp
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import AsyncGenerator, Dict, List
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

class BaseDataConnector(ABC):
    """数据源连接器基类"""
    
    def __init__(self, config: Dict):
        self.config = config
        self.rate_limiter = asyncio.Semaphore(config.get("concurrency", 5))
        self.session = None
    
    async def __aenter__(self):
        self.session = aiohttp.ClientSession(
            headers=self._get_headers(),
            timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=30)
        )
        return self
    
    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
        if self.session:
            await self.session.close()
    
    @abstractmethod
    def _get_headers(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def fetch(self, query: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取数据"""
        pass
    
    async def safe_request(self, url: str, method: str = "GET", 
                          payload: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """带重试和限流的请求"""
        async with self.rate_limiter:
            for attempt in range(3):
                try:
                    if method == "GET":
                        async with self.session.get(url) as resp:
                            if resp.status == 429:  # Rate limited
                                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** attempt)
                                continue
                            resp.raise_for_status()
                            return await resp.json()
                    else:
                        async with self.session.post(url, json=payload) as resp:
                            resp.raise_for_status()
                            return await resp.json()
                            
                except aiohttp.ClientError as e:
                    if attempt == 2:
                        raise
                    await asyncio.sleep(2 ** attempt)
            
            return {}

# LinkedIn连接器（职业图谱）
class LinkedInConnector(BaseDataConnector):
    """
    LinkedIn数据获取（通过官方API或合规爬虫）
    注意：需遵守LinkedIn服务条款，建议使用官方API
    """
    
    def _get_headers(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.config['access_token']}",
            "X-Restli-Protocol-Version": "2.0.0",
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
        }
    
    async def fetch(self, query: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        获取候选人职业履历
        query: {"profile_url": "...", "depth": "basic|deep"}
        """
        profile_id = self._extract_profile_id(query["profile_url"])
        
        # 基础信息
        basic_url = f"https://api.linkedin.com/v2/people/{profile_id}"
        basic_data = await self.safe_request(basic_url)
        
        # 经历信息
        positions_url = f"https://api.linkedin.com/v2/people/{profile_id}/positions"
        positions = await self.safe_request(positions_url)
        
        # 技能背书
        skills_url = f"https://api.linkedin.com/v2/people/{profile_id}/skills"
        skills = await self.safe_request(skills_url)
        
        return [{
            "source": "linkedin",
            "profile_id": profile_id,
            "basic": self._parse_basic(basic_data),
            "positions": self._parse_positions(positions),
            "skills": self._parse_skills(skills),
            "collected_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }]
    
    def _parse_positions(self, data: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """解析职业经历，检测跳槽频率、晋升轨迹"""
        positions = []
        for pos in data.get("elements", []):
            positions.append({
                "title": pos.get("title"),
                "company": pos.get("company", {}).get("name"),
                "start_date": self._parse_date(pos.get("startDate")),
                "end_date": self._parse_date(pos.get("endDate")),
                "duration_months": self._calculate_duration(pos),
                "is_current": pos.get("endDate") is None
            })
        
        # 计算职业稳定性指标
        avg_tenure = sum(p["duration_months"] for p in positions) / len(positions) if positions else 0
        job_hop_pattern = "stable" if avg_tenure > 36 else "moderate" if avg_tenure > 18 else "frequent"
        
        return {
            "positions": positions,
            "metrics": {
                "average_tenure_months": avg_tenure,
                "total_companies": len(set(p["company"] for p in positions)),
                "job_hop_pattern": job_hop_pattern,  # 用于评估合伙人稳定性
                "promotion_trajectory": self._detect_promotions(positions)
            }
        }

# Crunchbase连接器（融资情报）
class CrunchbaseConnector(BaseDataConnector):
    """
    Crunchbase API：获取融资历史、估值、投资人信息
    """
    
    def _get_headers(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        return {"X-cb-user-key": self.config["api_key"]}
    
    async def fetch(self, query: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        查询公司或人物融资记录
        query: {"entity_type": "person|organization", "name": "..."}
        """
        if query["entity_type"] == "person":
            return await self._fetch_person_funding(query["name"])
        else:
            return await self._fetch_company_funding(query["name"])
    
    async def _fetch_person_funding(self, person_name: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取个人参与的融资事件（作为创始人或高管）"""
        url = f"https://api.crunchbase.com/api/v4/searches/funding_rounds"
        payload = {
            "field_ids": ["funding_round_money_raised", "funding_round_date", "investor_identifiers"],
            "query": [
                {"type": "predicate", "field_id": "founder_identifiers", "operator_id": "includes", "values": [person_name]}
            ]
        }
        
        data = await self.safe_request(url, "POST", payload)
        
        rounds = []
        for round_data in data.get("entities", []):
            rounds.append({
                "round_type": round_data.get("funding_type"),
                "amount": round_data.get("money_raised", {}).get("value_usd"),
                "date": round_data.get("announced_on"),
                "lead_investors": [i.get("value") for i in round_data.get("investor_identifiers", [])],
                "valuation": round_data.get("pre_money_valuation", {}).get("value_usd")
            })
        
        return [{
            "source": "crunchbase",
            "person_name": person_name,
            "funding_history": rounds,
            "total_raised": sum(r["amount"] for r in rounds if r["amount"]),
            "investor_quality": self._assess_investor_quality(rounds),
            "fundraising_ability_score": self._calculate_fundraising_score(rounds)
        }]

# 专利数据库连接器（技术能力验证）
class PatentConnector(BaseDataConnector):
    """
    全球专利数据库（USPTO, EPO, CNIPA）
    验证候选人的技术创新能力
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Dict):
        super().__init__(config)
        self.uspto_api = "https://developer.uspto.gov/api/v1/patents"
        self.cnipa_api = "https://api.cnipa.gov.cn/patents"  # 示例
    
    async def fetch(self, query: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        查询个人或公司的专利
        query: {"inventor_name": "...", "company": "..."}
        """
        results = []
        
        # 并行查询多个局
        tasks = [
            self._query_uspto(query),
            self._query_cnipa(query)
        ]
        
        uspto_data, cnipa_data = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
        
        all_patents = uspto_data + cnipa_data
        
        # 技术能力分析
        tech_domains = self._classify_tech_domains(all_patents)
        innovation_consistency = self._check_innovation_timeline(all_patents)
        
        return [{
            "source": "patent_db",
            "total_patents": len(all_patents),
            "granted_patents": len([p for p in all_patents if p["status"] == "granted"]),
            "pending_applications": len([p for p in all_patents if p["status"] == "pending"]),
            "tech_domains": tech_domains,  # 技术广度
            "citation_count": sum(p.get("cited_by_count", 0) for p in all_patents),  # 影响力
            "innovation_velocity": len([p for p in all_patents 
                                       if p["filing_date"] > datetime.now() - timedelta(days=365*3)]),  # 近3年活跃度
            "quality_score": self._calculate_patent_quality(all_patents)
        }]

# 企查查/天眼查连接器（中国企业工商司法）
class QichachaConnector(BaseDataConnector):
    """
    中国企业工商信息、司法风险、知识产权
    """
    
    def _get_headers(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "Authorization": f"Token {self.config['app_key']}:{self.config['secret']}"
        }
    
    async def fetch(self, query: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        查询个人关联企业风险
        query: {"person_name": "...", "id_number": "..."}
        """
        # 1. 查关联企业（担任法人、股东、高管）
        companies_url = f"https://api.qichacha.com/personSearch?key={query['person_name']}"
        companies = await self.safe_request(companies_url)
        
        risks = []
        for company in companies.get("Result", []):
            company_id = company["KeyNo"]
            
            # 2. 查企业司法风险
            risk_url = f"https://api.qichacha.com/getRiskDetail?key={company_id}"
            risk_data = await self.safe_request(risk_url)
            
            risks.append({
                "company_name": company["Name"],
                "role": company["Oper"],
                "risk_items": {
                    "lawsuits": risk_data.get("LawSuitCount", 0),
                    "executed_person": risk_data.get("ExecutedPerson", False),  # 失信被执行人
                    "judicial_sale": risk_data.get("JudicialSaleCount", 0),
                    "bankruptcy": risk_data.get("BankruptcyCount", 0)
                }
            })
        
        # 汇总个人风险画像
        total_lawsuits = sum(r["risk_items"]["lawsuits"] for r in risks)
        is_executed = any(r["risk_items"]["executed_person"] for r in risks)
        
        return [{
            "source": "qichacha",
            "associated_companies": len(risks),
            "total_lawsuits_as_associate": total_lawsuits,
            "personal_credit_risk": "high" if is_executed else "medium" if total_lawsuits > 5 else "low",
            "detailed_risks": risks,
            "recommendation": "reject" if is_executed else "proceed_with_caution" if total_lawsuits > 3 else "acceptable"
        }]

3.2 数据融合与清洗管道

# intelligence_engine/data_fusion.py
class DataFusionEngine:
    """
    多源数据融合引擎：解决冲突、补全缺失、置信度评估
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.connectors = {
            "linkedin": LinkedInConnector,
            "crunchbase": CrunchbaseConnector,
            "patent": PatentConnector,
            "qichacha": QichachaConnector
        }
        self.conflict_resolvers = ConflictResolver()
    
    async def build_comprehensive_profile(self, candidate_identity: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        构建候选人全景画像
        """
        tasks = []
        for source, connector_class in self.connectors.items():
            if self._should_query_source(source, candidate_identity):
                connector = connector_class(self._get_config(source))
                tasks.append(self._safe_fetch(connector, candidate_identity))
        
        # 并行采集所有数据源
        results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
        
        # 过滤失败的任务
        valid_results = [r for r in results if not isinstance(r, Exception)]
        
        # 数据融合
        fused_profile = self._fuse_data(valid_results)
        
        # 置信度评估
        confidence = self._calculate_data_confidence(valid_results)
        
        return {
            "profile": fused_profile,
            "confidence": confidence,
            "data_sources": [r["source"] for r in valid_results],
            "conflicts_detected": self.conflict_resolvers.get_conflicts(),
            "recommendations": self._generate_diligence_recommendations(fused_profile)
        }
    
    def _fuse_data(self, results: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """融合多源数据（实体对齐、属性合并）"""
        fused = {}
        
        # 1. 基础信息对齐（姓名、公司）
        fused["identity"] = self._resolve_identity_conflicts(results)
        
        # 2. 职业经历合并（时间线对齐，去重）
        fused["career"] = self._merge_career_timeline(results)
        
        # 3. 风险信息合并（并集，最高风险优先）
        fused["risks"] = self._merge_risk_indicators(results)
        
        # 4. 能力指标融合（加权平均，按数据源可信度加权）
        fused["capabilities"] = self._merge_capability_signals(results)
        
        return fused
    
    def _merge_capability_signals(self, results: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """
        合并能力信号（如LinkedIn的职业稳定性 + Crunchbase的融资能力）
        """
        signals = {}
        
        # 融资能力（仅Crunchbase有）
        cb_result = next((r for r in results if r["source"] == "crunchbase"), None)
        if cb_result:
            signals["fundraising_proven"] = cb_result.get("fundraising_ability_score", 0)
        
        # 技术深度（专利数据）
        patent_result = next((r for r in results if r["source"] == "patent_db"), None)
        if patent_result:
            signals["technical_depth"] = min(10, patent_result.get("quality_score", 0) / 10)
        
        # 职业稳定性（LinkedIn）
        li_result = next((r for r in results if r["source"] == "linkedin"), None)
        if li_result:
            stability = li_result.get("metrics", {})
            signals["stability"] = 10 if stability.get("job_hop_pattern") == "stable" else 5
        
        return signals

四、其他高价值扩展方向

4.1 图神经网络（GNN）关系推理

利用合伙人关系网络的结构特征：

# extensions/gnn_relational_reasoning.py
import torch_geometric.nn as gnn
from torch_geometric.data import Data

class PartnerRelationGNN(nn.Module):
    """
    图神经网络：分析合伙人关系网络中的结构洞、权力中心、信息流动
    """
    
    def __init__(self, in_channels: int, hidden_channels: int, out_channels: int):
        super().__init__()
        
        # GCN层：聚合邻居特征
        self.conv1 = gnn.GCNConv(in_channels, hidden_channels)
        self.conv2 = gnn.GCNConv(hidden_channels, out_channels)
        
        # 注意力层：识别关键关系
        self.attention = gnn.GATConv(out_channels, out_channels, heads=4)
    
    def forward(self, data: Data):
        x, edge_index = data.x, data.edge_index
        
        # 第一层卷积
        x = self.conv1(x, edge_index)
        x = F.relu(x)
        x = F.dropout(x, training=self.training)
        
        # 第二层卷积
        x = self.conv2(x, edge_index)
        
        # 注意力机制：识别关键关系边
        x, attention_weights = self.attention(x, edge_index, return_attention_weights=True)
        
        return x, attention_weights

class NetworkAnalyzer:
    """
    分析合伙人候选人在商业网络中的位置
    """
    
    def analyze_network_position(self, candidate_id: str, 
                                 relationship_graph: Data) -> Dict:
        """
        分析候选人的网络位置价值
        """
        model = PartnerRelationGNN(in_channels=10, hidden_channels=32, out_channels=16)
        
        # 前向传播
        embeddings, attention = model(relationship_graph)
        candidate_embedding = embeddings[candidate_id]
        
        # 计算结构洞（Structural Holes）：是否连接不同社群
        betweenness = self._calculate_betweenness_centrality(relationship_graph, candidate_id)
        
        # 计算网络约束（Network Constraint）：自由度
        constraint = self._calculate_network_constraint(relationship_graph, candidate_id)
        
        return {
            "structural_holes": betweenness,  # 高=桥接不同圈子，有价值
            "network_constraint": constraint,   # 低=自由度大，不受限
            "embedding": candidate_embedding.tolist(),
            "influence_score": float(torch.norm(candidate_embedding)),
            "brokerage_potential": "high" if betweenness > 0.7 and constraint < 0.3 else "medium"
        }

4.2 联邦学习（隐私保护）

在保护客户数据隐私前提下联合学习：

# extensions/federated_learning.py
import syft as sy  # OpenMined PySyft

class FederatedMatchingLearning:
    """
    联邦学习：多个满意解研究所分部联合训练模型，数据不出本地
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.hook = sy.TorchHook(torch)
        self.workers = []
    
    def add_institutional_node(self, institution_id: str, data_pointer):
        """添加机构节点（如北京、上海、深圳分部）"""
        worker = sy.VirtualWorker(self.hook, id=institution_id)
        worker.add_dataset(data_pointer, "local_cases")
        self.workers.append(worker)
    
    def federated_update(self, global_model: nn.Module, epochs: int = 5) -> nn.Module:
        """
        联邦平均（FedAvg）算法更新全局模型
        """
        local_models = []
        
        for worker in self.workers:
            # 本地训练
            local_model = copy.deepcopy(global_model)
            local_model.send(worker)  # 模型发送到本地
            
            # 本地数据训练（数据不离开本地）
            optimizer = torch.optim.SGD(local_model.parameters(), lr=0.01)
            for _ in range(epochs):
                # 使用本地数据
                data = worker.search("local_cases")[0]
                optimizer.zero_grad()
                output = local_model(data)
                loss = F.mse_loss(output, data.labels)
                loss.backward()
                optimizer.step()
            
            # 获取本地模型更新（仅参数，无数据）
            local_models.append(local_model.get())
        
        # 联邦平均：聚合各本地模型
        global_dict = global_model.state_dict()
        for key in global_dict.keys():
            global_dict[key] = torch.stack([m.state_dict()[key] for m in local_models]).mean(0)
        
        global_model.load_state_dict(global_dict)
        return global_model

4.3 区块链存证（不可篡改的匹配记录）

# extensions/blockchain_attestation.py
from web3 import Web3

class MatchingAttestationChain:
    """
    区块链存证：关键匹配决策上链，防止事后篡改，建立信任
    """
    
    def __init__(self, provider_url: str, contract_address: str):
        self.w3 = Web3(Web3.HTTPProvider(provider_url))
        self.contract = self.w3.eth.contract(
            address=contract_address,
            abi=self._load_abi()
        )
    
    async def attest_match_decision(self, 
                                   match_result: Dict, 
                                   founder_consent: bool) -> str:
        """
        将匹配决策哈希上链存证
        """
        if not founder_consent:
            raise PermissionError("需要创始人明确同意上链")
        
        # 生成决策指纹（哈希）
        decision_data = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "founder_id_hash": hashlib.sha256(match_result["founder_id"].encode()).hexdigest()[:16],
            "candidate_id_hash": hashlib.sha256(match_result["candidate_id"].encode()).hexdigest()[:16],
            "match_score": match_result["overall_score"],
            "algorithm_version": match_result["algorithm_version"],
            "recommendation": match_result["recommendation"]
        }
        
        decision_hash = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(decision_data, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
        
        # 上链交易
        tx_hash = self.contract.functions.storeAttestation(
            decision_hash,
            decision_data["timestamp"],
            int(decision_data["match_score"] * 100)  整型存储
        ).transact({
            "from": self.w3.eth.defaultAccount,
            "gas": 200000
        })
        
        return {
            "tx_hash": tx_hash.hex(),
            "decision_hash": decision_hash,
            "block_number": self.w3.eth.getTransactionReceipt(tx_hash).blockNumber,
            "status": "confirmed"
        }
    
    def verify_historical_decision(self, tx_hash: str, claimed_data: Dict) -> bool:
        """
        验证历史决策是否被篡改
        """
        # 从链上获取原始哈希
        on_chain_hash = self.contract.functions.getAttestation(tx_hash).call()
        
        # 重新计算声称数据的哈希
        recalculated_hash = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(claimed_data, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
        
        return on_chain_hash == recalculated_hash
