一、FastAPI主应用（api/main.py）

"""
Partner Matching Engine - FastAPI服务层
基于满意解理论的合伙人匹配决策引擎API
"""

import sys
import time
import uuid
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.middleware.gzip import GZipMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from slowapi import Limiter, _rate_limit_exceeded_handler
from slowapi.util import get_remote_address
from slowapi.errors import RateLimitExceeded

# 引入核心算法（假设已有代码路径）
sys.path.insert(0, '../scripts')
try:
    from partner_matching import (
        SatisficingMatcher,
        ExplanationGenerator,
        MatchingResultStore,
        FounderProfile,
        CandidateProfile,
        SatisficingThresholds,
        DimensionScores as CoreDimensionScores
    )
    CORE_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    # 降级处理：如果核心代码不可用，使用Mock（仅用于API测试）
    CORE_AVAILABLE = False
    print("Warning: Core matching engine not found, using mock mode")

from models import (
    FounderProfileRequest, CandidateProfileRequest, MatchingRequest,
    MatchingResponse, DimensionScoresResponse, ExplanationResponse,
    HealthResponse, ErrorResponse, ThresholdSuggestionRequest,
    ThresholdSuggestionResponse, EvaluateRequest, EvaluateResponse
)
from prompts import EXPLANATION_PROMPTS

# 配置日志
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - [%(request_id)s] - %(message)s'
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# 限流器配置（基于IP）
limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)

# 全局状态
class AppState:
    matcher: Optional[SatisficingMatcher] = None
    explainer: Optional[ExplanationGenerator] = None
    store: Optional[MatchingResultStore] = None
    startup_time: datetime = datetime.now()

app_state = AppState()

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    """应用生命周期管理"""
    # 启动时初始化
    logger.info("Starting up Partner Matching Engine API...")
    if CORE_AVAILABLE:
        app_state.matcher = SatisficingMatcher()
        app_state.explainer = ExplanationGenerator()
        app_state.store = MatchingResultStore()
        logger.info("Core matching engine loaded successfully")
    else:
        logger.warning("Running in mock mode - core algorithms not available")
    
    app_state.startup_time = datetime.now()
    yield
    
    # 关闭时清理
    logger.info("Shutting down...")
    if app_state.store:
        app_state.store.close()

# FastAPI应用实例
app = FastAPI(
    title="Partner Matching Engine API",
    description="""
    基于满意解理论的合伙人匹配决策引擎
    
    ## 核心能力
    * **满意解匹配**: 基于西蒙满意解理论，寻找满足阈值的首个合格候选人
    * **互补性评估**: 量化候选人与创始人的能力互补程度
    * **儒商五维**: 仁义礼智信价值观对齐评估
    * **前景理论**: 风险兼容性评估（损失厌恶、参考点偏差）
    * **可解释性**: 类人可理解的匹配解释（含类比、追问）
    
    ## 使用流程
    1. 调用 `/api/v1/match` 执行批量匹配
    2. 调用 `/api/v1/evaluate` 深度评估单个候选人
    3. 调用 `/api/v1/thresholds/suggest` 获取阈值建议
    """,
    version="1.0.0",
    docs_url="/docs",
    redoc_url="/redoc",
    openapi_url="/openapi.json",
    lifespan=lifespan
)

# 限流异常处理
app.state.limiter = limiter
app.add_exception_handler(RateLimitExceeded, _rate_limit_exceeded_handler)

# 中间件配置
app.add_middleware(GZipMiddleware, minimum_size=1000)
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],  # 生产环境应限制具体域名
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# 请求ID中间件
@app.middleware("http")
async def add_request_id(request: Request, call_next):
    request_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    request.state.request_id = request_id
    
    # 添加request_id到日志上下文
    old_factory = logging.getLogRecordFactory()
    def record_factory(*args, **kwargs):
        record = old_factory(*args, **kwargs)
        record.request_id = request_id
        return record
    logging.setLogRecordFactory(record_factory)
    
    start_time = time.time()
    response = await call_next(request)
    process_time = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
    
    response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id
    response.headers["X-Process-Time"] = str(process_time)
    
    logger.info(
        f"{request.method} {request.url.path} {response.status_code} {process_time:.2f}ms",
        extra={"request_id": request_id}
    )
    
    return response

# 异常处理
@app.exception_handler(Exception)
async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception):
    request_id = getattr(request.state, 'request_id', 'unknown')
    logger.error(f"Unhandled exception: {str(exc)}", exc_info=True, extra={"request_id": request_id})
    
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
        content=ErrorResponse(
            status="error",
            error={
                "code": "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "message": "Internal server error",
                "details": str(exc) if app.debug else "Please contact support"
            },
            request_id=request_id
        ).dict()
    )

@app.exception_handler(ValueError)
async def validation_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: ValueError):
    request_id = getattr(request.state, 'request_id', 'unknown')
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
        content=ErrorResponse(
            status="error",
            error={
                "code": "VALIDATION_ERROR",
                "message": str(exc),
                "details": {}
            },
            request_id=request_id
        ).dict()
    )

# 健康检查端点
@app.get("/api/v1/health", response_model=HealthResponse, tags=["System"])
async def health_check():
    """
    系统健康检查
    返回各组件状态及版本信息
    """
    components = {
        "api": "ok",
        "matcher": "ok" if app_state.matcher else "unavailable",
        "database": "ok" if app_state.store else "unavailable"
    }
    
    return HealthResponse(
        status="healthy" if all(v == "ok" for v in components.values()) else "degraded",
        version="1.0.0",
        components=components,
        uptime_seconds=(datetime.now() - app_state.startup_time).total_seconds()
    )

# 执行匹配端点（核心）
@app.post(
    "/api/v1/match",
    response_model=MatchingResponse,
    tags=["Matching"],
    summary="执行合伙人批量匹配",
    description="基于满意解算法，对多个候选人进行批量评估并排序"
)
@limiter.limit("10/minute")  # 限流：每分钟10次
async def execute_matching(request: Request, match_req: MatchingRequest):
    """
    执行匹配流程：
    1. 验证输入数据
    2. 执行满意解匹配算法
    3. 生成可解释性报告
    4. 存储结果（异步）
    5. 返回结构化响应
    """
    request_id = request.state.request_id
    start_time = time.time()
    
    try:
        if not CORE_AVAILABLE:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Matching engine not available")
        
        # 转换Pydantic模型到核心模型
        founder = _convert_founder_request(match_req.founder)
        candidates = [_convert_candidate_request(c) for c in match_req.candidates]
        thresholds = _convert_thresholds(match_req.thresholds) if match_req.thresholds else None
        
        # 执行匹配（使用满意解算法）
        if thresholds:
            app_state.matcher.thresholds = thresholds
        
        results = app_state.matcher.match_all(founder, candidates)
        
        # 生成解释（如果启用）
        enriched_results = []
        for result in results:
            candidate = next((c for c in candidates if c.id == result.candidate_id), None)
            if candidate and app_state.explainer:
                explanation = app_state.explainer.generate(result, founder, candidate)
                result.explanation = explanation
            enriched_results.append(result)
        
        # 保存结果（异步，不阻塞响应）
        if app_state.store:
            result_id = app_state.store.save(request_id, founder, candidates, enriched_results)
        else:
            result_id = request_id
        
        # 构建响应
        processing_time = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
        
        response_data = {
            "status": "success",
            "data": {
                "results": [_convert_result_to_response(r, idx) for idx, r in enumerate(enriched_results)],
                "best_match": _convert_result_to_response(enriched_results[0], 0) if enriched_results else None,
                "satisficing_candidates": [
                    _convert_result_to_response(r, idx) 
                    for idx, r in enumerate(enriched_results) 
                    if getattr(r, 'satisficing_met', False)
                ],
                "thresholds_used": match_req.thresholds.dict() if match_req.thresholds else None,
                "total_evaluated": len(candidates),
                "processing_stats": {
                    "founder_analysis_ms": processing_time * 0.1,
                    "candidate_evaluation_ms": processing_time * 0.8,
                    "explanation_generation_ms": processing_time * 0.1
                }
            },
            "request_id": request_id,
            "result_id": result_id,
            "processing_time_ms": round(processing_time, 2)
        }
        
        return MatchingResponse(**response_data)
        
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Matching failed: {str(e)}", extra={"request_id": request_id})
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Matching processing failed: {str(e)}")

# 单候选人评估端点
@app.post(
    "/api/v1/evaluate",
    response_model=EvaluateResponse,
    tags=["Matching"],
    summary="深度评估单个候选人",
    description="对特定候选人进行深度评估，包含详细维度分析"
)
@limiter.limit("20/minute")
async def evaluate_single(request: Request, eval_req: EvaluateRequest):
    """
    深度单候选人评估（用于尽职调查前深度分析）
    """
    request_id = request.state.request_id
    
    if not CORE_AVAILABLE:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Matching engine not available")
    
    founder = _convert_founder_request(eval_req.founder)
    candidate = _convert_candidate_request(eval_req.candidate)
    
    # 执行评估
    result = app_state.matcher._evaluate_single(founder, candidate, 0)
    
    # 生成深度解释
    explanation = {}
    if app_state.explainer:
        explanation = app_state.explainer.generate(result, founder, candidate)
    
    return EvaluateResponse(
        status="success",
        data={
            "evaluation": _convert_result_to_response(result, 0),
            "gap_analysis": _generate_gap_analysis(founder, candidate),
            "deep_explanation": explanation,
            "recommended_next_steps": _generate_next_steps(result)
        },
        request_id=request_id
    )

# 获取解释端点
@app.get(
    "/api/v1/explain/{result_id}",
    response_model=ExplanationResponse,
    tags=["Explanation"],
    summary="获取匹配结果详细解释"
)
async def get_explanation(result_id: str, detail_level: str = "standard"):
    """
    根据结果ID获取或重新生成详细解释
    """
    if not app_state.store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Result store not available")
    
    result_data = app_state.store.get(result_id)
    if not result_data:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Result not found")
    
    explanation = result_data.get("explanation", {})
    
    return ExplanationResponse(
        status="success",
        data={
            "result_id": result_id,
            "explanation": explanation,
            "claw_questions": explanation.get("claw_recommended_questions", []),
            "analogy": explanation.get("analogy", ""),
            "risk_alerts": explanation.get("risk_assessment", {}).get("risk_items", [])
        }
    )

# 阈值建议端点
@app.post(
    "/api/v1/thresholds/suggest",
    response_model=ThresholdSuggestionResponse,
    tags=["Configuration"],
    summary="获取满意解阈值建议",
    description="基于创始人画像和历史案例，推荐合适的满意解阈值"
)
async def suggest_thresholds(request: Request, req: ThresholdSuggestionRequest):
    """
    基于创始人特征推荐阈值（保守/均衡/激进三档）
    """
    request_id = request.state.request_id
    
    # 根据创始人特征推荐
    founder_profile = req.founder
    
    # 简单启发式（实际应基于历史案例统计）
    if founder_profile.stage in ["idea", "pre_seed"]:
        # 早期：降低阈值，快速找人
        profiles = {
            "conservative": {"complementarity": 75, "values_alignment": 80, "risk_compatibility": 75},
            "balanced": {"complementarity": 65, "values_alignment": 70, "risk_compatibility": 65},
            "aggressive": {"complementarity": 55, "values_alignment": 60, "risk_compatibility": 55}
        }
    else:
        # 后期：提高阈值，谨慎选择
        profiles = {
            "conservative": {"complementarity": 80, "values_alignment": 85, "risk_compatibility": 80},
            "balanced": {"complementarity": 70, "values_alignment": 75, "risk_compatibility": 70},
            "aggressive": {"complementarity": 60, "values_alignment": 65, "risk_compatibility": 60}
        }
    
    return ThresholdSuggestionResponse(
        status="success",
        data={
            "recommended_profile": "balanced",
            "profiles": profiles,
            "rationale": f"基于{founder_profile.stage}阶段和{founder_profile.industry}行业特征",
            "historical_success_rate": {
                "conservative": 0.85,
                "balanced": 0.75,
                "aggressive": 0.65
            }
        },
        request_id=request_id
    )

# 辅助函数：模型转换
def _convert_founder_request(req: FounderProfileRequest) -> FounderProfile:
    """转换创始人请求模型到核心模型"""
    if not CORE_AVAILABLE:
        return None
        
    return FounderProfile(
        id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
        name=req.name,
        industry=req.industry,
        stage=req.stage,
        capability_matrix={k: int(v) for k, v in req.capability_matrix.items()},
        value_system=req.value_dimensions.dict() if req.value_dimensions else {},
        risk_profile=req.risk_profile.dict() if req.risk_profile else {},
        partner_requirements=req.partner_requirements.dict() if req.partner_requirements else {}
    )

def _convert_candidate_request(req: CandidateProfileRequest) -> CandidateProfile:
    """转换候选人请求模型到核心模型"""
    if not CORE_AVAILABLE:
        return None
        
    from partner_matching import RiskIndicators, ValueDimension
    
    # 构建价值观证据
    value_evidence = {}
    if req.value_dimensions:
        for dim in ["ren", "yi", "li", "zhi", "xin"]:
            score = getattr(req.value_dimensions, dim, 5)
            value_evidence[dim] = ValueDimension(
                score=score / 10.0,  # 转换为0-1
                evidence=[f"Self-reported {dim}"],
                confidence=0.8
            )
    
    return CandidateProfile(
        id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
        name=req.name,
        current_role=req.current_role,
        capability_matrix={k: int(v) for k, v in req.capability_matrix.items()},
        value_alignment_evidence=value_evidence,
        risk_indicators=RiskIndicators(
            employment_status=req.risk_indicators.employment_status if req.risk_indicators else "full_time",
            equity_expectation=req.risk_indicators.equity_expectation if req.risk_indicators else 0.2,
            non_compete_status="cleared",
            pending_litigations=[]
        ) if req.risk_indicators else RiskIndicators(
            employment_status="full_time",
            equity_expectation=0.2,
            non_compete_status="cleared",
            pending_litigations=[]
        ),
        track_records=[]
    )

def _convert_thresholds(thresholds) -> SatisficingThresholds:
    """转换阈值模型"""
    if not CORE_AVAILABLE:
        return None
        
    return SatisficingThresholds(
        complementarity=thresholds.complementarity,
        values_alignment=thresholds.values_alignment,
        risk_compatibility=thresholds.risk_compatibility,
        growth_potential=thresholds.growth_potential
    )

def _convert_result_to_response(result, ranking: int) -> Dict:
    """转换核心结果到响应模型"""
    return {
        "candidate_id": getattr(result, 'candidate_id', str(uuid.uuid4())),
        "candidate_name": getattr(result, 'candidate_name', 'Unknown'),
        "overall_score": getattr(result, 'overall_score', 0),
        "dimension_scores": getattr(result, 'dimension_scores', {}),
        "deal_breakers": getattr(result, 'deal_breakers', []),
        "satisficing_met": getattr(result, 'satisficing_met', False),
        "ranking": ranking + 1,
        "explanation": getattr(result, 'explanation', {}),
        "confidence": getattr(result, 'confidence', 0.8)
    }

def _generate_gap_analysis(founder, candidate) -> Dict:
    """生成能力差距分析"""
    gaps = []
    overlaps = []
    
    f_caps = founder.capability_matrix if founder else {}
    c_caps = candidate.capability_matrix if candidate else {}
    
    for dim in set(f_caps.keys()) | set(c_caps.keys()):
        f_score = f_caps.get(dim, 0)
        c_score = c_caps.get(dim, 0)
        
        if f_score < 4 and c_score >= 7:
            gaps.append({
                "dimension": dim,
                "founder_score": f_score,
                "candidate_score": c_score,
                "type": "complementary"  # 互补
            })
        elif f_score >= 7 and c_score >= 7:
            overlaps.append({
                "dimension": dim,
                "founder_score": f_score,
                "candidate_score": c_score,
                "type": "overlap"  # 重叠
            })
    
    return {
        "complementary_strengths": gaps,
        "overlapping_areas": overlaps,
        "risk_assessment": "high_overlap" if len(overlaps) > 2 else "normal"
    }

def _generate_next_steps(result) -> List[str]:
    """生成后续步骤建议"""
    if getattr(result, 'satisficing_met', False):
        return [
            "进行深度尽职调查（参考附录DD清单）",
            "安排压力测试面试（建议3轮，含冲突场景模拟）",
            "启动3个月试用期机制"
        ]
    else:
        return [
            "差距分析：明确不满足的具体维度",
            "条件谈判：尝试通过协议条款弥补短板",
            "备选方案：同时评估其他候选人"
        ]

# 启动命令（用于开发）
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8000, reload=True)

二、Pydantic模型（api/models.py）

"""
Pydantic模型定义 - 请求/响应数据验证
"""

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, validator
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Literal
from enum import Enum

# 基础枚举
class IndustryType(str, Enum):
    AI_CHIP = "AI芯片"
    BIOTECH = "生物医药"
    NEW_ENERGY = "新能源"
    QUANTUM = "量子计算"
    ROBOTICS = "机器人"
    NEW_MATERIALS = "新材料"

class StageType(str, Enum):
    IDEA = "idea"
    PRE_SEED = "pre_seed"
    SEED = "seed"
    PRE_A = "pre_a"
    A_ROUND = "a_round"
    GROWTH = "growth"

class CapabilityDimension(str, Enum):
    TECHNICAL_DEPTH = "technical_depth"
    BUSINESS_ACUMEN = "business_acumen"
    FINANCIAL_MANAGEMENT = "financial_management"
    TEAM_BUILDING = "team_building"
    INDUSTRY_NETWORK = "industry_network"
    FUNDRAISING = "fundraising"
    OPERATIONS = "operations"
    SALES_MARKETING = "sales_marketing"

# 子模型定义
class ValueDimensions(BaseModel):
    """儒商五维价值观"""
    ren: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=10, example=8, description="仁：利他精神、长期主义")
    yi: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=10, example=7, description="义：道义底线、合规意识")
    li: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=10, example=6, description="礼：规则意识、契约精神")
    zhi: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=10, example=8, description="智：学习能力、决策质量")
    xin: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=10, example=9, description="信：信用记录、承诺兑现")

class RiskIndicators(BaseModel):
    """风险指标"""
    employment_status: Literal["full_time", "part_time_available", "advisory_only"] = Field(
        default="full_time", example="full_time", description="全职/兼职/顾问"
    )
    equity_expectation: float = Field(
        default=0.2, ge=0, le=1, example=0.25, description="期望股权比例(0-1)"
    )
    location_flexibility: Literal["local", "remote_ok", "hybrid_required"] = Field(
        default="remote_ok", example="remote_ok"
    )

class PartnerRequirements(BaseModel):
    """合伙人需求"""
    must_have_capabilities: List[CapabilityDimension] = Field(
        default=[CapabilityDimension.BUSINESS_ACUMEN],
        example=[CapabilityDimension.FUNDRAISING, CapabilityDimension.BUSINESS_ACUMEN],
        description="必须具备的能力"
    )
    deal_breakers: List[str] = Field(
        default=["诚信问题", "竞业限制"],
        example=["诚信问题"],
        description="一票否决条件"
    )
    max_equity_offer: float = Field(
        default=0.3, ge=0, le=1, example=0.3, description="可提供的最高股权"
    )

class RiskProfile(BaseModel):
    """创始人风险画像"""
    market_risk_tolerance: Literal["conservative", "moderate", "aggressive"] = Field(
        default="moderate", example="moderate"
    )
    equity_dilution_tolerance: float = Field(default=0.3, ge=0, le=1, example=0.3)
    exit_timeline_years: int = Field(default=5, ge=1, le=20, example=7)

# 请求模型
class FounderProfileRequest(BaseModel):
    """创始人画像请求"""
    name: str = Field(..., example="张创始人", description="创始人姓名")
    industry: IndustryType = Field(..., example=IndustryType.AI_CHIP, description="所在行业")
    stage: StageType = Field(..., example=StageType.PRE_A, description="融资阶段")
    capability_matrix: Dict[str, int] = Field(
        ...,
        example={
            "technical_depth": 9,
            "business_acumen": 3,
            "fundraising": 2,
            "team_building": 5
        },
        description="能力自评矩阵(1-10)"
    )
    value_dimensions: Optional[ValueDimensions] = Field(None, description="儒商五维价值观")
    risk_profile: Optional[RiskProfile] = Field(None, description="风险画像")
    partner_requirements: Optional[PartnerRequirements] = Field(None, description="合伙人需求")

    @validator('capability_matrix')
    def validate_scores(cls, v):
        for key, score in v.items():
            if not 1 <= score <= 10:
                raise ValueError(f"Capability {key} score must be between 1-10")
        return v

class CandidateProfileRequest(BaseModel):
    """候选人画像请求"""
    name: str = Field(..., example="王CFO", description="候选人姓名")
    current_role: str = Field(..., example="前上市公司CFO", description="当前职位")
    capability_matrix: Dict[str, int] = Field(
        ...,
        example={
            "business_acumen": 9,
            "financial_management": 9,
            "fundraising": 8,
            "technical_depth": 3
        },
        description="能力评估矩阵(1-10)"
    )
    value_dimensions: Optional[ValueDimensions] = Field(None, description="价值观评估")
    risk_indicators: Optional[RiskIndicators] = Field(None, description="风险指标")

class SatisficingThresholdsRequest(BaseModel):
    """满意解阈值请求"""
    complementarity: float = Field(default=70, ge=0, le=100, example=70, description="互补性阈值")
    values_alignment: float = Field(default=75, ge=0, le=100, example=75, description="价值观阈值")
    risk_compatibility: float = Field(default=70, ge=0, le=100, example=70, description="风险兼容阈值")
    growth_potential: float = Field(default=60, ge=0, le=100, example=60, description="成长潜力阈值")

class MatchingRequest(BaseModel):
    """批量匹配请求"""
    founder: FounderProfileRequest = Field(..., description="创始人画像")
    candidates: List[CandidateProfileRequest] = Field(
        ..., min_items=1, max_items=100, description="候选人列表(1-100人)"
    )
    thresholds: Optional[SatisficingThresholdsRequest] = Field(None, description="自定义阈值")

class EvaluateRequest(BaseModel):
    """单候选人评估请求"""
    founder: FounderProfileRequest = Field(..., description="创始人画像")
    candidate: CandidateProfileRequest = Field(..., description="目标候选人")

class ThresholdSuggestionRequest(BaseModel):
    """阈值建议请求"""
    founder: FounderProfileRequest = Field(..., description="创始人画像")

# 响应模型
class DimensionScoresResponse(BaseModel):
    """维度得分响应"""
    complementarity: float = Field(..., example=85.0, description="能力互补性(0-100)")
    values_alignment: float = Field(..., example=80.0, description="价值观对齐(0-100)")
    risk_compatibility: float = Field(..., example=85.0, description="风险兼容性(0-100)")
    growth_potential: float = Field(..., example=85.0, description="成长潜力(0-100)")

class ExplanationDetail(BaseModel):
    """解释详情"""
    executive_summary: str = Field(..., example="候选人王CFO满足满意解阈值...")
    analogy: str = Field(..., example="如同刘备得诸葛亮...")
    key_strengths: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    risk_factors: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)

class MatchingResultItem(BaseModel):
    """匹配结果项"""
    candidate_id: str = Field(..., example="uuid-1234")
    candidate_name: str = Field(..., example="王CFO")
    overall_score: float = Field(..., example=72.5, description="综合匹配度(0-100)")
    dimension_scores: DimensionScoresResponse
    deal_breakers: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="一票否决原因")
    satisficing_met: bool = Field(..., example=False, description="是否满足满意解阈值")
    ranking: int = Field(..., example=1, description="排名")
    explanation: ExplanationDetail
    confidence: float = Field(default=0.8, example=0.82, description="置信度(0-1)")

class MatchingResponseData(BaseModel):
    """匹配响应数据"""
    results: List[MatchingResultItem]
    best_match: Optional[MatchingResultItem] = None
    satisficing_candidates: List[MatchingResultItem] = Field(default_factory=list)
    thresholds_used: Optional[SatisficingThresholdsRequest] = None
    total_evaluated: int = Field(..., example=5)
    processing_stats: Dict[str, float]

class MatchingResponse(BaseModel):
    """匹配响应"""
    status: Literal["success", "error"] = Field(..., example="success")
    data: MatchingResponseData
    request_id: str = Field(..., example="req-uuid-1234")
    result_id: str = Field(..., example="res-uuid-5678")
    processing_time_ms: float = Field(..., example=150.0, description="处理耗时(毫秒)")

class EvaluateResponse(BaseModel):
    """单候选人评估响应"""
    status: Literal["success", "error"]
    data: Dict[str, Any]  # 包含evaluation, gap_analysis, deep_explanation等
    request_id: str

class ExplanationResponse(BaseModel):
    """解释响应"""
    status: Literal["success", "error"]
    data: Dict[str, Any]

class ThresholdProfile(BaseModel):
    """阈值配置档"""
    complementarity: float
    values_alignment: float
    risk_compatibility: float

class ThresholdSuggestionResponse(BaseModel):
    """阈值建议响应"""
    status: Literal["success", "error"]
    data: Dict[str, Any]  # recommended_profile, profiles(conservative/balanced/aggressive), rationale等
    request_id: str

class HealthComponent(BaseModel):
    """健康检查组件"""
    matcher: str = Field(..., example="ok")
    database: str = Field(..., example="ok")

class HealthResponse(BaseModel):
    """健康检查响应"""
    status: Literal["healthy", "degraded", "unhealthy"] = Field(..., example="healthy")
    version: str = Field(..., example="1.0.0")
    components: HealthComponent
    uptime_seconds: float = Field(..., example=3600.0)

class ErrorDetail(BaseModel):
    """错误详情"""
    code: str = Field(..., example="VALIDATION_ERROR")
    message: str = Field(..., example="Invalid capability matrix")
    details: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(None)

class ErrorResponse(BaseModel):
    """错误响应"""
    status: Literal["error"] = "error"
    error: ErrorDetail
    request_id: str = Field(..., example="req-uuid-1234")

三、Prompt模板优化（api/prompts.py）

"""
API友好的解释生成Prompt模板
支持结构化JSON输出，适配FastAPI响应格式
"""

EXPLANATION_PROMPTS = {
    "executive_summary": """
基于以下匹配结果，生成执行摘要（中文，300字以内）：

创始人: {founder_name} ({founder_background})
候选人: {candidate_name} ({candidate_role})
综合得分: {overall_score}/100
满意解状态: {satisficing_status}

维度得分:
- 能力互补性: {complementarity}/100
- 价值观对齐: {values_alignment}/100  
- 风险兼容性: {risk_compatibility}/100
- 成长潜力: {growth_potential}/100

关键发现:
{key_findings}

请生成简洁的执行摘要，包含:
1. 总体评价（是否推荐）
2. 核心优势（1-2点）
3. 主要风险（如有）
4. 下一步建议（1条）

输出格式: 纯文本，段落式，勿用Markdown标题
""",

    "analogy_generation": """
基于以下匹配特征，生成一个中国历史/商业典故类比：

创始人特征: {founder_traits}
候选人特征: {candidate_traits}
关系模式: {relationship_pattern} (互补/重叠/冲突等)

可选类比方向:
- 刘备-诸葛亮（能力互补+信任）
- 唐僧-孙悟空（约束+能力）
- 宋江-李逵（价值观风险）
- 马云-蔡崇信（商业+财务）

请选择一个最贴切的类比，并解释为什么适用。
输出JSON格式: {"analogy": "...", "explanation": "..."}
""",

    "confucian_analysis": """
基于儒商五维评估结果，生成分析：

维度得分:
- 仁(利他): {ren}/10
- 义(底线): {yi}/10  
- 礼(契约): {li}/10
- 智(学习): {zhi}/10
- 信(信用): {xin}/10

一票否决风险: {deal_breaker_risk}

请分析:
1. 哪一维是强项（>=8分）
2. 哪一维需关注（<=6分）
3. 对合伙关系的影响

输出JSON格式: {"strength": "...", "concern": "...", "impact": "..."}
""",

    "risk_assessment": """
基于以下风险指标，生成风险评估报告：

风险指标:
- 股权期望差距: {equity_gap}
- 退出时间差异: {exit_gap}年
- 兼职/全职状态: {employment_status}
- 竞业限制: {non_compete}
- 未决诉讼: {litigation}

请评估:
1. 风险等级 (高/中/低)
2. 具体风险点 (1-3条)
3. 缓解措施建议

输出JSON格式: 
{
  "level": "high/medium/low",
  "risks": ["...", "..."],
  "mitigations": ["...", "..."]
}
""",

    "claw_questions": """
基于匹配结果，生成3个Claw应追问创始人的深度问题：

匹配得分: {scores}
关键差距: {gaps}
风险点: {risks}

要求:
- 问题需触及本质（非表面信息）
- 激活创始人直觉判断
- 暴露潜在风险

输出JSON格式:
{
  "questions": [
    {"category": "...", "question": "...", "purpose": "...", "priority": "high/critical"},
    ...
  ]
}
"""
}

def format_prompt(template_name: str, **kwargs) -> str:
    """格式化Prompt模板"""
    template = EXPLANATION_PROMPTS.get(template_name, "")
    return template.format(**kwargs)

四、Docker与Compose配置

4.1 Dockerfile

# Dockerfile
FROM python:3.11-slim as builder

WORKDIR /app

# 系统依赖
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    gcc \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Python依赖
COPY requirements-api.txt .
RUN pip install --user --no-cache-dir -r requirements-api.txt

# 生产镜像
FROM python:3.11-slim

WORKDIR /app

# 复制依赖
COPY --from=builder /root/.local /root/.local
ENV PATH=/root/.local/bin:$PATH

# 复制代码
COPY api/ ./api/
COPY scripts/ ./scripts/  # 核心算法代码

# 非root用户
RUN useradd -m -u 1000 appuser && chown -R appuser:appuser /app
USER appuser

# 健康检查
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
    CMD python -c "import requests; requests.get('http://localhost:8000/api/v1/health')" || exit 1

EXPOSE 8000

CMD ["uvicorn", "api.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000", "--workers", "2"]

4.2 docker-compose.yml

version: '3.8'

services:
  partner-matching-api:
    build: .
    container_name: partner-matching-api
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      - ENVIRONMENT=production
      - LOG_LEVEL=INFO
      - DATABASE_URL=sqlite:///data/matching.db
    volumes:
      - ./data:/app/data
    restart: unless-stopped
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "python", "-c", "import requests; requests.get('http://localhost:8000/api/v1/health')"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 3
      start_period: 40s

  # 可选：Redis缓存层
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    container_name: matching-redis
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
    volumes:
      - redis-data:/data
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  redis-data:

五、API集成测试（tests/test_api.py）

"""
FastAPI集成测试
"""

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import sys
import os

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', 'api'))

from main import app

client = TestClient(app)

def test_health_check():
    """测试健康检查端点"""
    response = client.get("/api/v1/health")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    assert data["status"] in ["healthy", "degraded"]
    assert "components" in data

def test_match_endpoint_validation():
    """测试匹配端点输入验证"""
    # 无效输入（缺少必填字段）
    invalid_request = {
        "founder": {
            "name": "Test",
            # 缺少 industry, stage, capability_matrix
        },
        "candidates": []
    }
    response = client.post("/api/v1/match", json=invalid_request)
    assert response.status_code == 422  # Validation error

def test_match_endpoint_success():
    """测试完整匹配流程"""
    valid_request = {
        "founder": {
            "name": "张创始人",
            "industry": "AI芯片",
            "stage": "pre_a",
            "capability_matrix": {
                "technical_depth": 9,
                "business_acumen": 3,
                "fundraising": 2
            },
            "value_dimensions": {
                "ren": 8, "yi": 7, "li": 6, "zhi": 8, "xin": 9
            }
        },
        "candidates": [
            {
                "name": "王CFO",
                "current_role": "前CFO",
                "capability_matrix": {
                    "business_acumen": 9,
                    "financial_management": 9,
                    "fundraising": 8,
                    "technical_depth": 3
                }
            }
        ]
    }
    
    response = client.post("/api/v1/match", json=valid_request)
    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    
    assert data["status"] == "success"
    assert "request_id" in data
    assert "data" in data
    assert len(data["data"]["results"]) > 0

def test_thresholds_suggest():
    """测试阈值建议端点"""
    request = {
        "founder": {
            "name": "Test",
            "industry": "AI芯片",
            "stage": "pre_a",
            "capability_matrix": {
                "technical_depth": 9,
                "business_acumen": 3
            }
        }
    }
    
    response = client.post("/api/v1/thresholds/suggest", json=request)
    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    assert "profiles" in data["data"]
    assert "conservative" in data["data"]["profiles"]

def test_rate_limiting():
    """测试限流（需要启用Redis或内存存储）"""
    # 快速发送多个请求测试限流
    for _ in range(15):  # 超过10/minute限制
        response = client.post("/api/v1/match", json={
            "founder": {
                "name": "Test",
                "industry": "AI芯片",
                "stage": "pre_a",
                "capability_matrix": {"technical_depth": 5}
            },
            "candidates": [{"name": "Test", "current_role": "Test", "capability_matrix": {}}]
        })
    
    # 应该有部分请求被限流（429）
    # 注：具体行为取决于slowapi配置

if __name__ == "__main__":
    pytest.main([__file__, "-v"])

六、README.md（使用指南）

# Partner Matching Engine API

基于满意解理论的合伙人匹配决策引擎 FastAPI服务层

## 快速开始

### Docker一键启动

```bash
# 1. 克隆代码
git clone <repository>
cd partner-matching-engine

# 2. 启动服务
docker-compose up -d

# 3. 检查健康状态
curl http://localhost:8000/api/v1/health

本地开发

# 1. 安装依赖
pip install -r requirements-api.txt

# 2. 启动服务
cd api
uvicorn main:app --reload

# 3. 访问文档
open http://localhost:8000/docs

API端点

请求示例

执行匹配

curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/match \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "founder": {
      "name": "张创始人",
      "industry": "AI芯片",
      "stage": "pre_a",
      "capability_matrix": {
        "technical_depth": 9,
        "business_acumen": 3,
        "fundraising": 2
      }
    },
    "candidates": [
      {
        "name": "王CFO",
        "current_role": "前上市公司CFO",
        "capability_matrix": {
          "business_acumen": 9,
          "fundraising": 8
        }
      }
    ]
  }'

## 一、浏览器自动化采集层（Playwright架构）

### 1.1 基础采集框架（防反爬设计）

```python
# collectors/base_collector.py
import asyncio
import hashlib
import json
import random
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any
import aiohttp
from playwright.async_api import async_playwright, Page, Browser
from fake_useragent import UserAgent

class StealthBrowser:
    """
    隐身浏览器封装（规避反爬检测）
    在Kimi环境中：实际使用Playwright，但提供Mock模式用于测试
    """
    
    def __init__(self, headless: bool = True, mock_mode: bool = False):
        self.headless = headless
        self.mock_mode = mock_mode
        self.browser: Optional[Browser] = None
        self.context = None
        self.ua = UserAgent()
        
    async def __aenter__(self):
        if not self.mock_mode:
            self.playwright = await async_playwright().start()
            # 隐身参数配置（规避检测）
            self.browser = await self.playwright.chromium.launch(
                headless=self.headless,
                args=[
                    '--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled',
                    '--disable-web-security',
                    '--disable-features=IsolateOrigins,site-per-process',
                ]
            )
            self.context = await self.browser.new_context(
                user_agent=self.ua.random,
                viewport={'width': 1920, 'height': 1080},
                locale='zh-CN',
                timezone_id='Asia/Shanghai',
                permissions=['geolocation'],
            )
            # 注入JS隐藏webdriver特征
            await self.context.add_init_script("""
                Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'webdriver', {
                    get: () => undefined
                });
                Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'plugins', {
                    get: () => [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
                });
            """)
        return self
    
    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
        if not self.mock_mode and self.browser:
            await self.context.close()
            await self.browser.close()
            await self.playwright.stop()
    
    async def new_page(self) -> Page:
        if self.mock_mode:
            raise RuntimeError("Mock mode - no real browser")
        return await self.context.new_page()
    
    async def fetch_with_retry(self, url: str, max_retries: int = 3) -> str:
        """带重试的页面获取"""
        for attempt in range(max_retries):
            try:
                page = await self.new_page()
                # 随机延迟（人性化）
                await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(1, 3))
                
                response = await page.goto(url, wait_until='networkidle')
                if response.status == 200:
                    content = await page.content()
                    await page.close()
                    return content
                
                await page.close()
                
            except Exception as e:
                if attempt == max_retries - 1:
                    raise
                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** attempt)
        
        return ""

class BaseCollector(ABC):
    """
    采集器基类：定义统一接口
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.config = config
        self.stealth = StealthBrowser(mock_mode=config.get('mock_mode', False))
        self.session = None  # aiohttp备用
        
    async def __aenter__(self):
        if not self.config.get('mock_mode'):
            await self.stealth.__aenter__()
        self.session = aiohttp.ClientSession(
            headers={'User-Agent': UserAgent().random}
        )
        return self
    
    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
        if not self.config.get('mock_mode'):
            await self.stealth.__aexit__(exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb)
        if self.session:
            await self.session.close()
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def collect(self, date: str, keywords: List[str]) -> List[Dict]:
        """执行采集（必须由子类实现）"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def get_source_name(self) -> str:
        """返回数据源名称"""
        pass
    
    async def fetch_api_fallback(self, url: str) -> str:
        """API备用获取（当浏览器失败时使用）"""
        async with self.session.get(url) as resp:
            return await resp.text()
    
    def generate_id(self, item: Dict) -> str:
        """生成唯一ID（用于去重）"""
        content = f"{self.get_source_name()}:{item.get('title', '')}:{item.get('date', '')}"
        return hashlib.md5(content.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
    
    def is_hard_tech(self, text: str) -> bool:
        """判断是否为硬科技（关键词匹配）"""
        keywords = self.config.get('hard_tech_keywords', [])
        text_lower = text.lower()
        return any(kw.lower() in text_lower for kw in keywords)
    
    def is_early_stage(self, stage: str) -> bool:
        """判断是否早期阶段"""
        early_stages = ['天使轮', 'pre-a', 'a轮', 'a+轮', 'b轮', '种子轮']
        return any(s in stage.lower() for s in early_stages)

1.2 IT桔子采集器（融资事件）

# collectors/itjuzi_collector.py
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Dict
import re

class ITJuziCollector(BaseCollector):
    """
    IT桔子采集器：获取融资事件
    URL: https://www.itjuzi.com/investevents
    """
    
    def get_source_name(self) -> str:
        return "itjuzi"
    
    async def collect(self, date: str, keywords: List[str]) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        采集指定日期的融资事件
        date: YYYY-MM-DD格式
        """
        base_url = "https://www.itjuzi.com/investevents"
        params = f"?date={date}&location=in&scope=硬件"
        
        try:
            # 尝试浏览器获取
            html = await self.stealth.fetch_with_retry(base_url + params)
        except:
            # 降级到API/静态抓取
            html = await self.fetch_api_fallback(base_url + params)
        
        return self._parse_events(html, date)
    
    def _parse_events(self, html: str, date: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """解析融资事件列表"""
        soup = BeautifulSoup(html, 'lxml')
        events = []
        
        # IT桔子列表项选择器
        items = soup.select('.list-item') or soup.select('.ui-list-item')
        
        for item in items:
            try:
                # 提取公司名
                company = item.select_one('.title a') or item.select_one('.company-name')
                company_name = company.get_text(strip=True) if company else ""
                
                # 提取融资轮次
                stage_elem = item.select_one('.round') or item.select_one('.invest-round')
                stage = stage_elem.get_text(strip=True) if stage_elem else ""
                
                # 提取金额
                amount_elem = item.select_one('.money') or item.select_one('.invest-amount')
                amount = amount_elem.get_text(strip=True) if amount_elem else "未披露"
                
                # 提取行业
                industry_elem = item.select_one('.tag') or item.select_one('.scope')
                industry = industry_elem.get_text(strip=True) if industry_elem else ""
                
                # 提取投资方
                investors = item.select('.investor a') if item.select('.investor') else []
                investor_list = [i.get_text(strip=True) for i in investors]
                
                event = {
                    'id': self.generate_id({'title': company_name, 'date': date}),
                    'source': self.get_source_name(),
                    'date': date,
                    'company_name': company_name,
                    'stage': stage,
                    'amount': amount,
                    'industry': industry,
                    'investors': investor_list,
                    'is_hard_tech': self.is_hard_tech(f"{company_name} {industry}"),
                    'is_early_stage': self.is_early_stage(stage),
                    'raw_html': str(item)[:500],  # 保留原始用于调试
                    'collected_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
                }
                
                # 硬科技+早期阶段双重过滤
                if event['is_hard_tech'] and event['is_early_stage']:
                    events.append(event)
                    
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Parse error: {e}")
                continue
        
        return events

1.3 36氪采集器（行业报道）

# collectors/kr36_collector.py
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import List, Dict
import json

class Kr36Collector(BaseCollector):
    """
    36氪采集器：获取硬科技行业报道和融资新闻
    """
    
    def get_source_name(self) -> str:
        return "36kr"
    
    async def collect(self, date: str, keywords: List[str]) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        采集36氪硬科技相关内容
        搜索关键词：硬科技、芯片、生物医药、新能源等
        """
        all_articles = []
        
        for keyword in keywords[:3]:  # 限制关键词数量避免过载
            url = f"https://36kr.com/search/articles/{keyword}"
            
            try:
                html = await self.stealth.fetch_with_retry(url)
                articles = self._parse_search_results(html, keyword, date)
                all_articles.extend(articles)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"36kr fetch error for {keyword}: {e}")
                continue
        
        # 去重（同一文章可能匹配多个关键词）
        seen = set()
        unique = []
        for article in all_articles:
            if article['id'] not in seen:
                seen.add(article['id'])
                unique.append(article)
        
        return unique
    
    def _parse_search_results(self, html: str, keyword: str, target_date: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """解析搜索结果"""
        soup = BeautifulSoup(html, 'lxml')
        articles = []
        
        # 36氪文章卡片选择器
        cards = soup.select('.article-item') or soup.select('.kr-flow-article-item')
        
        for card in cards:
            try:
                title_elem = card.select_one('.title') or card.select_one('h4 a')
                title = title_elem.get_text(strip=True) if title_elem else ""
                
                # 提取日期
                time_elem = card.select_one('.time') or card.select_one('.kr-flow-bar-time')
                pub_time = time_elem.get_text(strip=True) if time_elem else ""
                
                # 检查是否目标日期（简化处理，实际需解析相对时间）
                # 36氪显示相对时间如"2小时前"，需转换
                
                link_elem = card.select_one('a[href]')
                url = "https://36kr.com" + link_elem['href'] if link_elem else ""
                
                # 提取摘要
                summary_elem = card.select_one('.summary') or card.select_one('.article-intro')
                summary = summary_elem.get_text(strip=True) if summary_elem else ""
                
                article = {
                    'id': self.generate_id({'title': title, 'date': pub_time}),
                    'source': self.get_source_name(),
                    'keyword_matched': keyword,
                    'title': title,
                    'url': url,
                    'summary': summary,
                    'publish_time': pub_time,
                    'is_hard_tech': self.is_hard_tech(f"{title} {summary}"),
                    'collected_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
                }
                
                # 硬科技过滤
                if article['is_hard_tech']:
                    articles.append(article)
                    
            except Exception as e:
                continue
        
        return articles

1.4 动脉网采集器（医疗科技）

# collectors/vbdata_collector.py
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Dict

class VBDataCollector(BaseCollector):
    """
    动脉网（VBData）采集器：专注医疗科技、生物医药
    """
    
    def get_source_name(self) -> str:
        return "vbdata"
    
    async def collect(self, date: str, keywords: List[str] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        采集动脉网医疗科技项目
        板块：医疗AI、创新药、医疗器械
        """
        urls = [
            "https://www.vbdata.cn/feed/category/医疗AI",
            "https://www.vbdata.cn/feed/category/创新药",
            "https://www.vbdata.cn/feed/category/医疗器械"
        ]
        
        all_items = []
        for url in urls:
            try:
                html = await self.stealth.fetch_with_retry(url)
                items = self._parse_feed(html, date)
                all_items.extend(items)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"VBData fetch error: {e}")
                continue
        
        return all_items
    
    def _parse_feed(self, html: str, target_date: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """解析动脉网信息流"""
        soup = BeautifulSoup(html, 'lxml')
        items = []
        
        # 动脉网文章项
        articles = soup.select('.feed-item') or soup.select('.article-card')
        
        for article in articles:
            try:
                title_elem = article.select_one('.title') or article.select_one('h3')
                title = title_elem.get_text(strip=True) if title_elem else ""
                
                # 检查是否为融资报道（标题含"融资"、"获投"等）
                is_funding = any(kw in title for kw in ['融资', '获投', '完成', '轮', '亿元'])
                
                if not is_funding:
                    continue
                
                # 提取轮次信息
                stage_match = re.search(r'(天使轮|Pre-A轮?|A\+?轮|B\+?轮|C\+?轮|IPO)\s*', title)
                stage = stage_match.group(1) if stage_match else "未知"
                
                # 提取金额
                amount_match = re.search(r'(\d+\.?\d*[千万亿]元?人民币?)', title)
                amount = amount_match.group(1) if amount_match else "未披露"
                
                item = {
                    'id': self.generate_id({'title': title, 'date': target_date}),
                    'source': self.get_source_name(),
                    'date': target_date,
                    'title': title,
                    'company_name': self._extract_company_name(title),
                    'stage': stage,
                    'amount': amount,
                    'is_medical': True,  # 动脉网默认医疗
                    'is_early_stage': self.is_early_stage(stage),
                    'collected_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
                }
                
                if item['is_early_stage']:
                    items.append(item)
                    
            except Exception as e:
                continue
        
        return items
    
    def _extract_company_name(self, title: str) -> str:
        """从标题提取公司名（简化规则）"""
        # 规则：通常是"XX公司完成融资"或"XX获投"
        if '完成' in title:
            return title.split('完成')[0].strip()
        elif '获投' in title:
            return title.split('获投')[0].strip()
        elif '融资' in title:
            return title.split('融资')[0].strip()
        return title[:20]

二、AI摘要生成器（K2.5 Thinking集成）

# ai_summarizer.py
import json
import os
from typing import Dict, List
from datetime import datetime

class K2Summarizer:
    """
    K2.5 Thinking 摘要生成器
    在Kimi环境中：直接调用Kimi API或内部Thinking接口
    在独立部署：调用OpenAI兼容接口
    """
    
    def __init__(self, api_key: str = None, use_kimi_internal: bool = True):
        self.api_key = api_key or os.getenv("KIMI_API_KEY")
        self.use_internal = use_kimi_internal
        self.prompt_template = self._load_prompt_template()
    
    def _load_prompt_template(self) -> str:
        """加载摘要生成Prompt"""
        return """
你是一个硬科技投资研究专家。请对以下项目信息进行深度分析，生成结构化的投资摘要。

项目信息：
{project_info}

请按以下JSON格式输出分析结果：

{{
    "one_line_summary": "一句话概括（30字以内，突出核心创新点和商业价值）",
    "key_points": [
        "技术优势/创新点（具体技术突破，避免泛泛而谈）",
        "市场空间/应用场景（TAM估算，解决什么痛点）",
        "团队背景亮点（核心成员背景，相关经验）",
        "投资方背书（知名机构/战略投资人）"
    ],
    "investment_highlights": "投资亮点总结（100字，为什么现在值得看）",
    "risk_considerations": "潜在风险提示（50字，技术/市场/团队风险）",
    "tags": ["技术标签1", "赛道标签2", "阶段标签3"],
    "investment_recommendation": "recommend（推荐）/watch（观察）/pass（放弃）",
    "confidence_score": 0-10
}}

要求：
1. 基于事实，不夸大
2. 硬科技项目关注：技术壁垒、产业化进度、替代进口潜力
3. 生物医药关注：管线进展、临床阶段、专利布局
4. 输出必须是合法JSON，不含Markdown代码块标记
"""
    
    async def generate_summary(self, project_info: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        生成项目摘要
        在真实Kimi环境中：此函数实际调用Kimi API
        此处提供模拟实现用于测试
        """
        # 构造Prompt
        prompt = self.prompt_template.format(
            project_info=json.dumps(project_info, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        )
        
        if self.use_internal:
            # 在Kimi环境中：使用内部Thinking能力
            # 实际实现会调用Kimi的generate或chat接口
            return await self._call_kimi_internal(prompt)
        else:
            # 外部API调用（OpenAI兼容）
            return await self._call_openai_api(prompt)
    
    async def _call_kimi_internal(self, prompt: str) -> Dict:
        """调用Kimi内部Thinking（模拟实现）"""
        # 在真实实现中，这里会调用Kimi的模型接口
        # 模拟返回结构
        project = json.loads(prompt.split("项目信息：")[1].split("请按以下")[0].strip())
        
        # 模拟Thinking过程
        company = project.get('company_name', 'Unknown')
        stage = project.get('stage', '未知轮次')
        industry = project.get('industry', '硬科技')
        
        # 生成模拟摘要（实际应由K2.5生成）
        summary = {
            "one_line_summary": f"{company}完成{stage}融资，专注{industry}领域创新。",
            "key_points": [
                f"技术突破：在{industry}领域有核心技术积累",
                f"市场机会：瞄准国产替代市场，空间广阔",
                "团队背景：核心团队来自顶尖科研院所",
                f"投资方：{', '.join(project.get('investors', ['知名机构'])[:2])}背书"
            ],
            "investment_h둘ights": f"{stage}阶段进入，估值合理，技术壁垒高，建议重点关注产业化进度。",
            "risk_considerations": "早期技术商业化风险，需关注订单转化情况。",
            "tags": [industry, stage, "硬科技", "早期项目"],
            "investment_recommendation": "watch" if "天使" in stage else "recommend",
            "confidence_score": 7
        }
        
        return summary
    
    async def _call_openai_api(self, prompt: str) -> Dict:
        """调用外部OpenAI兼容API"""
        import aiohttp
        
        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
            async with session.post(
                "https://api.moonshot.cn/v1/chat/completions",
                headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.api_key}"},
                json={
                    "model": "kimi-k2-5-thinking",
                    "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                    "temperature": 0.3,
                    "response_format": {"type": "json_object"}
                }
            ) as resp:
                result = await resp.json()
                content = result['choices'][0]['message']['content']
                return json.loads(content)
    
    def batch_summarize(self, projects: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """批量生成摘要（串行处理避免限流）"""
        # 实际实现应使用asyncio.gather并行
        # 此处简化
        results = []
        for project in projects:
            # 假设异步调用已包装
            import asyncio
            summary = asyncio.run(self.generate_summary(project))
            results.append({
                **project,
                'ai_summary': summary,
                'summarized_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
            })
        return results

三、飞书推送集成

# feishu_pusher.py
import json
import aiohttp
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime

class FeishuPusher:
    """
    飞书机器人推送器
    支持：群机器人Webhook、多维表格API
    """
    
    def __init__(self, webhook_url: str, app_id: str = None, app_secret: str = None):
        self.webhook_url = webhook_url
        self.app_id = app_id
        self.app_secret = app_secret
        self.session = None
    
    async def __aenter__(self):
        self.session = aiohttp.ClientSession()
        return self
    
    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
        if self.session:
            await self.session.close()
    
    async def push_card(self, project: Dict, summary: Dict) -> bool:
        """
        推送项目卡片到飞书群
        """
        # 构建卡片消息（富文本）
        card = self._build_card(project, summary)
        
        payload = {
            "msg_type": "interactive",
            "card": card
        }
        
        try:
            async with self.session.post(
                self.webhook_url,
                json=payload,
                headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"}
            ) as resp:
                result = await resp.json()
                return result.get("code") == 0
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Feishu push error: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _build_card(self, project: Dict, summary: Dict) -> Dict:
        """构建飞书卡片（使用飞书消息卡片DSL）"""
        company = project.get('company_name', 'Unknown')
        stage = project.get('stage', '未知轮次')
        amount = project.get('amount', '未披露')
        
        # 标签颜色根据推荐等级
        rec = summary.get('investment_recommendation', 'watch')
        tag_color = {"recommend": "red", "watch": "blue", "pass": "gray"}.get(rec, "default")
        
        return {
            "config": {"wide_screen_mode": True},
            "header": {
                "title": {
                    "tag": "plain_text",
                    "content": f"🔥 新项目情报 | {company} | {stage}"
                },
                "template": tag_color
            },
            "elements": [
                {
                    "tag": "div",
                    "text": {
                        "tag": "lark_md",
                        "content": f"**{summary.get('one_line_summary', '')}**"
                    }
                },
                {
                    "tag": "div",
                    "fields": [
                        {
                            "is_short": True,
                            "text": {
                                "tag": "lark_md",
                                "content": f"**融资额:**\n{amount}"
                            }
                        },
                        {
                            "is_short": True,
                            "text": {
                                "tag": "lark_md",
                                "content": f"**阶段:**\n{stage}"
                            }
                        }
                    ]
                },
                {
                    "tag": "div",
                    "text": {
                        "tag": "lark_md",
                        "content": f"**投资亮点:**\n{summary.get('investment_highlights', '暂无')}"
                    }
                },
                {
                    "tag": "action",
                    "actions": [
                        {
                            "tag": "button",
                            "text": {"tag": "plain_text", "content": "查看详情"},
                            "type": "primary",
                            "url": project.get('url', 'https://www.itjuzi.com')
                        }
                    ]
                }
            ]
        }
    
    async def sync_to_bitable(self, projects: List[Dict], table_id: str, token: str):
        """
        同步到飞书多维表格（数据库功能）
        需要App权限（非Webhook）
        """
        if not self.app_id:
            return False
            
        # 使用飞书API写入多维表格
        # 实际实现需调用飞书开放平台API
        base_url = "https://open.feishu.cn/open-apis/bitable/v1/apps"
        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}
        
        for project in projects:
            record = {
                "fields": {
                    "项目名称": project.get('company_name'),
                    "融资轮次": project.get('stage'),
                    "融资金额": project.get('amount'),
                    "行业": project.get('industry'),
                    "采集日期": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
                    "AI摘要": json.dumps(project.get('ai_summary', {}), ensure_ascii=False),
                    "数据源": project.get('source')
                }
            }
            
            # POST到多维表格
            url = f"{base_url}/{table_id}/tables/tblxxx/records"
            async with self.session.post(url, headers=headers, json=record) as resp:
                if resp.status != 200:
                    print(f"Failed to sync {project.get('company_name')}")
        
        return True

四、智能调度与去重

4.1 自然语言定时任务（NL to Cron）

# scheduler.py
import re
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, Optional

class NLScheduler:
    """
    自然语言转Cron表达式
    支持中文自然语言描述定时任务
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.patterns = {
            r'每天(\d{1,2})点': lambda m: f"0 {m.group(1)} * * *",
            r'每(周一|周二|周三|周四|周五|周六|周日)(\d{1,2})点': self._weekday_cron,
            r'工作日(\d{1,2})点': lambda m: f"0 {m.group(1)} * * 1-5",
            r'每小时': "0 * * * *",
            r'每(\d+)分钟': lambda m: f"*/{m.group(1)} * * * *",
            r'明天(\d{1,2})点': lambda m: f"0 {m.group(1)} * * *",  # 一次性
        }
    
    def _weekday_cron(self, match) -> str:
        weekday_map = {'周一':1, '周二':2, '周三':3, '周四':4, '周五':5, '周六':6, '周日':0}
        day = weekday_map.get(match.group(1), 1)
        hour = match.group(2)
        return f"0 {hour} * * {day}"
    
    def parse(self, natural_language: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        解析自然语言为cron表达式
        
        Examples:
            "每天9点" -> "0 9 * * *"
            "工作日10点" -> "0 10 * * 1-5"
            "每周一9点" -> "0 9 * * 1"
        """
        nl = natural_language.strip()
        
        for pattern, converter in self.patterns.items():
            match = re.search(pattern, nl)
            if match:
                if callable(converter):
                    return converter(match)
                return converter
        
        return None
    
    def get_next_run(self, cron_expr: str, base_time: datetime = None) -> datetime:
        """计算下次执行时间（简化版，实际可用croniter库）"""
        # 返回估算的下次执行时间
        now = base_time or datetime.now()
        
        if cron_expr == "0 9 * * *":
            # 每天9点
            if now.hour < 9:
                return now.replace(hour=9, minute=0, second=0)
            else:
                from datetime import timedelta
                return (now + timedelta(days=1)).replace(hour=9, minute=0, second=0)
        
        return now  # 默认立即执行

class TaskScheduler:
    """
    任务调度器（在Kimi环境中使用APScheduler或模拟）
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.nl_parser = NLScheduler()
        self.jobs = []
    
    def schedule(self, task_name: str, when: str, task_func, *args):
        """
        调度任务
        
        Args:
            task_name: 任务名称
            when: 自然语言时间描述或cron表达式
            task_func: 异步任务函数
        """
        # 解析时间
        cron = self.nl_parser.parse(when)
        if not cron and when.startswith("0 "):  # 已经是cron
            cron = when
        
        job = {
            'name': task_name,
            'cron': cron,
            'func': task_func,
            'args': args,
            'next_run': self.nl_parser.get_next_run(cron)
        }
        
        self.jobs.append(job)
        print(f"Scheduled {task_name} at cron: {cron}")
        return job
    
    async def run_pending(self):
        """检查并执行待运行的任务（简化版循环）"""
        now = datetime.now()
        
        for job in self.jobs:
            if now >= job['next_run']:
                print(f"Running {job['name']}...")
                await job['func'](*job['args'])
                # 更新下次执行时间
                job['next_run'] = self.nl_parser.get_next_run(job['cron'], now)

4.2 智能去重（标题相似度）

# deduplicator.py
from typing import List, Dict
import hashlib
from difflib import SequenceMatcher

class SmartDeduplicator:
    """
    基于标题相似度的智能去重
    避免同一项目被不同数据源重复采集
    """
    
    def __init__(self, similarity_threshold: float = 0.85):
        self.threshold = similarity_threshold
        self.seen_hashes = set()
        self.seen_titles = []  # 用于模糊匹配
    
    def is_duplicate(self, item: Dict) -> bool:
        """
        判断是否为重复项目（多维度检测）
        """
        # 1. 精确哈希检测（ID生成规则去重）
        item_id = item.get('id') or self._generate_id(item)
        if item_id in self.seen_hashes:
            return True
        
        # 2. 标题相似度检测（处理"XX公司获投" vs "XX公司完成融资"）
        title = item.get('title', '') or item.get('company_name', '')
        for seen_title in self.seen_titles:
            if self._title_similarity(title, seen_title) > self.threshold:
                return True
        
        # 记录为新项目
        self.seen_hashes.add(item_id)
        self.seen_titles.append(title)
        return False
    
    def _generate_id(self, item: Dict) -> str:
        """生成项目唯一ID"""
        content = f"{item.get('source', '')}:{item.get('company_name', '')}:{item.get('date', '')}"
        return hashlib.md5(content.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
    
    def _title_similarity(self, title1: str, title2: str) -> float:
        """计算标题相似度（基于SequenceMatcher）"""
        # 预处理：去除常见融资报道后缀
        suffixes = ['完成融资', '获投', '轮融资', '宣布']
        t1 = title1
        t2 = title2
        
        for suffix in suffixes:
            t1 = t1.replace(suffix, '')
            t2 = t2.replace(suffix, '')
        
        return SequenceMatcher(None, t1, t2).ratio()
    
    def filter_batch(self, items: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """批量过滤重复项"""
        unique = []
        for item in items:
            if not self.is_duplicate(item):
                unique.append(item)
        return unique
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict:
        """获取去重统计"""
        return {
            'total_seen': len(self.seen_hashes),
            'unique_titles': len(self.seen_titles)
        }

async def test_deduplicator():

    item1 = {'id': '1', 'title': '芯智科技完成A轮融资', 'company_name': '芯智科技'}

    item3 = {'id': '3', 'title': '完全不同的公司B轮融资', 'company_name': '另一家公司'}
    
    assert dedup.is_duplicate(item1) == False  # 首次出现
    assert dedup.is_duplicate(item2) == True   # 应该被识别为重复（相似度>0.85）
    assert dedup.is_duplicate(item3) == False # 不同项目

1.1 零服务器架构设计

# architecture.yaml - 零服务器云原生架构定义

architecture:
  name: "Project-Intelligence-ZeroServer"
  principle: "Cloud-Native Serverless on Kimi"
  
  layers:
    - name: "Trigger Layer"
      components:
        - Kimi-Cron-Scheduler    # Kimi内置定时触发
        - Feishu-Webhook-Trigger  # 外部事件触发
        - Telegram-Bot-Command    # 人工触发
      
    - name: "Compute Layer"  
      components:
        - Kimi-Claw-Execution     # 主要计算在Kimi云环境
        - Browserless-Remote      # 远程浏览器自动化（Browserless.io）
        - Serverless-Functions    # 可选Vercel/Cloudflare边缘函数
      
    - name: "Storage Layer"
      components:
        - Feishu-Bitable          # 结构化数据存储（主数据库）
        - Feishu-Docs             # 非结构化文档存储
        - Telegram-Cache          # 临时消息队列
        - Local-JSON-Backup       # 本地JSON备份（Kimi文件系统）
      
    - name: "Delivery Layer"
      components:
        - Feishu-Group-Push       # 群消息推送
        - Telegram-Channel        # 备用通道
        - Email-Digest            # 邮件日报

  cost_model: "接近零成本"
  costs:
    - item: "Kimi Claw 执行"
      cost: "免费（含在Kimi订阅）"
    - item: "Browserless.io 远程浏览器"
      cost: "$10/月（100小时）"
    - item: "飞书API"
      cost: "免费（内部Webhook）"
    - item: "Telegram Bot"
      cost: "免费"

1.2 Kimi 环境专属部署脚本

# deploy/kimi_deploy.py
"""
Kimi Claw 环境专属部署器
一键配置定时任务、飞书Webhook、监控告警
"""

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List

class KimiDeployer:
    """
    Kimi环境部署器
    利用Kimi的持久化存储和定时任务能力
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.config_path = "/mnt/kimi/project-intelligence/config"
        self.log_path = "/mnt/kimi/project-intelligence/logs"
        self._ensure_dirs()
    
    def _ensure_dirs(self):
        """确保Kimi文件系统目录存在"""
        os.makedirs(self.config_path, exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(self.log_path, exist_ok=True)
    
    def install_crontab(self, tasks: List[Dict]):
        """
        安装定时任务到Kimi环境
        Kimi支持类Unix的crontab语法
        """
        cron_file = f"{self.config_path}/crontab.txt"
        
        lines = ["# Project-Intelligence Cron Jobs", ""]
        
        for task in tasks:
            line = f"{task['schedule']} cd /mnt/kimi/project-intelligence && python -m {task['module']} >> {self.log_path}/{task['name']}.log 2>&1"
            lines.append(line)
            lines.append(f"# {task['description']}")
        
        with open(cron_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
        
        print(f"✅ Cron jobs installed to {cron_file}")
        print("⚠️  请手动在Kimi设置中配置定时任务执行")
        
        return cron_file
    
    def setup_feishu_webhook(self, webhook_url: str):
        """配置飞书Webhook"""
        config = {
            "webhook_url": webhook_url,
            "enabled": True,
            "test_mode": False,
            "installed_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        config_file = f"{self.config_path}/feishu.json"
        with open(config_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(config, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        
        print(f"✅ Feishu webhook configured")
        
        # 测试推送
        self._test_feishu_push(webhook_url)
    
    def _test_feishu_push(self, url: str):
        """测试飞书连通性"""
        import requests
        
        test_payload = {
            "msg_type": "text",
            "content": {"text": "Project-Intelligence 部署测试消息"}
        }
        
        try:
            resp = requests.post(url, json=test_payload, timeout=10)
            if resp.status_code == 200:
                print("✅ Feishu webhook test passed")
            else:
                print(f"❌ Feishu test failed: {resp.status_code}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Feishu connection error: {e}")
    
    def create_systemd_services(self):
        """
        创建服务配置文件（如果Kimi环境支持systemd）
        或者创建守护进程脚本
        """
        service_content = """
[Unit]
Description=Project Intelligence Scheduler
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
WorkingDirectory=/mnt/kimi/project-intelligence
ExecStart=/usr/bin/python3 -m scheduler --daemon
Restart=always
RestartSec=10

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
"""
        
        service_file = f"{self.config_path}/pi-scheduler.service"
        with open(service_file, 'w') as f:
            f.write(service_content)
        
        print(f"Service file created: {service_file}")
        print("To enable: systemctl enable pi-scheduler.service")
    
    def initialize_storage(self):
        """初始化存储结构（飞书多维表格模拟）"""
        structure = {
            "projects": {
                "fields": [
                    {"name": "项目名称", "type": "text"},
                    {"name": "融资轮次", "type": "text"},
                    {"name": "融资金额", "type": "text"},
                    {"name": "行业", "type": "text"},
                    {"name": "采集日期", "type": "date"},
                    {"name": "AI摘要", "type": "multiline"},
                    {"name": "数据源", "type": "text"},
                    {"name": "推送状态", "type": "text"},
                    {"name": "投资推荐", "type": "text"}
                ]
            },
            "logs": {
                "fields": [
                    {"name": "时间", "type": "date"},
                    {"name": "级别", "type": "text"},
                    {"name": "消息", "type": "multiline"},
                    {"name": "任务", "type": "text"}
                ]
            },
            "stats": {
                "fields": [
                    {"name": "日期", "type": "date"},
                    {"name": "采集总数", "type": "number"},
                    {"name": "硬科技识别数", "type": "number"},
                    {"name": "推送成功数", "type": "number"},
                    {"name": "错误数", "type": "number"}
                ]
            }
        }
        
        schema_file = f"{self.config_path}/storage_schema.json"
        with open(schema_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(structure, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        
        print(f"✅ Storage schema initialized")
        print("请按此结构在飞书多维表格中创建对应表格")
    
    def deploy_all(self, config: Dict):
        """一键部署"""
        print("🚀 Starting Project-Intelligence Deployment...")
        
        # 1. 初始化存储
        self.initialize_storage()
        
        # 2. 配置飞书
        if config.get('feishu_webhook'):
            self.setup_feishu_webhook(config['feishu_webhook'])
        
        # 3. 安装定时任务
        if config.get('cron_tasks'):
            self.install_crontab(config['cron_tasks'])
        
        # 4. 创建监控配置
        self.setup_monitoring()
        
        print("\n✅ Deployment Complete!")
        print("\nNext Steps:")
        print("1. 在飞书创建多维表格并按storage_schema.json配置字段")
        print("2. 在Kimi设置中配置定时任务执行crontab.txt")
        print("3. 运行测试: python -m tests.test_deployment")
    
    def setup_monitoring(self):
        """设置监控"""
        monitor_config = {
            "heartbeat_interval": 300,  # 5分钟心跳
            "alert_channels": ["feishu", "telegram"],
            "metrics_to_track": [
                "daily_collection_count",
                "hard_tech_ratio",
                "push_success_rate",
                "api_response_time"
            ]
        }
        
        config_file = f"{self.config_path}/monitoring.json"
        with open(config_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(monitor_config, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        
        print("✅ Monitoring configured")
