二、监控告警体系（零服务器实现）

2.1 健康检查与心跳监控

# monitoring/health_monitor.py
import json
import time
import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List
import aiohttp

class ZeroServerMonitor:
    """
    零服务器监控器
    利用飞书/ Telegram作为监控面板
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Dict):
        self.config = config
        self.heartbeat_file = "/mnt/kimi/project-intelligence/logs/heartbeat.json"
        self.alert_cooldown = {}  # 防止告警风暴
        
    async def start_monitoring(self):
        """启动监控循环"""
        while True:
            try:
                await self._check_system_health()
                await self._send_heartbeat()
                await asyncio.sleep(self.config.get('heartbeat_interval', 300))
            except Exception as e:
                await self._alert_error("Monitor Loop Error", str(e))
                await asyncio.sleep(60)
    
    async def _check_system_health(self):
        """检查系统健康状态"""
        checks = {
            "storage": await self._check_storage(),
            "collectors": await self._check_collectors(),
            "feishu": await self._check_feishu_connection(),
            "last_collection": await self._check_last_collection()
        }
        
        # 记录状态
        status = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "checks": checks,
            "overall": "healthy" if all(c['status'] == 'ok' for c in checks.values()) else "degraded"
        }
        
        # 写入状态日志
        with open(self.heartbeat_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(status, f, ensure_ascii=False)
        
        # 如果有异常，发送告警
        for check_name, check_result in checks.items():
            if check_result['status'] != 'ok':
                await self._alert_error(
                    f"Health Check Failed: {check_name}",
                    check_result.get('message', 'Unknown error')
                )
    
    async def _check_storage(self) -> Dict:
        """检查存储（飞书API连通性）"""
        try:
            # 尝试读取飞书表格
            # 实际实现调用飞书API
            return {"status": "ok", "latency_ms": 150}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}
    
    async def _check_collectors(self) -> Dict:
        """检查采集器状态"""
        # 检查最近一次采集时间
        last_run = self._get_last_run_time()
        if last_run and datetime.now() - last_run < timedelta(hours=2):
            return {"status": "ok", "last_run": last_run.isoformat()}
        else:
            return {
                "status": "warning", 
                "message": "No collection in last 2 hours",
                "last_run": last_run.isoformat() if last_run else None
            }
    
    async def _check_feishu_connection(self) -> Dict:
        """检查飞书连接"""
        try:
            # 发送测试消息
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.post(
                    self.config['feishu_webhook'],
                    json={"msg_type": "text", "content": {"text": "Health check"}},
                    timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=10)
                ) as resp:
                    if resp.status == 200:
                        return {"status": "ok"}
                    else:
                        return {"status": "error", "message": f"HTTP {resp.status}"}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}
    
    async def _check_last_collection(self) -> Dict:
        """检查最近一次采集数据质量"""
        stats = self._get_today_stats()
        
        if stats['total'] == 0:
            return {"status": "warning", "message": "No data collected today"}
        elif stats['hard_tech_ratio'] < 0.5:
            return {"status": "warning", "message": f"Low hard-tech ratio: {stats['hard_tech_ratio']:.1%}"}
        else:
            return {
                "status": "ok", 
                "today_total": stats['total'],
                "hard_tech_count": stats['hard_tech_count']
            }
    
    async def _send_heartbeat(self):
        """发送心跳（记录到日志）"""
        heartbeat = {
            "time": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "status": "alive",
            "memory_usage": self._get_memory_usage(),
            "disk_usage": self._get_disk_usage()
        }
        
        # 每24小时发送一次正常心跳到飞书（避免打扰）
        if datetime.now().hour == 9 and datetime.now().minute < 5:
            await self._send_status_card(heartbeat)
    
    async def _alert_error(self, title: str, message: str):
        """发送告警（带冷却）"""
        alert_key = f"{title}:{message[:50]}"
        now = time.time()
        
        # 检查冷却（同一告警30分钟内不重复）
        if alert_key in self.alert_cooldown:
            if now - self.alert_cooldown[alert_key] < 1800:
                return
        
        self.alert_cooldown[alert_key] = now
        
        # 构建告警卡片
        alert_card = {
            "msg_type": "interactive",
            "card": {
                "config": {"wide_screen_mode": True},
                "header": {
                    "title": {"tag": "plain_text", "content": f"🚨 系统告警: {title}"},
                    "template": "red"
                },
                "elements": [
                    {
                        "tag": "div",
                        "text": {
                            "tag": "lark_md",
                            "content": f"**时间:** {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}\n**详情:** {message}"
                        }
                    },
                    {
                        "tag": "action",
                        "actions": [
                            {
                                "tag": "button",
                                "text": {"tag": "plain_text", "content": "查看日志"},
                                "type": "primary",
                                "url": "file:///mnt/kimi/project-intelligence/logs"
                            }
                        ]
                    }
                ]
            }
        }
        
        # 发送到飞书
        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
            await session.post(self.config['feishu_webhook'], json=alert_card)
    
    async def _send_status_card(self, status: Dict):
        """发送状态卡片"""
        card = {
            "msg_type": "interactive",
            "card": {
                "header": {
                    "title": {"tag": "plain_text", "content": "✅ 系统心跳正常"},
                    "template": "green"
                },
                "elements": [
                    {
                        "tag": "div",
                        "text": {
                            "tag": "lark_md",
                            "content": f"**系统时间:** {status['time']}\n**状态:** {status['status']}"
                        }
                    }
                ]
            }
        }
        
        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
            await session.post(self.config['feishu_webhook'], json=card)
    
    def _get_last_run_time(self) -> datetime:
        """获取最后执行时间（从日志文件）"""
        try:
            with open(self.heartbeat_file, 'r') as f:
                data = json.load(f)
                return datetime.fromisoformat(data['timestamp'])
        except:
            return None
    
    def _get_today_stats(self) -> Dict:
        """获取今日统计"""
        # 从飞书表格或本地缓存读取
        return {"total": 15, "hard_tech_count": 12, "hard_tech_ratio": 0.8}
    
    def _get_memory_usage(self) -> str:
        """获取内存使用（Kimi环境可能受限）"""
        try:
            import psutil
            return f"{psutil.virtual_memory().percent}%"
        except:
            return "N/A"
    
    def _get_disk_usage(self) -> str:
        """获取磁盘使用"""
        try:
            import psutil
            return f"{psutil.disk_usage('/mnt/kimi').percent}%"
        except:
            return "N/A"

# 监控启动脚本
async def start_monitor():
    config = {
        "feishu_webhook": "your-webhook-url",
        "heartbeat_interval": 300,  # 5分钟
        "alert_channels": ["feishu"]
    }
    
    monitor = ZeroServerMonitor(config)
    await monitor.start_monitoring()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(start_monitor())

2.2 日志聚合与分析

# monitoring/log_aggregator.py
import json
import gzip
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict

class LogAggregator:
    """
    日志聚合器（Kimi环境文件系统实现）
    """
    
    def __init__(self, log_dir: str = "/mnt/kimi/project-intelligence/logs"):
        self.log_dir = Path(log_dir)
        self.log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
    def write_log(self, level: str, message: str, task: str = None, extra: Dict = None):
        """写入结构化日志"""
        entry = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "level": level,
            "message": message,
            "task": task,
            "extra": extra or {}
        }
        
        # 按日期分文件
        date_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        log_file = self.log_dir / f"{date_str}.log"
        
        with open(log_file, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n')
    
    def get_recent_logs(self, hours: int = 24, level: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取最近日志"""
        logs = []
        cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=hours)
        
        # 读取最近几天的日志文件
        for i in range(3):
            date_str = (datetime.now() - timedelta(days=i)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            log_file = self.log_dir / f"{date_str}.log"
            
            if log_file.exists():
                with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    for line in f:
                        try:
                            entry = json.loads(line.strip())
                            entry_time = datetime.fromisoformat(entry['timestamp'])
                            
                            if entry_time > cutoff:
                                if level is None or entry['level'] == level:
                                    logs.append(entry)
                        except:
                            continue
        
        return sorted(logs, key=lambda x: x['timestamp'], reverse=True)
    
    def generate_daily_report(self, date: str = None) -> str:
        """生成每日报告"""
        if date is None:
            date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        
        log_file = self.log_dir / f"{date}.log"
        
        if not log_file.exists():
            return f"暂无 {date} 的日志"
        
        stats = {
            "total": 0,
            "info": 0,
            "warning": 0,
            "error": 0,
            "tasks": {}
        }
        
        with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            for line in f:
                try:
                    entry = json.loads(line.strip())
                    stats['total'] += 1
                    level = entry.get('level', 'info')
                    stats[level] = stats.get(level, 0) + 1
                    
                    task = entry.get('task', 'unknown')
                    stats['tasks'][task] = stats['tasks'].get(task, 0) + 1
                except:
                    continue
        
        report = f"""
📊 项目情报系统日报 ({date})
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
总日志数: {stats['total']}
INFO: {stats.get('info', 0)}
WARNING: {stats.get('warning', 0)}
ERROR: {stats.get('error', 0)}

任务执行统计:
"""
        for task, count in stats['tasks'].items():
            report += f"  - {task}: {count}次\n"
        
        return report
    
    def compress_old_logs(self, days: int = 7):
        """压缩旧日志"""
        cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=days)
        
        for log_file in self.log_dir.glob("*.log"):
            try:
                date_str = log_file.stem
                file_date = datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y-%m-%d")
                
                if file_date < cutoff:
                    # gzip压缩
                    with open(log_file, 'rb') as f_in:
                        with gzip.open(f"{log_file}.gz", 'wb') as f_out:
                            f_out.writelines(f_in)
                    log_file.unlink()  # 删除原文件
                    print(f"Compressed {log_file}")
            except:
                continue

# 快捷函数
logger = LogAggregator()

def log_info(message: str, task: str = None):
    logger.write_log("info", message, task)

def log_error(message: str, task: str = None):
    logger.write_log("error", message, task)

def log_warning(message: str, task: str = None):
    logger.write_log("warning", message, task)

三、故障恢复与容错机制

3.1 自愈机制（Self-Healing）

# resilience/self_healing.py
import asyncio
import json
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, Optional
import random

class SelfHealingSystem:
    """
    自愈系统：自动检测并恢复常见故障
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.state_file = "/mnt/kimi/project-intelligence/state/system_state.json"
        self.failure_counts = {}
        self.circuit_breakers = {}  # 熔断器状态
    
    async def run_health_loop(self):
        """健康检查循环"""
        while True:
            await self._check_and_heal()
            await asyncio.sleep(600)  # 每10分钟检查
    
    async def _check_and_heal(self):
        """检查并修复"""
        issues = []
        
        # 1. 检查飞书连接
        feishu_ok = await self._check_feishu()
        if not feishu_ok:
            issues.append("feishu_connection")
            await self._heal_feishu()
        
        # 2. 检查采集器状态
        collector_ok = await self._check_collector_health()
        if not collector_ok:
            issues.append("collector_stuck")
            await self._heal_collector()
        
        # 3. 检查存储空间
        storage_ok = await self._check_storage()
        if not storage_ok:
            issues.append("storage_full")
            await self._heal_storage()
        
        # 记录自愈操作
        if issues:
            self._log_healing_action(issues)
    
    async def _check_feishu(self) -> bool:
        """检查飞书连接"""
        # 简单ping检查
        return True  # 模拟
    
    async def _heal_feishu(self):
        """修复飞书连接（刷新token、切换webhook）"""
        print("🔧 Healing Feishu connection...")
        # 尝试刷新配置
        # 如果主webhook失败，切换到备用
        pass
    
    async def _check_collector_health(self) -> bool:
        """检查采集器是否卡住"""
        # 检查最后更新时间
        return True
    
    async def _heal_collector(self):
        """修复采集器（重启浏览器、清理缓存）"""
        print("🔧 Healing collector...")
        # 清理浏览器临时文件
        # 重置会话
        pass
    
    async def _check_storage(self) -> bool:
        """检查存储空间"""
        try:
            import shutil
            stat = shutil.disk_usage("/mnt/kimi")
            free_ratio = stat.free / stat.total
            return free_ratio > 0.1  # 保留10%空间
        except:
            return True
    
    async def _heal_storage(self):
        """修复存储（清理旧日志、压缩数据）"""
        print("🔧 Healing storage...")
        # 触发日志压缩
        from monitoring.log_aggregator import LogAggregator
        agg = LogAggregator()
        agg.compress_old_logs(days=3)
    
    def _log_healing_action(self, issues: list):
        """记录自愈操作"""
        record = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "issues_found": issues,
            "action": "auto_heal",
            "status": "completed"
        }
        
        with open(self.state_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(record, f, ensure_ascii=False)

class CircuitBreaker:
    """
    熔断器：防止级联故障
    """
    
    def __init__(self, failure_threshold: int = 5, recovery_timeout: int = 300):
        self.failure_threshold = failure_threshold
        self.recovery_timeout = recovery_timeout
        self.failures = {}
        self.last_failure_time = {}
        self.state = {}  # closed, open, half-open
    
    def can_execute(self, service: str) -> bool:
        """检查是否可以执行"""
        if service not in self.state:
            self.state[service] = 'closed'
        
        if self.state[service] == 'open':
            # 检查是否过了冷却期
            last_fail = self.last_failure_time.get(service, 0)
            if time.time() - last_fail > self.recovery_timeout:
                self.state[service] = 'half-open'
                return True
            return False
        
        return True
    
    def record_success(self, service: str):
        """记录成功"""
        self.failures[service] = 0
        self.state[service] = 'closed'
    
    def record_failure(self, service: str):
        """记录失败"""
        self.failures[service] = self.failures.get(service, 0) + 1
        self.last_failure_time[service] = time.time()
        
        if self.failures[service] >= self.failure_threshold:
            self.state[service] = 'open'
            print(f"🚫 Circuit breaker opened for {service}")

# 全局熔断器实例
circuit_breaker = CircuitBreaker()

四、性能优化（Kimi环境适配）

4.1 资源受限优化

# performance/optimizer.py
import asyncio
import psutil
from typing import List, Any
import functools

class ResourceAwareOptimizer:
    """
    资源感知优化器（适配Kimi环境限制）
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.memory_limit_mb = 512  # 假设Kimi内存限制
        self.cpu_limit = 2  # 假设CPU限制
    
    def limit_concurrency(self, max_concurrent: int = 3):
        """限制并发数装饰器"""
        semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(max_concurrent)
        
        def decorator(func):
            @functools.wraps(func)
            async def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
                async with semaphore:
                    return await func(*args, **kwargs)
            return wrapper
        return decorator
    
    def batch_process(self, items: List[Any], batch_size: int = 10, 
                     process_func, *args):
        """分批处理（避免内存溢出）"""
        results = []
        
        for i in range(0, len(items), batch_size):
            batch = items[i:i+batch_size]
            batch_results = asyncio.run(process_func(batch, *args))
            results.extend(batch_results)
            
            # 检查内存，如果过高则暂停
            if self._memory_usage_percent() > 80:
                print("⚠️  Memory high, pausing...")
                asyncio.sleep(5)
        
        return results
    
    def _memory_usage_percent(self) -> float:
        """获取内存使用百分比"""
        try:
            return psutil.virtual_memory().percent
        except:
            return 50.0  # 默认假设
    
    def cache_with_ttl(self, ttl_seconds: int = 3600):
        """带TTL的简单缓存（内存受限环境）"""
        cache = {}
        
        def decorator(func):
            @functools.wraps(func)
            async def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
                # 生成缓存键
                key = str(args) + str(kwargs)
                
                # 检查缓存
                if key in cache:
                    result, timestamp = cache[key]
                    if time.time() - timestamp < ttl_seconds:
                        return result
                
                # 执行并缓存
                result = await func(*args, **kwargs)
                cache[key] = (result, time.time())
                
                # 清理过期缓存（简单LRU）
                self._clean_cache(cache, max_size=100)
                
                return result
            return wrapper
        return decorator
    
    def _clean_cache(self, cache: dict, max_size: int):
        """清理缓存"""
        if len(cache) > max_size:
            # 删除最旧的
            oldest = min(cache.items(), key=lambda x: x[1][1])
            del cache[oldest[0]]

# 使用示例
optimizer = ResourceAwareOptimizer()

@optimizer.limit_concurrency(max_concurrent=2)
async def fetch_url(url: str):
    """限制并发请求"""
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url) as resp:
            return await resp.text()

@optimizer.cache_with_ttl(ttl_seconds=1800)
async def summarize_project(project_id: str):
    """缓存摘要结果30分钟"""
    # 昂贵的AI调用
    return await ai_summarizer.generate(project_id)

一、情报智能分析层（从数据到洞察）

1.1 融资趋势预测引擎（Time-Series Analysis）

# analytics/trend_predictor.py
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from collections import defaultdict
import json

class FundingTrendPredictor:
    """
    融资趋势预测：基于历史数据预测赛道热度
    为合伙人匹配提供市场时机信号
    """
    
    def __init__(self, case_repository):
        self.repo = case_repository
        self.lookback_days = 90
        
    def analyze_sector_momentum(self, sector: str) -> Dict:
        """
        分析赛道动量（加速度指标）
        """
        # 获取历史数据
        recent_cases = self.repo.get_recent_cases(days=self.lookback_days, industry=sector)
        
        if len(recent_cases) < 5:
            return {"status": "insufficient_data", "confidence": 0}
        
        # 时间序列分析
        df = pd.DataFrame(recent_cases)
        df['date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'])
        df = df.set_index('date').sort_index()
        
        # 计算7日移动平均
        weekly_counts = df.resample('W').size()
        ma4 = weekly_counts.rolling(window=4).mean()
        ma8 = weekly_counts.rolling(window=8).mean()
        
        # 动量指标：短期趋势 vs 长期趋势
        if len(ma4) > 0 and len(ma8) > 0:
            current_momentum = ma4.iloc[-1] / (ma8.iloc[-1] + 1)  # 避免除0
        else:
            current_momentum = 1.0
        
        # 金额趋势
        amount_trend = self._analyze_amount_trend(df)
        
        # 阶段分布变化（早期项目占比）
        early_stage_ratio = len(df[df['stage'].isin(['天使轮', 'pre-A', 'A轮'])]) / len(df)
        
        return {
            "sector": sector,
            "momentum_score": float(current_momentum),
            "trend_direction": "up" if current_momentum > 1.2 else "down" if current_momentum < 0.8 else "stable",
            "amount_trend": amount_trend,
            "early_stage_ratio": early_stage_ratio,
            "confidence": min(1.0, len(recent_cases) / 20),  # 数据越多置信度越高
            "avg_deal_size": df['amount'].mean() if 'amount' in df else 0,
            "hot_keywords": self._extract_emerging_keywords(df),
            "recommendation": self._generate_sector_recommendation(current_momentum, early_stage_ratio)
        }
    
    def _analyze_amount_trend(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
        """分析金额趋势"""
        if len(df) < 2 or 'amount' not in df:
            return "stable"
        
        # 简单线性回归
        x = np.arange(len(df))
        y = df['amount'].fillna(df['amount'].median()).values
        
        if len(y) > 1:
            slope = np.polyfit(x, y, 1)[0]
            if slope > 0.1:
                return "increasing"
            elif slope < -0.1:
                return "decreasing"
        return "stable"
    
    def _extract_emerging_keywords(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[str]:
        """提取新兴关键词（对比历史词频）"""
        # 简化实现：返回最近高频词
        all_titles = ' '.join(df['title'].tolist()) if 'title' in df else ''
        
        hard_tech_keywords = ["AI芯片", "GPU", "生物医药", "基因编辑", "新能源", "储能", "机器人"]
        found = [kw for kw in hard_tech_keywords if kw in all_titles]
        
        return list(set(found))[:5]
    
    def _generate_sector_recommendation(self, momentum: float, early_ratio: float) -> str:
        """生成赛道投资建议"""
        if momentum > 1.5 and early_ratio > 0.6:
            return "强烈推荐：赛道加速，早期项目丰富，是进入时机"
        elif momentum > 1.2:
            return "推荐：赛道热度上升，关注优质项目"
        elif momentum < 0.8:
            return "谨慎：赛道降温，建议观望或寻找细分机会"
        else:
            return "观察：赛道平稳，按常规节奏推进"

class InvestorNetworkAnalyzer:
    """
    投资人网络分析：识别活跃投资人和共同投资模式
    为合伙人匹配提供背书价值评估
    """
    
    def __init__(self, case_repository):
        self.repo = case_repository
    
    def analyze_investor_influence(self, investor_name: str) -> Dict:
        """
        分析特定投资人的影响力和投资模式
        """
        # 获取该投资人的所有投资记录
        investments = self.repo.get_investor_history(investor_name)
        
        if not investments:
            return {"investor": investor_name, "status": "no_data"}
        
        # 分析投资节奏
        sectors = defaultdict(int)
        stages = defaultdict(int)
        co_investors = defaultdict(int)
        
        for inv in investments:
            sectors[inv.get('sector', 'Unknown')] += 1
            stages[inv.get('stage', 'Unknown')] += 1
            
            # 共同投资人
            for co in inv.get('co_investors', []):
                if co != investor_name:
                    co_investors[co] += 1
        
        return {
            "investor": investor_name,
            "total_deals": len(investments),
            "preferred_sectors": sorted(sectors.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:3],
            "preferred_stages": sorted(stages.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True),
            "top_co_investors": sorted(co_investors.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:5],
            "investment_style": self._classify_style(stages),
            "credibility_score": self._calculate_credibility(len(investments), len(sectors))
        }
    
    def _classify_style(self, stages: Dict) -> str:
        """分类投资风格"""
        if stages.get('天使轮', 0) + stages.get('pre-A', 0) > sum(stages.values()) * 0.7:
            return "early_stage_focused"
        elif stages.get('C轮', 0) + stages.get('D轮', 0) > 0:
            return "late_stage_growth"
        else:
            return "balanced"
    
    def _calculate_credibility(self, deal_count: int, sector_diversity: int) -> float:
        """计算投资人可信度分数"""
        base = min(10, deal_count / 10)  # 项目数量
        diversity_bonus = min(2, sector_diversity / 3)  # 领域多样性
        return min(10, base + diversity_bonus)

1.2 项目质量评分模型（Scoring Engine）

# analytics/project_scorer.py
from typing import Dict, List
import numpy as np

class ProjectQualityScorer:
    """
    项目质量综合评分（为合伙人匹配提供输入）
    将情报系统发现的项目转化为匹配引擎可用的案例
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.weights = {
            "team_quality": 0.25,
            "market_opportunity": 0.25,
            "technology_barrier": 0.25,
            "funding_momentum": 0.15,
            "investor_quality": 0.10
        }
    
    def score_project(self, project: Dict, founder_profile: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """
        为项目计算综合质量分（0-100）
        
        Args:
            project: 情报系统采集的项目数据
            founder_profile: 可选，当前创始人画像（用于匹配度计算）
        """
        scores = {}
        
        # 1. 团队质量（基于公开信息推断）
        scores["team_quality"] = self._score_team(
            project.get('founder_background', ''),
            project.get('team_size', 0),
            project.get('previous_exits', 0)
        )
        
        # 2. 市场机会（基于赛道热度）
        scores["market_opportunity"] = self._score_market(
            project.get('sector', ''),
            project.get('tam_estimate', 0)
        )
        
        # 3. 技术壁垒（基于专利/技术描述）
        scores["technology_barrier"] = self._score_tech_barrier(
            project.get('patents_count', 0),
            project.get('tech_description', ''),
            project.get('rd_spending_ratio', 0)
        )
        
        # 4. 融资动量（近期融资频率）
        scores["funding_momentum"] = self._score_funding_momentum(
            project.get('funding_history', [])
        )
        
        # 5. 投资人质量（基于投资方历史表现）
        scores["investor_quality"] = self._score_investor_quality(
            project.get('investors', [])
        )
        
        # 加权总分
        total_score = sum(scores[k] * self.weights[k] for k in scores)
        
        # 如果提供了创始人画像，计算匹配度
        match_score = None
        if founder_profile:
            match_score = self._calculate_founder_project_match(founder_profile, project)
        
        return {
            "project_id": project.get('id'),
            "project_name": project.get('company_name'),
            "total_score": round(total_score, 1),
            "dimension_scores": {k: round(v, 1) for k, v in scores.items()},
            "grade": self._score_to_grade(total_score),
            "match_score": round(match_score, 1) if match_score else None,
            "investment_recommendation": self._generate_recommendation(total_score, match_score),
            "key_risks": self._identify_risks(scores, project)
        }
    
    def _score_team(self, background: str, team_size: int, exits: int) -> float:
        """评分团队"""
        score = 50  # 基础分
        
        # 名校/名企背景加分
        elite_signals = ["清华", "北大", "MIT", "斯坦福", "Google", "腾讯", "阿里"]
        if any(s in background for s in elite_signals):
            score += 20
        
        # 成功退出经验
        score += exits * 15
        
        # 团队规模（早期10-50人最佳）
        if 10 <= team_size <= 50:
            score += 10
        
        return min(100, score)
    
    def _score_market(self, sector: str, tam: int) -> float:
        """评分市场"""
        # 基于赛道热度
        hot_sectors = {"AI芯片": 90, "生物医药": 85, "新能源": 80}
        base = hot_sectors.get(sector, 60)
        
        # TAM调整
        if tam > 1000:  # 千亿市场
            base += 10
        elif tam < 100:  # 百亿以下
            base -= 10
        
        return min(100, base)
    
    def _score_tech_barrier(self, patents: int, tech_desc: str, rd_ratio: float) -> float:
        """评分技术壁垒"""
        score = 50
        
        # 专利数量
        score += min(30, patents * 3)
        
        # 技术描述关键词
        tech_keywords = ["自主知识产权", "首创", "突破", "专利", "算法"]
        score += sum(5 for kw in tech_keywords if kw in tech_desc)
        
        # 研发投入比例
        if rd_ratio > 0.3:
            score += 15
        
        return min(100, score)
    
    def _score_to_grade(self, score: float) -> str:
        """分数转等级"""
        if score >= 85: return "A+"
        if score >= 75: return "A"
        if score >= 65: return "B"
        if score >= 55: return "C"
        return "D"
    
    def _generate_recommendation(self, score: float, match_score: float = None) -> str:
        """生成投资建议"""
        if score >= 80:
            base = "强烈推荐跟进"
        elif score >= 65:
            base = "值得关注"
        elif score >= 50:
            base = "保持观察"
        else:
            base = "暂不推荐"
        
        if match_score and match_score > 80:
            base += "（高度匹配当前创始人画像）"
        
        return base
    
    def _identify_risks(self, scores: Dict, project: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """识别关键风险"""
        risks = []
        
        if scores["team_quality"] < 60:
            risks.append("团队背景相对薄弱，需深度尽调")
        if scores["technology_barrier"] < 60:
            risks.append("技术壁垒不明显，竞争风险高")
        if scores["market_opportunity"] < 60:
            risks.append("市场空间有限，增长天花板可见")
        if project.get('cash_runway_months', 12) < 6:
            risks.append("现金流紧张，需紧急融资")
        
        return risks

二、双系统集成架构（情报→匹配的价值闭环）

2.1 系统间数据流设计

# integration/intelligence_matching_bridge.py
"""
情报系统与匹配系统的桥梁
实现：项目发现→案例沉淀→匹配优化→投资决策的闭环
"""

class IntelligenceMatchingBridge:
    """
    情报-匹配双系统桥接器
    
    数据流向：
    1. 情报系统发现新项目 → 自动创建Case（待定状态）
    2. 匹配系统评估创始人时 → 查询情报系统获取行业动态
    3. 投资决策后 → 结果回流优化两个系统的算法
    """
    
    def __init__(self, intelligence_system, matching_engine, case_repository):
        self.intelligence = intelligence_system
        self.matcher = matching_engine
        self.case_repo = case_repository
        
        # 初始化评分器
        self.project_scorer = ProjectQualityScorer()
        self.trend_predictor = FundingTrendPredictor(case_repository)
    
    async def enrich_matching_with_intelligence(self, founder: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        为合伙人匹配增强情报数据（关键集成点）
        
        当创始人在寻找合伙人时，提供：
        1. 所在赛道的最新融资动态（市场热度证明）
        2. 竞品公司的合伙人配置（对标分析）
        3. 活跃投资人名单（潜在连接资源）
        """
        industry = founder.get('industry')
        
        # 1. 获取赛道情报
        sector_trend = self.trend_predictor.analyze_sector_momentum(industry)
        
        # 2. 获取竞品合伙人配置（对标分析）
        peer_analysis = await self._analyze_peer_partners(industry, founder.get('stage'))
        
        # 3. 获取活跃投资人（潜在FA资源）
        active_investors = await self._get_active_investors(industry)
        
        return {
            "market_context": {
                "sector_heat": sector_trend,
                "investment_momentum": "high" if sector_trend['momentum_score'] > 1.2 else "normal",
                "recommended_timing": sector_trend['recommendation']
            },
            "peer_intelligence": peer_analysis,
            "network_resources": {
                "active_investors": active_investors,
                "potential_introductions": self._match_investors_to_founder(active_investors, founder)
            },
            "matching_implications": self._generate_matching_strategy(sector_trend, founder)
        }
    
    async def _analyze_peer_partners(self, industry: str, stage: str) -> Dict:
        """分析竞品公司的合伙人配置（情报系统数据）"""
        # 查询情报系统中同行业同阶段的公司
        peer_companies = self.intelligence.query_projects(
            industry=industry,
            stage=stage,
            limit=10
        )
        
        partner_patterns = {
            "tech_cto_ratio": 0,
            "business_coo_ratio": 0,
            "cfo_present_ratio": 0,
            "avg_founding_team_size": 0
        }
        
        for company in peer_companies:
            # 分析公开信息中的团队配置
            team_desc = company.get('team_description', '')
            if 'CTO' in team_desc or '技术合伙人' in team_desc:
                partner_patterns["tech_cto_ratio"] += 1
            if 'COO' in team_desc or '运营' in team_desc:
                partner_patterns["business_coo_ratio"] += 1
            if 'CFO' in team_desc or '财务' in team_desc:
                partner_patterns["cfo_present_ratio"] += 1
        
        total = len(peer_companies)
        if total > 0:
            for key in partner_patterns:
                partner_patterns[key] /= total
        
        return {
            "peer_count": total,
            "partner_configuration_trends": partner_patterns,
            "recommendation": self._interpret_peer_patterns(partner_patterns, industry)
        }
    
    def _interpret_peer_patterns(self, patterns: Dict, industry: str) -> str:
        """解读竞品模式"""
        if patterns['tech_cto_ratio'] > 0.7 and industry in ["AI芯片", "生物医药"]:
            return "该赛道技术合伙人几乎是标配，建议优先寻找技术背景合伙人"
        elif patterns['cfo_present_ratio'] > 0.5:
            return "财务合伙人配置率高，可能进入精细化运营阶段，建议考虑财务背景"
        else:
            return "合伙人配置多样化，根据具体短板选择"
    
    def auto_create_case_from_project(self, project: Dict, founder_id: str = None) -> str:
        """
        将情报系统发现的项目自动沉淀为匹配系统的案例
        
        当情报系统发现优质项目时，自动创建为案例供后续匹配参考
        """
        # 评分项目
        score_result = self.project_scorer.score_project(project)
        
        # 转换为Case格式
        case_data = {
            "case_id": f"INT-{project.get('id')}",
            "case_name": f"{project.get('company_name')}自动采集案例",
            "industry": project.get('industry'),
            "stage": project.get('stage'),
            "source": "intelligence_auto_import",
            "project_quality_score": score_result['total_score'],
            "founder_profile": {
                "background": "自动推断",  # 可从情报解析
                "implied_capabilities": self._infer_capabilities_from_project(project)
            },
            "selected_partner": {
                "implied_need": self._infer_partner_need(project, score_result)
            },
            "outcome": "observation",  # 观察中，待确认
            "intelligence_data": {
                "original_project": project,
                "quality_assessment": score_result,
                "trend_context": self.trend_predictor.analyze_sector_momentum(project.get('industry'))
            }
        }
        
        # 存入案例库
        case_id = self.case_repo.save(case_data)
        
        # 如果质量分高且匹配某创始人，主动推送
        if score_result['total_score'] > 80 and founder_id:
            self._notify_high_quality_opportunity(case_id, founder_id, score_result)
        
        return case_id
    
    def _infer_capabilities_from_project(self, project: Dict) -> Dict:
        """从项目信息推断创始人能力"""
        # 基于技术描述推断技术能力
        tech_score = 8 if project.get('patents_count', 0) > 5 else 6
        
        return {
            "technical_depth": tech_score,
            "business_acumen": 5,  # 默认中等，需验证
            "industry_knowledge": 7  # 能创业通常有行业积累
        }
    
    def _infer_partner_need(self, project: Dict, score_result: Dict) -> Dict:
        """推断项目需要的合伙人类型"""
        risks = score_result.get('key_risks', [])
        
        needs = []
        if any("团队" in r for r in risks):
            needs.append("资深运营合伙人")
        if any("技术壁垒" in r for r in risks):
            needs.append("技术大咖CTO")
        if any("现金流" in r for r in risks):
            needs.append("融资型CFO")
        
        return {
            "implied_partner_type": needs,
            "urgency": "high" if any("现金流" in r for r in risks) else "normal"
        }
    
    def feedback_loop(self, case_id: str, actual_outcome: str, matching_quality: float):
        """
        反馈闭环：投资结果回流优化两个系统
        
        当项目有了实际投资结果后：
        1. 更新情报系统的项目状态
        2. 优化匹配系统的案例库
        3. 调整趋势预测模型的权重
        """
        # 更新案例结果
        self.case_repo.update_outcome(case_id, actual_outcome, {
            "matching_quality": matching_quality,
            "feedback_source": "actual_investment"
        })
        
        # 如果投资成功，提升该赛道的趋势权重
        if actual_outcome == "success":
            case = self.case_repo.get_by_id(case_id)
            sector = case.get('industry')
            # 调整趋势预测器的内部权重（EWC更新）
            self.trend_predictor.boost_sector_confidence(sector, boost=0.1)
        
        # 记录到情报系统（避免重复推荐）
        self.intelligence.mark_project_status(
            case_id.replace("INT-", ""),
            "invested" if actual_outcome == "success" else "passed"
        )

# 集成API端点（FastAPI扩展）
from fastapi import APIRouter

integration_router = APIRouter(prefix="/integration", tags=["Integration"])

@integration_router.post("/enrich-matching")
async def enrich_matching(founder: Dict):
    """为匹配提供情报增强数据"""
    bridge = IntelligenceMatchingBridge(
        intelligence_system=intelligence_api,
        matching_engine=matcher,
        case_repository=case_repo
    )
    enrichment = await bridge.enrich_matching_with_intelligence(founder)
    return enrichment

@integration_router.post("/project-to-case")
async def convert_project(project: Dict, founder_id: str = None):
    """将项目情报转为匹配案例"""
    bridge = IntelligenceMatchingBridge(...)
    case_id = bridge.auto_create_case_from_project(project, founder_id)
    return {"case_id": case_id, "status": "created"}

三、智能推荐引擎（情报驱动的主动推送）

3.1 创始人-项目匹配推荐（Reverse Matching）

# recommendation/reverse_matching.py
"""
反向匹配：为创始人主动推荐项目（而非仅匹配合伙人）
利用情报系统的项目库，主动发现适合创始人投资/加入的项目
"""

class ReverseMatchingEngine:
    """
    反向匹配引擎：项目找人
    
    适用场景：
    - 创始人有资金，寻找投资项目
    - 潜在合伙人寻找加入的创业公司
    - 满意解研究所向客户推荐标的
    """
    
    def __init__(self, intelligence_api, matching_engine):
        self.intelligence = intelligence_api
        self.matcher = matching_engine
    
    async def recommend_projects_for_founder(self, founder: Dict, 
                                              top_n: int = 5) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        为创始人推荐适合投资/加入的项目
        
        匹配维度：
        1. 能力互补性（创始人能提供什么）
        2. 价值观契合度（企业文化匹配）
        3. 时机匹配度（融资阶段与创始人资源匹配）
        4. 地域匹配度（地理位置）
        """
        # 获取最新项目池
        recent_projects = self.intelligence.get_recent_projects(
            days=30,
            sectors=[founder.get('industry')],
            stages=self._infer_suitable_stages(founder)
        )
        
        matches = []
        for project in recent_projects:
            # 将项目视为"候选人"，创始人视为"评估者"
            # 复用匹配引擎的评估逻辑
            
            match_score = self._calculate_project_founder_fit(project, founder)
            
            if match_score['overall'] > 60:  # 及格线
                matches.append({
                    "project": project,
                    "match_score": match_score,
                    "value_proposition": self._generate_value_proposition(project, founder, match_score),
                    "recommended_action": self._recommend_action(match_score, founder)
                })
        
        # 排序并截断
        matches.sort(key=lambda x: x['match_score']['overall'], reverse=True)
        return matches[:top_n]
    
    def _calculate_project_founder_fit(self, project: Dict, founder: Dict) -> Dict:
        """计算项目-创始人契合度（反向匹配核心算法）"""
        
        # 1. 能力供给匹配（创始人能给项目带来什么）
        founder_caps = founder.get('capability_matrix', {})
        project_needs = project.get('implied_needs', {})
        
        capability_match = self._match_capability_supply(founder_caps, project_needs)
        
        # 2. 价值观契合度（减少未来冲突）
        values_match = self._estimate_values_alignment(
            founder.get('value_system', {}),
            project.get('culture_signals', {})
        )
        
        # 3. 时机匹配（融资阶段vs创始人资金/时间）
        timing_match = self._match_timing(
            project.get('stage'),
            project.get('cash_runway_months'),
            founder.get('available_resources', {})
        )
        
        # 4. 风险兼容（创始人风险承受度vs项目风险等级）
        risk_match = self._assess_risk_compatibility(
            founder.get('risk_profile', {}),
            project.get('risk_indicators', {})
        )
        
        # 加权总分（权重可调整）
        weights = {
            'capability': 0.35,
            'values': 0.25,
            'timing': 0.25,
            'risk': 0.15
        }
        
        overall = sum([
            capability_match * weights['capability'],
            values_match * weights['values'],
            timing_match * weights['timing'],
            risk_match * weights['risk']
        ])
        
        return {
            "overall": round(overall, 1),
            "capability_match": round(capability_match, 1),
            "values_match": round(values_match, 1),
            "timing_match": round(timing_match, 1),
            "risk_match": round(risk_match, 1),
            "satisficing_met": overall > 75  # 满意解阈值
        }
    
    def _generate_value_proposition(self, project: Dict, founder: Dict, score: Dict) -> str:
        """生成价值主张说明"""
        strengths = []
        
        if score['capability_match'] > 80:
            strengths.append(f"您的{founder.get('core_strength')}恰好补足该项目短板")
        if score['timing_match'] > 80:
            strengths.append("当前融资阶段与您的资金/资源到位时机契合")
        if score['values_match'] > 80:
            strengths.append("企业文化与您的管理风格高度兼容")
        
        if not strengths:
            return "综合匹配度良好，建议进一步尽调"
        
        return "；".join(strengths)
    
    def _recommend_action(self, score: Dict, founder: Dict) -> str:
        """推荐行动"""
        if score['overall'] > 85 and score['satisficing_met']:
            return "强烈建议：立即安排创始人与项目方面谈，匹配度极高"
        elif score['overall'] > 70:
            return "推荐：纳入重点观察名单，等待合适进入时机"
        elif score['overall'] > 60:
            return "观察：保持关注，可作为备选标的"
        else:
            return "暂不推荐：匹配度不足，建议寻找其他标的"

# 主动推送服务（Daily Digest）
class ProactivePushService:
    """
    主动推送服务：每日扫描并向创始人推送匹配项目
    """
    
    def __init__(self, reverse_matcher, feishu_pusher):
        self.matcher = reverse_matcher
        self.pusher = feishu_pusher
    
    async def daily_push_routine(self):
        """每日推送例程"""
        # 获取所有活跃创始人（订阅了推送服务）
        active_founders = self._get_active_founders()
        
        for founder in active_founders:
            # 获取推荐
            recommendations = await self.matcher.recommend_projects_for_founder(
                founder, 
                top_n=3  # 每人每天最多3个
            )
            
            if recommendations:
                # 构建推送卡片
                card = self._build_recommendation_card(founder, recommendations)
                
                # 推送到创始人飞书
                await self.pusher.push_to_user(founder['feishu_user_id'], card)
                
                # 记录推送（避免重复）
                self._log_push(founder['id'], [r['project']['id'] for r in recommendations])

四、数据闭环与持续学习架构

# learning/feedback_learner.py
"""
双系统联合学习：情报+匹配的数据闭环
"""

class JointFeedbackLearner:
    """
    联合反馈学习器
    
    学习信号：
    1. 情报系统推荐的项目被投资 → 提升推荐算法权重
    2. 匹配系统推荐的合伙人成功合作 → 沉淀为成功案例
    3. 投资失败案例 → 分析是情报误判还是匹配失误
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.learning_buffer = []
        self.correction_patterns = {}
    
    def record_interaction(self, interaction: Dict):
        """
        记录完整交互流程（用于学习）
        
        interaction格式：
        {
            "founder_id": "...",
            "project_id": "...",  # 情报系统推荐
            "partner_candidates": [...],  # 匹配系统推荐
            "final_decision": "invested/partnered/rejected",
            "outcome_after_6m": "success/failure/ongoing",
            "satisfaction_score": 8,  # 创始人满意度
            "key_factors": [...]  # 影响决策的关键因素
        }
        """
        self.learning_buffer.append(interaction)
        
        # 批量学习（每10条触发一次）
        if len(self.learning_buffer) >= 10:
            self._trigger_learning_update()
    
    def _trigger_learning_update(self):
        """触发学习更新"""
        batch = self.learning_buffer[:10]
        self.learning_buffer = self.learning_buffer[10:]
        
        # 1. 更新情报系统的项目质量模型
        self._update_project_scoring(batch)
        
        # 2. 更新匹配系统的权重
        self._update_matching_weights(batch)
        
        # 3. 发现模式（如"CTO合伙人+芯片项目"成功率高）
        self._discover_success_patterns(batch)
    
    def _discover_success_patterns(self, batch: List[Dict]):
        """发现成功模式（关联规则挖掘）"""
        from collections import defaultdict
        
        success_cases = [b for b in batch if b['outcome_after_6m'] == 'success']
        
        patterns = defaultdict(int)
        for case in success_cases:
            # 提取特征组合
            features = (
                case.get('project_sector'),
                case.get('founder_background'),
                case.get('partner_type_selected')
            )
            patterns[features] += 1
        
        # 保存高置信度模式
        for pattern, count in patterns.items():
            if count >= 3:  # 至少3次验证
                self.correction_patterns[str(pattern)] = {
                    "confidence": count / len(success_cases),
                    "recommendation": "highly_recommended"
                }
        
        # 应用到系统
        if self.correction_patterns:
            print(f"Discovered {len(self.correction_patterns)} success patterns")
            # 实际应写入配置或模型文件
